# Simplification survey, round 2: 40 verified cross-file candidates

**Date**: 2026-08-26 · **Method**: 24 macroscopic lenses, adversarially verified · **Net if all land**: -2,263 LoC, -142 elements

## 0. Method and headline

Round 1 ([2026-08-25 survey](./2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md), shipped as #11416-#11422) partitioned the repo by directory and gave each surveyor one subtree. That partition is blind by construction to anything whose two halves live in different directories: a symbol exported from `src/` whose only caller is in `packages/cli/`, one fact derived independently in all four hosts, a Zod schema mirrored by a hand-written interface two trees away.

Round 2 replaced the directory partition with **24 cross-cutting lenses** — dual representation, re-derivation helpers, lifecycle mirrors, defensive machinery after same-process typed handoffs, hand-rolled code the ES2022 floor already covers, compatibility past its window, options bags with one caller, cross-provider handler duplication, webview-tree parity, and more. Each lens followed its pattern wherever it led, capped at four candidates, with explicit permission to return zero.

Every candidate then went to an independent adversarial verifier whose default was REFUTED, with eight named kill conditions: duplicate of round 1, already filed or already done, wrong consumer count, deliberately justified by a dated ruling, settled surface, net-adds or churns, misread masking site (checklist §15 M1-M6 are defects to fix loudly, not elegance to delete), or too thin to stand alone.

|                           | Count  |
| ------------------------- | ------ |
| Candidates proposed       | 54     |
| **Survived verification** | **40** |
| Refuted                   | 14     |

Two guards against round 2 rehashing round 1: every surveyor read a 90-title index of what round 1 shipped and refuted before proposing anything, and kill condition #1 for every verifier was "check whether this is already done at HEAD" — since #11416-#11422 landed the day before, a stale candidate now looks identical to a live one.

Verifiers found factual errors in the evidence of most surviving candidates. Those corrections are recorded inline under each candidate and **override the claim above them**.

Estimates are the verifiers' figures; measured numbers are `git diff --shortstat origin/main` on each merged lane branch. The gap is almost entirely `M9-progress`, where the desktop host-interactions suite was kept rather than deleted: the verifier's own correction found five behaviours in it with no counterpart in the extension suite, including the repo's only dispose-cancellation test.

### Shared-file contention

Four files are touched by two lanes each. All four are different-region edits, so they merge cleanly in the common case; land `M9-progress` first or last and rebase the sibling rather than resolving twice.

| File                                                           | Lanes                             |
| -------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- |
| `packages/extension/src/commands/housekeeping/fileOpRunner.ts` | `M9-progress`, `M3-handlers-auth` |
| `src/transcript/StreamSnapshotStore.ts`                        | `M9-progress`, `M5-shared`        |
| `src/controllers/progressView/backend/ProgressBackend.ts`      | `M9-progress`, `M5-shared`        |
| `src/tools/inquiry/ExternalInquiryTool.ts`                     | `M9-progress`, `M4-tools`         |

Any lane that deletes an export also shrinks `config/ratchets/knip-baseline.json`. That file is a merge magnet across all nine lanes: re-run `npm run check:dead-code-ratchet` after every rebase rather than hand-resolving it.

#### Delete the never-populated PR/CI half of the worktree chip (WorktreePRInfo, WORKTREE_PR_STATE, WORKTREE_CI_STATE)

- **Lens**: `event-fact-plumbing` · **Kind**: speculative-generality · **Risk**: low
- **Net**: -213 LoC, -14 elements (claimed -190 LoC, -13 elements; verifier figure governs)

**Evidence**

`StreamTabInfo.worktree.pr` is a wire sub-shape with a full renderer and ZERO producers, 3+ months after it was declared as a stub.

Schema surface: src/shared/schemas/stream.ts:217-222 `WORKTREE_PR_STATE` (exported), :224 `WorktreePRStateSchema`, :225 `export type WorktreePRState`, :227-233 `WORKTREE_CI_STATE` (exported), :235 `WorktreeCIStateSchema`, :236 `export type WorktreeCIState`, :238-245 `WorktreePRInfoSchema` (number/state/title/additions/deletions/ciState), :254-255 `pr: WorktreePRInfoSchema.optional()` on `WorktreeInfoSchema`.

Production PRODUCERS of `worktree.pr`: ZERO. The only resolver is `probeWorktree` at src/utils/git/worktreeInfo.ts:63-70, which returns exactly `{ workingDirectory, branch, dirty }` — the module header at :5-7 says so outright: "PR enrichment is intentionally out of scope for this module — that will layer on top once the chip is in the UI." The only other construction site is src/controllers/session/streamTabInfo.ts:70-73, which either passes `inputs.worktreeInfo` through or emits `{ workingDirectory }`. `rg -n "pr:" src packages` returns 8 lines and none of them build a `WorktreePRInfo` (installGithubAction.ts:375 is a CLI flag object; PRPollingSource/RepoPollingSource are the unrelated GitHub polling tools).
Production CONSUMERS (all dead-on-arrival): packages/extension/src/progressView/frontend/components/WorktreeChip.ts:20-25 `PR_STATE_LABEL`, :28-36 `PR_STATE_VARIANT`, :38-44 `CI_ICON`, :46-52 `CI_LABEL`, :167-171 the two `pr ? ... : nothing` branches in `render()`, :174-181 `renderPRPill`, :213-248 `renderPRDetails`, plus the CSS rules only those branches use — `.pill` (:77-84), `.pr-number` (:109-112), `.pr-title` (:114-119), `.diff-stats` (:121-127), `.diff-added` (:129-131), `.diff-removed` (:133-135), `.ci-icon` and its five state variants (:137-157) — and the `wa-badge` import at :17. One more site: packages/extension/src/progressView/frontend/components/StreamTabs.ts:87 builds `Worktree: ${branch}${info.worktree.pr ? ` (#${info.worktree.pr.number})` : ''}`, a branch that can never be taken.
`WORKTREE_PR_STATE` / `WORKTREE_CI_STATE` / `WorktreePRState` / `WorktreeCIState` have exactly one importer each, WorktreeChip.ts:5-9; `rg -n "WORKTREE_PR_STATE|WORKTREE_CI_STATE|WorktreeCIState|WorktreePRState|WorktreePRInfo" src packages` returns hits only in stream.ts and WorktreeChip.ts. Neither appears in config/ratchets/knip-baseline.json (`grep -n -i worktree config/ratchets/*.json` → no hits).
Non-production: src/test-kernel/progressView/WorktreeChip.vitest.ts:26-50 — one test that hand-feeds a synthetic `pr:{number,state,title,additions,deletions,ciState}` and asserts 4 tooltips. Per AGENTS.md "Testing discipline" this pins retired behavior and goes with it; the other two cases (:18-24 working-directory fallback, :52-63 dirty dot) exercise the live branch-only chip and stay.
Ambiguous corpus: none — no scripts/, prompts/, resources/, supabase/ or package.json reference.
History: `git log -S"WorktreePRInfoSchema"` bottoms out at 74e899ab56, 2026-05-14, "feat(progress): show worktree chip on stream tabs", whose own body says the chip has "slots for PR pill, #NNN, title, +A -D, and CI icon (unused until the PR enrichment lands)". The enrichment never landed; the three later commits touching WorktreeChip.ts (43ce3aaee6, 0ae8580de6, 3e3ac737cb) are styling/token sweeps, not enrichment. This is squarely the SKILL's "speculative product generality with no product owner" and AGENTS.md "Compatibility and format retirement" window (>3 months).
Dedupe: not in the round-1 index and not in docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md (grep for "worktree" there hits only unrelated worktree-lane prose and delegation-availability text). `gh issue list --label tech-debt --state all --search "worktree"` returns 15 rows, none about the chip's PR fields (#8326 layering, #9043/#9053 memento eviction, #11183 git-root test). `git log --all --grep=WorktreePRInfo` → nothing. Settled surfaces: untouched — no ratchet, no @agent/* edge, no PocketFlow engine, no browser-safe util (stream.ts is not in the six-module reachable set), no AgentEvent/SessionFact rail change, and `worktree` is not part of the frozen CLI result-JSON or NDJSON progress vocabulary (`StreamTabInfo` reaches the CLI only through the internal tab projection).

**Proposal**

1. src/shared/schemas/stream.ts: delete lines 217-245 (`WORKTREE_PR_STATE`, `WorktreePRStateSchema`, `WorktreePRState`, `WORKTREE_CI_STATE`, `WorktreeCIStateSchema`, `WorktreeCIState`, `WorktreePRInfoSchema`) and the `pr` field plus its doc comment at :253-255. `WorktreeInfoSchema` keeps `workingDirectory`, `branch`, `dirty` — exactly what `probeWorktree` produces.
2. WorktreeChip.ts: delete `PR_STATE_LABEL`, `PR_STATE_VARIANT`, `CI_ICON`, `CI_LABEL`, `renderPRPill`, `renderPRDetails`; `render()` becomes `return this.renderBranch();` (drop the `pr` local at :167 and the two conditional slots at :169-170). Delete the CSS rules `.pill`, `.pill::part(base)`, `.pr-number`, `.pr-title`, `.diff-stats`, `.diff-added`, `.diff-removed`, `.ci-icon` and its five `.ci-*` variants. Drop the now-unused imports: `WORKTREE_PR_STATE`, `WORKTREE_CI_STATE`, `WorktreePRState`, `WorktreeCIState`, `TeXRAIconName`, and `@awesome.me/webawesome/dist/components/badge/badge.js`. `renderBranch`, `worktreeLabel`, `.branch*`, `.dirty-dot` and the `code-branch` icon all stay — the branch/dirty chip is live.
3. StreamTabs.ts:86-87: simplify the tooltip to `Worktree: ${info.worktree.branch}`.
4. Delete the second test case in src/test-kernel/progressView/WorktreeChip.vitest.ts (:26-50); keep the fallback and dirty-state cases.
   Keep the class name `worktree-chip` (renaming it would be pure churn under R5). Run `npm run check:dead-code-ratchet` — no baseline row shrinks or grows, since none of these were baselined.

**What we give up**

The pre-wired slots for a future "show the PR state pill, #NNN + title, +A -D diff stats and a CI dot on the stream tab" feature. Nothing computes any of it today: there is no GitHub enrichment path feeding `resolveWorktreeInfo`, and building one would mean adding a gh/API call, a refresh cadence, and auth handling — at which point re-adding a six-field schema object and one render method is the cheapest part of that work. The live branch-name + uncommitted-changes chip is untouched.

This verifier folded its corrections into the reasoning block below rather than filling the corrections field. Read that block: the numbered corrections it references are stated there.

**1. Duplicate check.** `grep -c -i worktree` on `scratchpad/round1-index.md` → 0. `docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md` mentions "worktree" only in unrelated prose (lane infrastructure at :55, `maxSessions` at :1082, `withDelegationWorktreeAvailability` at :1118-1144, `WorktreeStateStore.sharedKeys` at :2471) — nothing about the chip's PR fields. Not a duplicate.

**2. Already done at HEAD?** No. HEAD is `d8c6dfc071` (post-#11416..#11422, all merged). I read the working tree at HEAD: `stream.ts:217-255` still declares the full PR/CI block, `WorktreeChip.ts` is still 255 lines with `PR_STATE_LABEL`/`PR_STATE_VARIANT`/`CI_ICON`/`CI_LABEL`/`renderPRPill`/`renderPRDetails` intact, `StreamTabs.ts:87` still interpolates `info.worktree.pr`. Round-1 PRs #11417 (extension) and #11421 (shared) did not touch it.

**3. Already filed?** `gh issue list --label tech-debt --state all --search "worktree"` → 20 rows, none on point (#8326 layering, #9043/#9053 memento eviction, #11183 git-root coverage, #11425 unrelated). `gh issue list --state all --search "WorktreeChip in:body,title"` → 4 rows, all styling/tooltip/icon follow-ups (#9725 `.sr-only`, #9232 waIcon, #9168 wa-tooltip, #8693 badge dimensions) — none proposes deleting the PR half. `git log` on both paths shows only styling/token sweeps since introduction (43ce3aaee6, 0ae8580de6, 3e3ac737cb, 434559d50c). The one commit that came close, #7612, deleted the dead `WorktreePRInfo` alias and explicitly kept the schema (see corrections).

**4. Consumer count, re-grepped myself.**

- Producers: `rg -n "^\s*pr:" src packages` → 6 hits. `stream.ts:255` (the declaration), `WorktreeChip.vitest.ts:30` (the synthetic test), and four genuinely unrelated: `installGithubAction.ts:375` (CLI flag object), `PRPollingSource.ts:132` / `RepoPollingSource.ts:473` / `prPollingSourceState.ts:50` (GitHub polling tool, `PRKey`/`GhPullsListEntry` types). **Zero** production sites build a `WorktreePRInfo`. `probeWorktree` (`src/utils/git/worktreeInfo.ts:63-70`) returns `{ workingDirectory, branch, dirty }` and is the only resolver; `streamTabInfo.ts:70-74` either passes `inputs.worktreeInfo` through or emits `{ workingDirectory }`; `streamInfoUtils.ts:24-42` feeds it only from `peekWorktreeInfo`/`resolveWorktreeInfo`. Confirmed.
- Consumers: `rg -n "WORKTREE_PR_STATE|WORKTREE_CI_STATE|WorktreeCIState|WorktreePRState|WorktreePRInfo"` across the whole repo → hits only in `src/shared/schemas/stream.ts` and `packages/extension/src/progressView/frontend/components/WorktreeChip.ts`. `rg "\.worktree"` across `src packages` → the extension chip, the builder, and tests only.
- Ambiguous-corpus sweep I ran independently: `docs/`, `scripts/`, `packages/extension/resources/`, `prompts/`, `supabase/`, `config/` → zero hits for `WorktreePRInfo|WORKTREE_PR_STATE|WORKTREE_CI_STATE|worktree-chip|ciState`. `packages/desktop/src` and `packages/trace-viewer/src` → zero hits for `worktree` at all (case-insensitive), despite the introducing commit having touched `desktopAgentExecution.ts` — that wiring has since moved into `src/controllers/`. `packages/extension/package.json`, `commands.ts`, `coreSettings.ts`/`stateSettings.ts`: nothing — these are internal symbols, not command IDs or config keys.
- The chip itself is **live**: `StreamTabs.ts:40` imports it and `:285-287` renders `<worktree-chip .info=${stream.worktree}>`, and `StreamTab.styles.ts:332` styles it. Only the PR half is dead. The proposal correctly keeps `renderBranch`/`worktreeLabel`/`.branch*`/`.dirty-dot`.

**5. Deliberately justified?** No dated doc, ADR, or checklist rule rules on this: `rg -ni "PR enrichment|worktree chip|pr pill|ci icon" docs .claude` → zero hits. The only written statement of intent is the introducing commit `74e899ab56` (2026-05-14) itself, which I read in full: "slots for PR pill, `#NNN`, title, `+A −D`, and CI icon (**unused until the PR enrichment lands**)" and "so a follow-up can layer those on". Three months and four commits later the follow-up has not landed — this is precisely AGENTS.md's compatibility/format-retirement window and the skill's "speculative product generality with no product owner", not a defended design.

**6. Settled surface?** Untouched. `stream.ts` is a wire-contract schema in `src/shared/` (correctly so — no new `@agent/*` import); `WorktreeInfo` is not an `AgentEvent` or `SessionFact` field, so the event-channel ruling is not engaged; no PocketFlow, no platform port, no vscode-free-zone change (both files are already vscode-free), and `stream.ts` is not one of the six browser-reachable `@utils` modules. `StreamTabInfo` is a live webview message (`progressView/outbound.ts:53,68`), **not persisted** — nothing reads a stored `worktree.pr`, so there is no migration-tier concern; and even a stale in-flight payload would be silently stripped by Zod's default object behavior rather than failing to parse.

**7. Ratchets.** `grep -n -i worktree config/ratchets/*.json` → empty, as claimed. The only `src/shared/schemas/stream.ts` row in `knip-baseline.json` is `EXECUTION_META_SCHEMA_VERSION` (:2050-2055) — untouched. Deleting exports can only shrink knip's dead set, never widen a baseline; `host-agent-import-baseline`, `shared-schemas-deep-import`, `host-agent-mock` and `architecture-edges` are all unaffected (no import specifier changes — `WorktreeChip.ts` imports from the `@shared/schemas` barrel, not a deep path).

**8. Masking site?** No. The deletions are `pr ? … : nothing` render branches and lookup tables, not a `catch`, `??` over a failed read, `.catch(default)`, or a swallowing `default:`. Checklist §15 M1-M6 does not apply. The one bug in the neighborhood ("Worktree: undefined", correction 4) is a _display_ defect that the PR should fix loudly rather than inherit.

**9. Net-adds / churn (R5)?** No. Pure deletion; nothing is added except a one-line `import type`. The candidate correctly refuses to rename `worktree-chip`. The only file-count expansion I add is three stale comments (correction 3), all deletions.

**10. Too thin?** No — ~213 lines, 14 declarations, 12 CSS rules and a whole render path across four files, spanning the schema-to-renderer seam. It stands alone.

**Verdict: SURVIVES**, low risk. Post-delete verification should be `npm run typecheck` (the `TeXRAIconName` and `wa-badge` imports become unused; esbuild will not catch that) plus `npx vitest run src/test-kernel/progressView/`, since `StreamTabsExpandChevron.vitest.ts:125-127` also mounts a `worktree` fixture — I checked it and it passes only `{ workingDirectory, branch }`, so it is unaffected.

</details>

#### Collapse three parity-only knobs in the shared webview base classes: `ViewBundle`, the `viewPath` fallback, and the `readyCommand` extension point

- **Lens**: `webview-tree-parity` · **Kind**: speculative-generality · **Risk**: medium
- **Net**: -46 LoC, -5 elements (claimed -52 LoC, -5 elements; verifier figure governs)

**Evidence**

(A) `ViewBundle` PARAMETERIZES A CONSTANT. `BaseViewContentProvider.ts:33-42` declares `interface ViewBundle { dist; bundleKey; styleKey }` and the constructor takes it plus an optional `viewPath` (:49-60), defaulting `viewPath` to `viewName` with a lowercased first char (:57-58). All three production call sites: `MainViewProvider.ts:104-113` `{dist:'webview', bundleKey:'mainViewBundleUri', styleKey:'mainViewStyleUri'}` + `viewPath:'webview'`; `ProgressViewProvider.ts:145-153` `{dist:'progressView', bundleKey:'progressBundleUri', styleKey:'progressStyleUri'}`, no viewPath (derived = 'progressView'); `SettingsViewProvider.ts:29-37` `{dist:'settingsView', …}`, no viewPath (derived = 'settingsView'). `dist === viewPath` in all three — so two parameters carry one fact and the lowercase-derivation fallback is never the value that differs from `dist`. The same folder string is then typed a THIRD time per provider for the resource roots: `MainViewProvider.ts:328-331` `getCombinedLocalResourceRoots(this.context, ['webview','progressView'])`, `ProgressViewProvider.ts:435-438` `'progressView'`, `SettingsViewProvider.ts:87-90` `'settingsView'`. `bundleKey`/`styleKey` exist only because the three parallel templates picked three different names for the identical slot: `webview/index.html:11,22` `${mainViewStyleUri}`/`${mainViewBundleUri}`; `progressView/index.html:11,24` `${progressStyleUri}`/`${progressBundleUri}`; `settingsView/index.html:11,34` `${settingsStyleUri}`/`${settingsBundleUri}`. Two dev scripts pay for the divergence too, each hand-writing a per-view `replacements` map: `scripts/capture-walkthrough-media.mjs:187-191` and `:237-241`, `scripts/smoke-webviews-electron.mjs:293-298` (ambiguous consumers — inspected: both are dev tooling that fills the template by hand, so they must be updated in the same PR, and they get simpler).

(B) `readyCommand` IS AN EXTENSION POINT WITH NOTHING BEHIND IT. `src/shared/BaseWebviewApp.ts:91-96` documents "Override to change or suppress the ready command" and returns `COMMON_COMMANDS.WEBVIEW_READY`; `:173-177` branches on `if (command)` for the suppress case. Subclasses (`rg "extends BaseWebviewApp|BaseWebviewApp as unknown"` → exactly three: `MainApp.ts:131`, `ProgressApp.ts:74-76`, `SettingsApp.ts:125`). MainApp and SettingsApp take the default. ProgressApp overrides at `:144-146` returning `PROGRESS_VIEW_COMMANDS.WEBVIEW_READY` — and `src/shared/ipc.ts:108-109` shows `PROGRESS_VIEW_COMMANDS = { ...COMMON_COMMANDS, … }`, so that is the identical string `'webviewReady'` (`ipc.ts:7`). The override is a no-op; nothing anywhere returns `null`, so the suppress branch is unreachable.

(C) THE READY POST IS COPY-PASTED. `BaseWebviewApp.ts:174-176` does `const view = this.getAttribute('data-desktop-view'); postMessage(command, view == null ? {} : { view });`. `SettingsApp.ts:168-172` repeats that four-line shape verbatim for its monthly-refresh re-post (`SETTINGS_VIEW_COMMANDS.WEBVIEW_READY` — again the same `'webviewReady'` via `ipc.ts:304-305`'s `...COMMON_COMMANDS`).

DEDUPE CHECKS. `gh issue list --state all --search "BundledViewContentProvider"` → 0; `--search "readyCommand"` → 0. `git log -10 -- packages/extension/src/common/webview/BaseViewContentProvider.ts` → last functional touch 6337f454b5 (#10708, logger binding). `git log -8 -- src/shared/BaseWebviewApp.ts` → most recent is c11bf8b1f3 (#11421, a round-1 PR) which only retyped `themeContext`/`theme` to `Theme`; `git show c11bf8b1f3 -- src/shared/BaseWebviewApp.ts` confirms `readyCommand` was untouched. IMPORTANT NON-DUPLICATE NOTE: round 1's shipped batch "drop the phantom onColorTheme activation event … and 11 file-local exports" included removing the `export` keyword from `ViewBundle` — that is already merged and is a different change. This candidate deletes the interface outright.

**Proposal**

1. Rename the template slots in all three `index.html` files to one shared pair: `${styleUri}` (line 11 of each) and `${bundleUri}` (`webview:22`, `progressView:24`, `settingsView:34`).
2. Collapse the content provider's constructor to `(context, viewName, viewFolder)`: delete the `ViewBundle` interface (`BaseViewContentProvider.ts:33-42`), delete the `viewPath` parameter and its lowercase-derivation fallback (:52-58), and have `getHtmlContent` fill the fixed `bundleUri`/`styleUri` keys from `dist/<viewFolder>/bundle.js` and `dist/<viewFolder>/index.css` (:78-89).
3. Update the three providers to pass their single folder string, and take the same constant for `getSharedLocalResourceRoots`/`getCombinedLocalResourceRoots` (`MainViewProvider.ts:104-113`+`:328-331`, `ProgressViewProvider.ts:145-153`+`:435-438`, `SettingsViewProvider.ts:29-37`+`:87-90`) so each provider names its folder once.
4. Update the two dev scripts' `replacements` maps to the shared key names (`capture-walkthrough-media.mjs:187-191,237-241`, `smoke-webviews-electron.mjs:293-298`); each per-view map becomes the same two keys.
5. Delete the `readyCommand` getter (`BaseWebviewApp.ts:91-96`) and ProgressApp's override (`ProgressApp.ts:144-146`); replace the `connectedCallback` branch (:173-177) with an unconditional `this.postReady()`, a small `protected postReady()` that owns the `data-desktop-view` read + `postMessage(COMMON_COMMANDS.WEBVIEW_READY, …)`. Have `SettingsApp.ts:167-173` call `this.postReady()` instead of restating it. (Net: one new 4-line protected method replaces a getter, an override, a null-branch, and a copy — hence the element count still falls.)
   Risk note for the implementer: step 1's rename is the one part TypeScript cannot check — a missed placeholder yields a blank webview at runtime, not a build error. `rg "mainViewBundleUri|progressBundleUri|settingsBundleUri|mainViewStyleUri|progressStyleUri|settingsStyleUri"` (excluding `dist/`) returns exactly the 12 sites listed above and must return 0 after.

**What we give up**

(A) The ability for a view's `dist/` output folder, its `src/` template folder, and its two template variable names to diverge from each other. Nothing has ever used that freedom, and the three trees' aligned directory structure is deliberate — this makes the naming match the alignment. Restoring divergence for a future view means re-adding one parameter. (B) The ability for a subclass to send a different ready command or suppress it. Since every `*_VIEW_COMMANDS.WEBVIEW_READY` is the same `'webviewReady'` literal spread from `COMMON_COMMANDS`, "a different ready command" is not currently expressible; a view that genuinely needed to skip the handshake would override `connectedCallback`, which is one line.

**Verifier corrections to the evidence above**

Seven factual errors / omissions in the evidence, none fatal:

1. WRONG SCRIPT CONSUMER COUNT (the biggest error). The candidate's own risk note claims `rg "mainViewBundleUri|progressBundleUri|settingsBundleUri|mainViewStyleUri|progressStyleUri|settingsStyleUri"` (excluding dist/) "returns exactly the 12 sites listed above". It returns 24 lines: the 12 in `packages/extension/src` (6 provider lines + 6 template lines) PLUS 12 in scripts. The evidence cites only 3 of the 7 script replacement maps. Missed: `scripts/capture-walkthrough-media.mjs:130-132` (inside the `mainWebview()` factory, used twice) and `:159-161` (the inline `main-agent-model` view); `scripts/smoke-webviews-electron.mjs:19-22` (the module-level `progressViewReplacements` const) and `:39-41` (the main view map). Correct count: 4 maps in capture-walkthrough-media.mjs (main x2, settings :186-192, progress :231-242) and 3 in smoke-webviews-electron.mjs (:19-22, :39-41, :293-297) — 7 maps, not 3. The cited ranges themselves are also off by a line or two (settings map is :186-192 not :187-191; smoke settings is :293-297).

2. UNNOTED SCRIPT ASYMMETRY. `smoke-webviews-electron.mjs`'s main (:39-41) and settings (:293-297) maps supply only the bundle key and no style key, so `${mainViewStyleUri}` / `${settingsStyleUri}` are left unreplaced in that harness today (`renderWebviewHtml` in `scripts/webview-electron-harness.mjs:6-14` silently leaves unmatched placeholders). After the rename both views' style slot becomes the same `${styleUri}` name; the implementer must preserve (or deliberately fix) those omissions rather than assume the maps are parallel.

3. WRONG GIT HISTORY. "git log -10 -- BaseViewContentProvider.ts → last functional touch 6337f454b5 (#10708, logger binding)" is wrong twice. The logger migration that touched this file is `99622178a3` (#10707); `6337f454b5` (#10708) is a different logger PR. And it is not the last touch: `1d17d1b29a` (#10875) and, more importantly, `16d66fd077` (#11417 — a round-1 PR merged 2026-08-26) touched it after. `git show 16d66fd077 -- <file>` is the 1-line change that dropped `export` from `ViewBundle`, which the candidate correctly flags as a different, already-merged change — but the "last functional touch" claim is stale by two commits.

4. OFF-BY-A-FEW LINE NUMBERS in `BaseViewContentProvider.ts`. `ViewBundle` is at :34-42 (not :33-42; :33 is blank). The constructor is :52-62, not :49-60 — :49 is the `private readonly viewPath: string` field. The lowercase derivation is at :58-60 (comment :58, assignment :59-60), not :57-58. The replacements object is :76-90, not :78-89. All other cited line numbers verified exact: MainViewProvider :104-113 and :328-331, ProgressViewProvider :145-153 and :435-438, SettingsViewProvider :29-37 and :87-90, the three templates' :11 / :22 / :24 / :34, BaseWebviewApp :91-96 and :169-178 (the post itself is :175-176, not :174-176), ProgressApp :144-146, SettingsApp :168-172, ipc.ts :7 / :108-109 / :303-305.

5. MISSED CORROBORATION that strengthens the claim. `packages/extension/vite.config.ts:7,43,47-48` already treats the folder name as ONE fact: `const webviews = ['progressView','settingsView','webview']`, input `src/${webviewName}/frontend/index.ts`, output `${webviewName}/bundle.js` and `${webviewName}/[name][extname]`. So the build itself already forbids `src/` folder and `dist/` folder from diverging — the `viewPath`-vs-`dist` freedom the provider parameterizes is not merely unused, it is already contradicted by the build config. That file is not in the candidate's path list and does not need editing, but it should be cited.

6. OVERSTATED STEP 3. "so each provider names its folder once" does not hold for MainViewProvider: `:328-331` passes `['webview','progressView']` to `getCombinedLocalResourceRoots` because the sidebar can host the progress surface, so that provider still names a second view's folder regardless. Progress (`getSharedLocalResourceRoots(this.context,'progressView')`) and Settings (`'settingsView'`) do collapse to one mention each.

MISSED CONSUMERS (harmless, but must be checked before the constructor change): three test-kernel mocks stub the class — `src/test-kernel/progressView/ProgressThemeSync.vitest.ts:63`, `src/test-kernel/settings/ExtensionWorkspaceTransition.vitest.ts:75` (both `class {}`), and `src/test-kernel/webview/SidebarSurfaceOwnership.vitest.ts:35-40` (a stub with only `getHtmlContent`). None constructs with real arguments, so the signature change needs no test edits — but the candidate listed zero non-production consumers, which reads as "none exist". Also `scripts/verify-extension-package-invariants.mjs:58-60` and `scripts/extension-package-invariants.snapshot.json:690-692` pin the three `index.html` PATHS (not their placeholder names), so no gate covers the rename — which is exactly why the risk note's "TypeScript cannot check this" caveat is right and must survive into the PR.

<details><summary>Verifier reasoning</summary>

I re-ran every check independently at HEAD (d8c6dfc071).

(A) ViewBundle. Read `packages/extension/src/common/webview/BaseViewContentProvider.ts` in full. `interface ViewBundle` (now unexported, :34-42) carries `dist`/`bundleKey`/`styleKey`; the constructor (:52-62) takes it plus an optional `viewPath` whose fallback is `viewName.charAt(0).toLowerCase() + viewName.slice(1)` (:58-60). `rg BundledViewContentProvider` over the repo (excluding dist/node_modules) returns exactly 3 production construction sites — MainViewProvider :104-113 (`dist:'webview'`, explicit `viewPath:'webview'`), ProgressViewProvider :145-153 (`dist:'progressView'`, derived `'progressView'`), SettingsViewProvider :29-37 (`dist:'settingsView'`, derived `'settingsView'`) — plus 3 test-kernel mocks. So `dist === viewPath` in all three, and the derivation only ever reproduces `dist`. The 'HistoryView' example in the comment at :58 corresponds to no live view. The six template-variable names are confirmed unique per view at `webview/index.html:11,22`, `progressView/index.html:11,24`, `settingsView/index.html:11,34`, all next to a shared `${commonStyleUri}` at :10 — i.e. one slot already proves the shared-name pattern works. `resourceRoots.ts:4-7,21-31` shows both root helpers take the folder string, so the folder is typed a third time per provider. And `vite.config.ts:7,43,47-48` already collapses src-folder and dist-folder into one name, so the parameterized divergence is dead by construction.

ProgressApp's override returns `PROGRESS_VIEW_COMMANDS.WEBVIEW_READY`, and `src/shared/ipc.ts:108-109` spreads `...COMMON_COMMANDS` (:3-9, `WEBVIEW_READY:'webviewReady'`), so it returns the identical literal — a genuine no-op. Nothing returns `null`, so the `if (command)` suppress branch at :174-177 is statically unreachable. This is speculative generality, not a §15 masking site: there is no catch, no fallback over a failed read, no persisted `.catch`, no silently-dropped event.

(C) Duplicated ready post. Confirmed verbatim: `BaseWebviewApp.ts:175-176` and `SettingsApp.ts:168-172` (`SETTINGS_VIEW_COMMANDS.WEBVIEW_READY` resolves through `ipc.ts:303-305`'s `...COMMON_COMMANDS` to the same `'webviewReady'`). Both apps keep other uses of their `*_VIEW_COMMANDS` import (ProgressApp :342,346; SettingsApp :201,224,453,501), so no import churn. The consumers of that string — `ProgressViewMessageHandler.ts:319`, `desktopSettingsIpc.ts:477`, `desktopProgressIpc.ts:107-109`, `desktopViewStateIpc.ts:47`, `desktopOnboardingIpc.ts:170`, `desktopMainViewStartup.ts:64`, and the renderer's own explicit posts at `packages/desktop/src/renderer/main.ts:1526-1527` — all key off the same literal and are unaffected.

DUPLICATE/FILED/DONE. Not in the 90-title round-1 index: the closest entry, "Batch: drop the phantom onColorTheme activation event … and 11 file-local exports", removed only the `export` keyword from `ViewBundle` (confirmed by `git show 16d66fd077 --stat`), and the round-1 item "Derive the fixed template-variable vocabulary from one Zod map" is area `agent-core-node` (prompt template vars), not webview HTML. Grepping the 49-candidate doc for `ViewBundle|readyCommand|bundleKey|styleKey|viewPath|BundledViewContentProvider` hits only those two unrelated entries. `gh issue list --state all --search` for "readyCommand" and "BundledViewContentProvider" → 0 rows. `git log --all --grep=readyCommand` → only #7609 (a knip export batch that never touched these files). Not done at HEAD: the interface, both key fields, the `viewPath` fallback and the ProgressApp override are all present right now.

DELIBERATE RULING. The only dated doc naming any of these symbols is `docs/proposals/2026-08-07-prod-structural-leads-triage.md:383`, an "Unexport micro-sweep" line covering `ViewBundle` — already executed by #11417 and about the `export` keyword, not the interface's existence. Nothing in AGENTS.md, the review checklist, `config/ratchets/`, or docs/architecture defends per-view template-key names or a pluggable ready command.

SETTLED SURFACE. None touched: no `@agent/*` deep import (the ratchet is about host→@agent specifiers; these files import `@shared/ipc`, `@common/webview`, `@logger/logUtils`), no PocketFlow kernel, no AgentEvent/SessionFact channel, no platform port, no browser-safe-utils count, no vscode-free-zone violation (`src/shared/BaseWebviewApp.ts` stays vscode-free; `postMessage` there is the existing shared helper), no knip-baseline row (these are not knip findings), no CLI result-JSON contract.

NET-ADDS. No. One new 4-6 line `protected postReady()` replaces a getter + its doc comment, an override, a 5-line null branch and a 5-line copy; everything else is pure deletion or rename-in-place.

TOO THIN. No — three related knobs in one seam (the shared webview base pair), roughly -45 LoC across 12 files, and the two dev scripts get simpler rather than being collateral churn.

RESIDUAL RISK (why medium, not low): step 1's placeholder rename is checked by nothing in CI — `verify-extension-package-invariants.mjs` pins the template paths, not their contents, and `renderWebviewHtml` leaves unmatched `${…}` in place silently. A missed slot yields a blank webview at runtime. The mitigation is the 24-hit grep going to zero, plus running the electron webview smoke script.

#### Batch two shared-webview style duplications: the 5x-repeated interactive-control selector list and the mirrored prose-textarea rule block

- **Lens**: `large-files` · **Kind**: duplication · **Risk**: low
- **Net**: -45 LoC, 2 elements (claimed -55 LoC, 1 elements; verifier figure governs)

**Evidence**

(a) `src/shared/styles/controlStyles.ts` repeats one verbatim seven-class selector list five times. Verified by regex over the file (`\.btn-primary,\s*\.btn-secondary,\s*\.btn-ghost,\s*\.action-button,\s*\.header-action,\s*\.icon-button,\s*\.action-icon-button`): occurrences at :75-81, :238-244, :252-258, :264-270, :398-404 — five identical blocks, each nine source lines including the `:is(` / `)` wrapper. The sibling file in the same directory already solves exactly this: `src/shared/styles/requestPanelSharedStyles.ts:93-95` defines `selectorGroup(names, suffix): CSSResult` returning `unsafeCSS(...)`, and interpolates the result at :239, :361, :374-375, :380, :418. So the fix is applying the directory's own established pattern, not inventing an abstraction. `controlStyles.ts` already imports `css, type CSSResult` from lit (:22). Two near-variants at :298-306 and :320-326 differ deliberately (one narrows `.action-button`, one drops the two btn skins) and stay as-is. Consumers of `controlStyles` (production): `src/shared/styles/index.ts`, `selectStyles.ts`, `litStyles.ts`, `commonViewStyles.ts`; non-production: `src/test-kernel/progressView/ToolEditRequestPanel.vitest.ts`, `src/test-kernel/settings/SettingsStyleContracts.vitest.ts`, `src/test-kernel/desktop/DesktopControlSystem.vitest.ts`. `unsafeCSS` interpolation produces byte-identical `cssText`, so none of the three tests change meaning. (Noted but out of scope: `packages/desktop/src/renderer/styles.css` carries the same list 5x, but it is a plain `.css` light-DOM mirror with no interpolation available and `controlStyles.ts:11-15` documents why that mirror exists.)

(b) `packages/extension/src/progressView/frontend/components/FollowUpInput.styles.ts:93-107` (`#followUpInput::part(textarea)`) is byte-identical to `src/shared/styles/requestPanelSharedStyles.ts:380-395` (`:is(${FEEDBACK_INPUTS})::part(textarea)`) — all 15 declarations, in the same order, verified line by line: `field-sizing`, `width`, `height`, `min-height`, `max-height`, `padding`, `box-sizing`, `overflow-x`, `overflow-y`, `white-space`, `overflow-wrap`, `font-family`, `font-size`, `line-height`. The host blocks (`FollowUpInput.styles.ts:70-79` vs `requestPanelSharedStyles.ts:361-372`) are identical except the `--textarea-max-height` clamp, which is already the parameterization point. The duplication is self-declared: `requestPanelSharedStyles.ts:355-360` reads "Prose feedback — same sizing contract as FollowUpInput's composer (#9773) … Mirror the follow-up textarea rules so the two prose boxes stay in lockstep." Consumers: `FollowUpInput.styles.ts` has exactly one importer, `FollowUpInput.ts`; `requestPanelSharedStyles.ts` has 8 production importers under `packages/extension/src/progressView/frontend/components/` plus `src/shared/styles/index.ts`.

Dedupe checks run: round-1 index has no styles entry other than the shipped desktop `themeTokens.css` orphan-property and `texra-<kind>` body-class items, neither of which touches these files. `grep -in 'controlStyles|selectorGroup|FollowUpInput.styles|requestPanelSharedStyles' docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md` → 0 hits. `gh issue list --label tech-debt --state all --search "controlStyles selector"` → 0; `--search "textarea styles duplicate"` → 0. `git log --oneline -10 -- src/shared/styles/controlStyles.ts .../FollowUpInput.styles.ts` shows prior work in the neighborhood (`c6cfc0e2ad refactor(ui): remove duplicate composer size rule`, `82ed5f2fc9 fix(ui): unify composer action button`) but neither touched the selector list or the textarea part block.

**Proposal**

1. In `controlStyles.ts`, add one module-local `const INTERACTIVE_CONTROLS: CSSResult = unsafeCSS('.btn-primary, .btn-secondary, .btn-ghost, .action-button, .header-action, .icon-button, .action-icon-button')` (importing `unsafeCSS` from lit alongside the existing `css`), then replace the five nine-line blocks at :75-81, :238-244, :252-258, :264-270, :398-404 with `:is(${INTERACTIVE_CONTROLS})`. No new export — the const is file-local, matching `requestPanelSharedStyles.ts`'s own consts.
2. Move the 15-declaration prose-textarea rule body into one exported `CSSResult` fragment in `src/shared/styles/` (e.g. `proseTextareaPartStyles`), have `requestPanelSharedStyles.ts:380` and `FollowUpInput.styles.ts:93` each interpolate it under their own selector, and leave the two `--textarea-max-height` clamps where they are (they are the intended per-surface difference). Delete the "mirror … stay in lockstep" instruction at `requestPanelSharedStyles.ts:355-360`, replacing it with a pointer to the shared fragment.
3. Run `npm run test -- SettingsStyleContracts DesktopControlSystem ToolEditRequestPanel` to confirm the three suites that read `controlStyles`' cssText are unaffected.

**What we give up**

Nothing behavioral: both edits are `cssText`-preserving. The cost is one new exported CSS fragment (+1 element) and one more indirection when reading either sheet — offset by the drift risk removed, which the mirror comment itself documents as manually maintained. The desktop `styles.css` light-DOM mirror keeps its five copies of the selector list; that mirror is a settled, documented consequence of Shadow DOM not adopting document stylesheets, so this proposal does not touch it.

**Verifier corrections to the evidence above**

Nine factual errors / omissions in the evidence. None of them changes the verdict, but all should be fixed before the PR is written.

1. "unsafeCSS interpolation produces byte-identical cssText, so none of the three tests change meaning" — WRONG ON BOTH HALVES.
   (a) cssText is NOT byte-identical. `:is(\n      .btn-primary,\n      ...\n    ):not([disabled]):hover` becomes `:is(.btn-primary, .btn-secondary, ...):not([disabled]):hover`. Whitespace inside the selector differs; CSS semantics are identical, which is the actual (and sufficient) safety argument.
   (b) The three tests never read cssText. All three read the .ts SOURCE with readFileSync/`read()`: `src/test-kernel/desktop/DesktopControlSystem.vitest.ts:42`, `src/test-kernel/settings/SettingsStyleContracts.vitest.ts:83`, `src/test-kernel/progressView/ToolEditRequestPanel.vitest.ts:198`. They pass only because the literal class names survive verbatim inside the new const string. DesktopControlSystem.vitest.ts:46-49 asserts `toContain('.btn-primary')`, `'.btn-secondary'`, `'.btn-ghost'`, `'.icon-button'` on the source text — so the const MUST be a literal string, not built by `.map()` over an array of names. That constraint belongs in the proposal.

2. Consumer list for `controlStyles` is wrong in both directions. Claimed production consumers `src/shared/styles/index.ts`, `selectStyles.ts`, `litStyles.ts` are not consumers at all — those are comment mentions (`index.ts:8`, `selectStyles.ts:10`, `litStyles.ts:144`). The only in-directory importer is `commonViewStyles.ts:9`. Four real importers were MISSED, all in packages/extension: `src/webview/frontend/components/LatexDiffsSection.ts:19`, `src/webview/frontend/fileSelectStyles.ts:13`, `src/progressView/frontend/progressAppStyles.ts:6`, `src/progressView/frontend/components/UserMessage.ts:27` (all import `buttonStyles`). Harmless — the const is file-local and no exported symbol changes — but the count as stated is wrong.

3. "all 15 declarations" — there are 14. The candidate's own enumeration lists 14 (`field-sizing, width, height, min-height, max-height, padding, box-sizing, overflow-x, overflow-y, white-space, overflow-wrap, font-family, font-size, line-height`). Verified by diffing the two slices: identical, 14 lines.

4. Line range off by one on the FollowUpInput side. The rule is `FollowUpInput.styles.ts:92-107` (selector `#followUpInput::part(textarea) {` is at :92), not :93-107. The requestPanelSharedStyles range :380-395 is correct.

5. Desktop light-DOM mirror count is wrong: `packages/desktop/src/renderer/styles.css` carries the seven-selector list SEVEN times (`.action-icon-button` closers at :102, :261, :274, :285, :316, :335, :378), not five. Out of scope either way, and the file is 1718 lines.

6. MISSED the stronger precedent for proposal step 2. `src/shared/styles/requestPanelSharedStyles.ts:29-33` already defines `truncateTextRule: CSSResult` — a bare declaration-list fragment whose doc comment reads "Single-line text truncation declarations (no selector), interpolated into shadow-part rules below — parts can't take a utility class" — and interpolates it at :317. That is exactly the shape step 2 needs, in the very file being edited. The evidence cites only `selectorGroup`, which is the precedent for step 1.

7. MISSED a second `#followUpInput::part(textarea)` rule at `FollowUpInput.styles.ts:141-144`, inside `@container (max-width: 440px)`, overriding `padding-inline`. It has no counterpart on the requestPanelSharedStyles side (which instead has an `@media (max-height: 900px)` rule at :417-421 for a different selector group). So the "two prose boxes stay in lockstep" mirror is already only partly true; the shared fragment must sit under the base selector only and this override stays FollowUpInput-local.

8. git-log attribution is scrambled. `c6cfc0e2ad` and `82ed5f2fc9` are in `controlStyles.ts`'s history, not FollowUpInput's. `packages/extension/src/progressView/frontend/components/FollowUpInput.styles.ts` has exactly ONE commit in its whole history: `8b71e6149d refactor: code-simplifier sweeps across 132 production source files (#9990)`. That is worth stating positively — the duplication survived a dedicated simplifier sweep of that file, which is evidence it is non-obvious rather than already-triaged.

9. "grep … docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md → 0 hits" is true only for the narrow pattern. `controlStyles|selectorGroup|requestPanelSharedStyles` is genuinely 0, but a bare `FollowUpInput` hits survey line 550 and `textarea` hits :623/:1505 — all unrelated items (a `disabled` descriptor field, `--textarea-h-l` in themeTokens.css, and a `src/shared/utils` barrel note). Not duplicates; the claim just overstates its own grep.

Additional confirmations that were NOT stated and should be: no ratchet touches these paths (`grep 'shared/styles' config/ratchets/` → 0; no knip-baseline row for any of the three files), so the new export needs no baseline change and has two same-PR consumers, satisfying the exports-are-contracts rule.

<details><summary>Verifier reasoning</summary>

RE-VERIFICATION AT HEAD (d8c6dfc071, main).

Duplicate check. Round-1 index (90 titles) has exactly two style-adjacent entries — "Delete 12 orphan custom properties from the desktop themeTokens.css bridge" (shipped) and "Retire the duplicate texra-<kind> body-class family" (shipped). Neither touches controlStyles.ts, requestPanelSharedStyles.ts, or FollowUpInput.styles.ts. Survey doc: `grep -ciE 'controlStyles|selectorGroup|requestPanelSharedStyles'` → 0. Not a duplicate.

Already filed / already done. `gh issue list --label tech-debt --state all --search "controlStyles"` → 0. `--search "duplicate css selector styles"` → 0. `"FollowUpInput textarea"` → #11224 (clipboard-image chip text, closed) and #8097 (pasted-image persistence) — neither is this. All five selector-list repetitions and both textarea blocks are present at HEAD. Not done.

Printing :73-85, :235-272, :394-410 confirms five verbatim seven-name groups.
