#### Delete the six fixed template variables no prompt template anywhere renders

- **Lens**: `resources-and-prompts` · **Kind**: speculative-generality · **Risk**: low
- **Net**: -58 LoC, -8 elements (claimed -60 LoC, -6 elements; verifier figure governs)

**Evidence**

The fixed UserVars vocabulary is 43 keys (`UserVariableValueSchemas`, src/agent/core/definition/AgentCycleOptions.ts:116-160; `USER_VAR_RUNTIME_TOKENS` derived from it at :179-180 after #11419). Six of those keys are computed on every single run, threaded into the persisted user-variable channel, and rendered by NOTHING.

DEAD SET AND EVERY REFERENCE (repo-wide `rg`, excluding node_modules, `**/dist/**`, `.texra-validate-run`, `docs/proposals/`):

- AUTO_EXTRACT_FIGURE -> 4 hits: AgentCycleOptions.ts:69 (type), :148 (schema), userVars.ts:563 (ToolFlagVars union), :591 (assignment).
- AUTO_EXTRACT_TIKZ_FIGURE -> 4 hits: AgentCycleOptions.ts:70, :149, userVars.ts:564, :592.
- INCLUDE_TEX_COUNT -> 4 hits: AgentCycleOptions.ts:71, :150, userVars.ts:565, :593.
- AUTO_COMPILE_INPUT_PDF -> 4 hits: AgentCycleOptions.ts:73, :152, userVars.ts:567, :595.
- PRINT_INPUT_PROMPT -> 4 production hits (AgentCycleOptions.ts:72, :151, userVars.ts:566, :594) + 3 test hits (src/test-kernel/agent/prompt/userVars.vitest.ts:78,82,87).
- MEDIA_CONTENT -> production: AgentCycleOptions.ts:65 (`MEDIA_CONTENT: null`), :146 (`z.null()`), userVars.ts:344 (`Pick<UserVars,'MEDIA_FILE'|'MEDIA_CONTENT'>`), :372 (default literal), :442 (comment). It is initialized to `null` at :372 and NEVER assigned anywhere; only `MEDIA_FILE` is set (:434). Its declared type is literally `null`.

PRODUCTION PROMPT CORPUS, ALL ZERO. I extracted the 43-key vocabulary and grepped every prompt-bearing file under `packages/extension/resources/` (agents/, tool_use_agents/, templates/, goal/, docs/agent-creation/), `prompts/agents/remote/` (21 YAMLs, 4311 lines), `skills/` (42 files), and `docs/`. Each of the six returns 0. For contrast the same scan proves the mechanism works: INSTRUCTION 48 YAML hits, CONTEXT 22, IS_ANTHROPIC_MODEL 5 (presenter.yaml:107, review.yaml:50, research.yaml:28, prover.yaml:35, assistant.yaml:105), AGENT_DOCS_DIR/BUILTIN_*_DIR/CUSTOM_AGENTS_DIR 1 each (creator.yaml), CODEX_GUIDANCE/CLAUDE_CODE_GUIDANCE 2 each (orchestrator.yaml, Lean4/leanOrchestrator.yaml).

THE ONE TS-SIDE PROMPT TEMPLATE DOES NOT USE THEM EITHER. `src/agent/prompt/PromptBuilder.ts:23-49` is the only Nunjucks template living in TypeScript; it renders `{{ CWD }}` (:24), `{% if IS_ANTHROPIC_MODEL %}` (:29), `{% if DEFAULT_BIB_PATH %}` (:39), `{% if AVAILABLE_SKILLS %}` (:40) — that is why CWD and DEFAULT_BIB_PATH are alive despite zero YAML hits, and it is exactly why I re-ran every token through `rg` instead of trusting the YAML scan. None of the six appear in it.

NOT A DOCUMENTED PUBLIC TOKEN. `docs/.vitepress/components/TemplateVarsPalette.vue` publishes 12 tokens (INSTRUCTION, INPUT_FILE, INPUT_CONTENT, CONTEXT_FILE, CONTEXT_CONTENT, EDITED_FILE, EDITED_CONTENT, MEDIA_FILE, ALL_INPUTS, ALL_CONTEXTS, LIST_OF_ALL_INPUTS, LIST_OF_ALL_CONTEXTS) — none of the six. `docs/guide/custom-agents.md:121` states the opposite of MEDIA_CONTENT existing: "Media is the exception: `MEDIA_FILE` is a path, but the media itself is sent to multimodal models separately rather than inlined as text." The model-facing schema docs name only IS_ANTHROPIC_MODEL (`packages/extension/resources/docs/agent-creation/workflow_schema.md:65`, `tooluse_schema.md:41`).

NEVER USED, EVER. `git log --all -S "AUTO_EXTRACT_FIGURE" -- packages/extension/resources prompts docs`, same for PRINT_INPUT_PROMPT and MEDIA_CONTENT: zero commits. No prompt file has ever referenced them.

THIS IS THE PROMPT MIRROR ONLY, NOT THE toolConfig FIELDS. `autoExtractFigure`/`autoExtractTikzFigure`/`attachTeXCount`/`autoCompileInputPdf` keep real readers: src/latex/LatexMediaManager.ts:564,574,582; src/agent/implementations/flows/reflection/nodes/TeXCountNode.ts:26; src/controllers/mainView/MainViewStateRestoreController.ts:62-65; packages/extension/src/progressView/frontend/components/ProposalRequestPanel.ts:305-306. Closed issue #10868 explicitly kept them for that reason ("Sibling flags ... have readers"). Nothing in this candidate touches them.

DEDUPE. Not in round 1: the round-1 index has no matching title, and `grep -ic "MEDIA_CONTENT|AUTO_EXTRACT_FIGURE" docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md` -> 0. Round 1's vocabulary candidate ("Derive the fixed template-variable vocabulary from one Zod map") collapsed the triplicated LISTING, explicitly leaving the key set at 43/43/43 and untouched; this shrinks the set itself. `gh issue list --label tech-debt --state all --search` for "template variable" / "UserVars" / "AUTO_EXTRACT" / "MEDIA_CONTENT" / "PRINT_INPUT_PROMPT": only #10015 (closed, asked to TYPE the grab-bag), #7791, #10404, #10405, #11147 — none proposes removing unused keys, none is a rejection. `git log -12 -- src/agent/prompt/userVars.ts`: no prior attempt.

SAFETY. (a) Persisted checkpoints: `UserVariableChannelRecordSchema` is `z.looseObject(...)` (AgentCycleOptions.ts:188) so channels written by older versions that still carry these keys keep parsing untouched — no migration, no `.catch`. (b) The collision guard survives intact: `assertNoFixedVarCollision` (userVars.ts:~447) rejects a `requiredFilesInternal` name whose generated `X_FILE` OR `X_CONTENT` hits the fixed set; MEDIA_FILE stays fixed, so `MEDIA` still throws loudly — every fixed `*_CONTENT` key has a `*_FILE` twin, so dropping one `_CONTENT` changes no rejection. (c) Nunjucks runs with `throwOnUndefined` disabled, so even a hypothetical user template referencing one of these already renders the same empty output.

**Proposal**

1. In `src/agent/core/definition/AgentCycleOptions.ts`, delete `MEDIA_CONTENT` (type :65, schema :146) and the five tool-flag keys (type :68-73 including the `/** Tool toggles projected for template conditionals. */` comment, schema :148-152). `USER_VAR_RUNTIME_TOKENS` and `FIXED_USER_VAR_KEYS` are derived from the map, so both shrink automatically.
2. In `src/agent/prompt/userVars.ts`: narrow `FileVars` to `Pick<UserVars,'MEDIA_FILE'>` (:344); drop the `MEDIA_CONTENT: null` default (:372); drop the five names from the `ToolFlagVars` union (:563-567) and their five assignments in `getToolFlags` (:591-595); drop the now-unused `shouldSaveModelIO` import (:10). `getToolFlags` keeps CODEX_GUIDANCE / CLAUDE_CODE_GUIDANCE / ROUNDS and no longer touches `agentConfig.toolConfig` at all.
3. Delete the tests that pin only the removed behavior: `src/test-kernel/agent/prompt/userVars.vitest.ts:77-88` (the `PRINT_INPUT_PROMPT` / saveModelIO case) plus the MEDIA_CONTENT lines at :256, :354-355, :426; and `src/test-kernel/agent/core/AgentCycleOptions.vitest.ts:21-23 and :59-68` (the `rejects a string MEDIA_CONTENT` case). Do NOT add replacements — the surviving `MEDIA_FILE` collision case in `userVars.vitest.ts:413` already covers the guard.
4. Optional second step, stated separately because it is riskier and should be a reviewer decision, not a silent rider: `IS_OPENAI_MODEL` (AgentCycleOptions.ts:29,:119; userVars.ts:204,:244) and `IS_GOOGLE_MODEL` (:31,:121; userVars.ts:206,:246) have the same zero renderers, and removing them collapses `ModelProviderFlags` (userVars.ts:~93) from three booleans to one and simplifies its construction in `AgentLaunchContext`. Unlike the six above, their sibling `IS_ANTHROPIC_MODEL` is advertised generically to agent authors as model-specific branching, so a user's out-of-repo custom agent is marginally more plausible here. Ship step 4 only if the reviewer accepts that.

**What we give up**

The ability for a future agent YAML to branch on whether figure/TikZ auto-extraction, TeXcount attachment, or input-PDF auto-compile ran, and on the debug saveModelIO setting. No agent has ever used it in the repo's history, and the underlying `toolConfig` fields stay, so re-adding a key is a two-line change in one map. MEDIA_CONTENT gives up nothing at all: it is a typed `null` that no code assigns and the published docs already describe as non-existent.

**Verifier corrections to the evidence above**

1. MISSED DERIVED CONSUMER (the only substantive omission). The evidence says "`USER_VAR_RUNTIME_TOKENS` and `FIXED_USER_VAR_KEYS` are derived from the map, so both shrink automatically" and stops there. There is a THIRD derived consumer: `buildUserVarPassthrough()` (src/agent/prompt/userVars.ts:72-79) maps every runtime token to the literal `{{ TOKEN }}` so tokens survive a creation-time render. It has two production consumers — src/agent/templates/agentTemplateRenderer.ts:19 (`renderAgentTemplateString`, used for agentTemplate-toolUse.yaml / agentTemplate-workflowSingle.yaml) and src/agent/implementations/agentCreator/agentCreatorFlow.ts:276. Shrinking the vocabulary shrinks that passthrough map, so an LLM-generated agent YAML that emitted `{{ AUTO_EXTRACT_FIGURE }}` during agent creation would no longer pass through literally. No repo template does, and the creator-facing schema docs advertise only `IS_ANTHROPIC_MODEL`, so the behavior change is negligible — but it is a real consumer the candidate did not enumerate, and the ratchet test src/test-kernel/agent/AgentTemplateRenderer.vitest.ts:65-68 pins this surface (it asserts only ALL_CONTEXTS / LIST_OF_ALL_CONTEXTS, so it stays green).

2. WRONG TEST LINE RANGES — two of them would break the build if applied literally.
   a. "the MEDIA_CONTENT lines at :256, :354-355, :426" — src/test-kernel/agent/prompt/userVars.vitest.ts:354 is `MEDIA_FILE: null`, not MEDIA_CONTENT. Only :355 goes. Deleting :354 deletes a surviving assertion.
   b. "src/test-kernel/agent/core/AgentCycleOptions.vitest.ts:21-23" — :21 is `IS_OPENAI_MODEL: true` and :22 is `INPUT_FILES: ['paper.tex']`; :32 asserts `parsed.input.IS_OPENAI_MODEL`. Only :23 goes; deleting :21-22 breaks the surviving assertion (and :21 only goes under the vetoed step 4).
   c. The MEDIA_CONTENT rejection test is :59-71, not ":59-68" (the `it` block closes at 71: `z.ZodError,` on 69, `);` on 70, `});` on 71).

3. MISSED EDIT SITE. The evidence lists MEDIA_CONTENT's production hits but omits the test comment at src/test-kernel/agent/prompt/userVars.vitest.ts:389-391, which justifies the collision guard "for validators like MEDIA_CONTENT's z.null()". That comment and the mirror comment at src/agent/prompt/userVars.ts:440-444 both have to be rewritten — the guard's stated rationale is entirely MEDIA_CONTENT-shaped.

4. MISSED CONSEQUENCE OF STEP 2. After the four `agentConfig.toolConfig.*` reads are removed, `getToolFlags`' first parameter `agentConfig` (src/agent/prompt/userVars.ts:583) is unused. It is not a lint break (`@typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars` is 'off' for this zone, eslint.config.mjs:647), but the param should be dropped, updating the one production call site (userVars.ts:185) and the test call at userVars.vitest.ts:101. That in turn makes the `toolConfig: {...}` block in the test's `baseConfig` (userVars.vitest.ts:69-74, 6 lines) dead. Neither is in the proposal; both are extra deletions, so the LoC claim is if anything conservative.

5. OVERSTATED PRIOR RULING. "Closed issue #10868 explicitly kept them for that reason" — the quoted line ("Sibling flags ... have readers") is from #10868's `attachDiagnostics` section and is about the `toolConfig` fields, not about the prompt mirror. #10868 in fact names `userVars` as one of the "canonical readers" of `autoExtract*`. The conclusion still holds: after this change the fields keep real readers (src/latex/LatexMediaManager.ts:564/574/582, src/agent/implementations/flows/reflection/nodes/TeXCountNode.ts:26, src/controllers/mainView/MainViewStateRestoreController.ts:62-65, packages/extension/src/progressView/frontend/components/ProposalRequestPanel.ts:305-306, plus packages/extension/src/webview/frontend/{store.ts:55-58,persistence.ts:103-104,sessionDefaults.ts:16-18}), so nothing is orphaned — but #10868 is not a ruling in this candidate's favor.

6. UNDERSTATED LOSS. "The collision guard survives intact" is true for the build-time guard, but deleting `MEDIA_CONTENT: z.null()` from `UserVariableChannelRecordSchema` removes a boundary validator: a checkpoint carrying a string `MEDIA_CONTENT` is no longer rejected — it becomes a passthrough custom key. Since `assertNoFixedVarCollision` still rejects a `MEDIA` required-file name via `MEDIA_FILE`, the application cannot produce that shape, so this is defense-in-depth only. It should be stated, not glossed.

7. STEP 4 SHOULD BE DROPPED, NOT LEFT TO THE REVIEWER. I verified it and it is materially worse than steps 1-3: (a) it is not confined to two files — `ModelProviderFlags` is constructed in src/agent/runtime/AgentLaunchContext.ts:497-499 and six test fixtures spell all three booleans (src/test-kernel/agent/prompt/userVars.vitest.ts:135,168,193,283; UserVarsDelegationScope.vitest.ts:77; PromptBuilder.vitest.ts:68-94), so it is exactly the unrelated-fixture churn checklist §13/R5 warns about; (b) packages/extension/resources/docs/agent-creation/workflow_schema.md:65 and tooluse_schema.md:41 advertise `{% if IS_ANTHROPIC_MODEL %}` generically as "model-specific branching", so an out-of-repo custom agent branching on `IS_OPENAI_MODEL` is plausible and would silently go always-false. Ship steps 1-3 only.

8. Minor: "userVars.vitest.ts:413 already covers the guard" — :413 is the error-message string inside that test; the surviving MEDIA_FILE collision cases are the `it.each([...'MEDIA'])` at :394-404 and the named-key test at :406-415. Both survive, so the claim's substance holds.

<details><summary>Verifier reasoning</summary>

DEDUPE (criterion 1). Round-1 index has no matching title. Its nearest neighbor, "Derive the fixed template-variable vocabulary from one Zod map instead of listing all 43 keys three times" (shipped as a5fdd1b504 / #11419), collapsed the triplicated LISTING and left the key set at 43 — I confirmed HEAD still has all 43 keys with all six present (AgentCycleOptions.ts:65,69-73 and :146,148-152). `grep -ic "MEDIA_CONTENT|AUTO_EXTRACT_FIGURE|PRINT_INPUT_PROMPT" docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md` -> 0. Not done at HEAD.

CONSUMER COUNT (criterion 3) — RE-RAN MYSELF, not trusted. Repo-wide `rg` over `.` excluding node_modules, `**/dist/**`, `.texra-validate-run`, `docs/proposals/` for all six tokens returns EXACTLY the four files claimed, at exactly the claimed line numbers (AUTO_EXTRACT_FIGURE 69/148/563/591; AUTO_EXTRACT_TIKZ_FIGURE 70/149/564/592; INCLUDE_TEX_COUNT 71/150/565/593; PRINT_INPUT_PROMPT 72/151/566/594 + 3 test lines; AUTO_COMPILE_INPUT_PDF 73/152/567/595; MEDIA_CONTENT 65/146/344/372/442 + 5 test lines). That single repo-wide grep is authoritative over the whole tree, so it already covers the surfaces I was told to check individually: packages/extension/package.json contributions, commands.ts, src/shared/schemas/*Settings.ts, packages/extension/resources/, prompts/, supabase/functions/ — zero hits in all of them. I separately scanned all 52 YAMLs under packages/extension/resources + prompts/agents/remote and docs/: the only provider-gate hit anywhere is `IS_ANTHROPIC_MODEL` (5 YAMLs + 2 schema docs), which proves the scan method works. No dynamic dispatch escape either: the only string-keyed access paths are `USER_VAR_RUNTIME_TOKENS` (3 derived consumers, all enumerable — FIXED_USER_VAR_KEYS, buildUserVarPassthrough, and the loose channel schema) and Nunjucks rendering, which I checked against the entire template corpus.

NEVER USED, EVER. `git log --all -S` for each token, name-only: the tokens have only ever appeared in src/agent/prompt/userVars.ts, src/agent/utils/userVars.ts (its predecessor), and AgentCycleOptions.ts. Zero commits under packages/extension/resources, prompts, or docs. The one unrelated historical hit (src/common/webview/commands.ts, deleted in "chore: remove unused checkbox commands") is a webview command constant, not a template token.

ALREADY FILED (criterion 2). `gh issue list --state all` for "template variable", "UserVars", "MEDIA_CONTENT", "AUTO_EXTRACT": #10015 (closed — asked to TYPE the grab-bag, which #10346 did; explicitly says nothing about removing unused keys), #7791/#7678 (token passthrough drift), #10403/#10404/#10405 (collision guard, return type, FileVars assertion), #10868 (toolConfig leftovers). None proposes removing unused vocabulary keys and none is a rejection of it. `git log -8` on both files shows no prior attempt.

DELIBERATE JUSTIFICATION (criterion 4). The only stated rationale for the vocabulary is AgentCycleOptions.ts:8-23: "closed on purpose: a misspelled fixed variable is a compile error." That argues for a closed set, not for a set containing keys nothing renders. No ratchet, AGENTS.md rule, or dated proposal defends these six.

SETTLED SURFACE (criterion 5). `UserVars` is repo-root `src/agent/core/definition/`, not the frozen `@agent/*` public manifest; no host imports these tokens (the repo-wide grep proves it), so the host-agent-import baseline is untouched. No PocketFlow, platform-port, browser-safe-utils, AgentEvent/SessionFact or vscode-free-zone surface is involved.

NET-ADDS (criterion 6). Pure deletion. Nothing is relocated, no new symbol, no new file, no baseline widened. `USER_VAR_RUNTIME_TOKENS`, `FIXED_USER_VAR_KEYS`, `buildUserVarPassthrough`, and `UserVariableChannelRecordSchema` all shrink for free because #11419 already made them derived — this candidate is the payoff of that PR, not churn against it.

MASKING SITE (criterion 7). Nothing removed is a catch, `??` fallback, `.catch(default)`, or silent `default: return`. `PRINT_INPUT_PROMPT: shouldSaveModelIO()` is a projection of a debug setting, not error handling; `shouldSaveModelIO` keeps its real consumer at src/agent/debug/debugMessageSaver.ts:57.

PERSISTENCE SAFETY — verified myself, not taken on faith. `UserVariableChannelRecordSchema` is `z.looseObject(optionalizeUserVariableSchemas(...))` (AgentCycleOptions.ts:187-189), so a checkpoint written by an older build that carries `AUTO_EXTRACT_FIGURE: true` or `MEDIA_CONTENT: null` still parses, with the values passing through as unknown custom keys. No migration, no `.catch`, no persisted-data-tier violation.

COLLISION GUARD — verified. `assertNoFixedVarCollision` (userVars.ts:446-454) rejects a `requiredFilesInternal` name if EITHER `X_FILE` or `X_CONTENT` is fixed. `MEDIA_FILE` stays fixed, so `MEDIA` still throws with the same message the test at :406-415 asserts, and the `it.each(['INPUT','CONTEXT','EDITED','MEDIA'])` case at :394 stays green. Removing one `_CONTENT` key changes no rejection.

TOO THIN (criterion 8). It is at the small end but clears the bar: it deletes 6 entries from a persisted, checkpointed, three-times-derived vocabulary, and it severs a genuine cross-layer coupling — after step 2, `getToolFlags` stops reading `agentConfig.toolConfig` entirely and stops calling into `@agent/debug/debugMessageSaver`, so the prompt layer no longer depends on the LaTeX tool-config surface or the debug-settings module at all. That is a structural win, not just a line count, and it is the same size as several round-1 items that shipped.

WHAT IS GIVEN UP, honestly. Any out-of-repo custom agent YAML branching on `{% if AUTO_EXTRACT_FIGURE %}` etc. would silently become always-false. Nunjucks runs with `throwOnUndefined` off, so it degrades to the empty/false render rather than erroring. None of the six is in the published `TemplateVarsPalette.vue` 12-token palette, none is in the model-facing agent-creation schema docs, and docs/guide/custom-agents.md:121 affirmatively documents that media content is NOT inlined — so `MEDIA_CONTENT` gives up literally nothing. This is the reason step 4 must be excluded: `IS_OPENAI_MODEL`/`IS_GOOGLE_MODEL` sit beside a token the schema docs do advertise generically, and their removal also drags in AgentLaunchContext and six test fixtures.

VERDICT: survives, with the seven corrections above folded in and step 4 dropped from scope.

</details>

#### Inline deliverLiveNotification into its one surviving caller and delete src/agent/followUp/liveNotification.ts

- **Lens**: `single-caller-wrappers` · **Kind**: single-caller-wrapper · **Risk**: low
- **Net**: -46 LoC, -2 elements (claimed -45 LoC, -2 elements; verifier figure governs)

**Evidence**

`deliverLiveNotification` is a 54-line module (src/agent/followUp/liveNotification.ts:20) whose docstring at :1-10 still claims it is "The one delivery path for detached live notifications" shared by "execution-status subscriptions, GitHub polling sources". Full-repo grep for the symbol (`grep -rn deliverLiveNotification --include='*.ts' --include='*.tsx' src packages/*/src config docs`) returns exactly four hits: the definition (src/agent/followUp/liveNotification.ts:20), one import (src/tools/github/StreamSubscriptionRegistry.ts:15), one call (src/tools/github/StreamSubscriptionRegistry.ts:104), and one docstring mention (src/tools/github/StreamSubscriptionRegistry.ts:7). PRODUCTION consumers: 1 (StreamSubscriptionRegistry.ts:104). NON-PRODUCTION consumers: 0 — no src/test-kernel file names it, no docs/ file names it. AMBIGUOUS (scripts/, docs/scripts/): 0.

Why the second producer is gone, not merely elsewhere: `git log --oneline --diff-filter=A -- src/agent/followUp/liveNotification.ts` shows it was added by 868d0940f8 (#10475) as a two-producer sharing point. `git log --oneline -S deliverLiveNotification` shows exactly one later commit, 93cc177020 "refactor(executions): drop the model-facing subscribe capability" (2026-08-24), and `git show 93cc177020` confirms it deleted `ExecutionSubscriptionBinder` (276 lines) including its `import { deliverLiveNotification }` and its `private send(text)` call site. The remaining `live_notification` producer, src/agent/runtime/childRunLoop.ts:879, does NOT route through this helper — it calls `deliverChildRunFollowUp` (src/agent/followUp/childRunDelivery.ts:13), which has three independent production callers (src/tools/delegation/DelegationTools.ts:88, src/agent/runtime/childRunLoop.ts:728, :879) and is not part of this candidate.

Dedupe checks run: not in the round-1 index (neither the 49 shipped nor the 41 refuted); `grep -n 'sanitizeTag|liveNotification|deliverLiveNotification|StreamSubscriptionRegistry' docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md` returns nothing; `gh issue list --label tech-debt --state all --search "liveNotification"` returns nothing and the broader "live notification follow-up" search returns only unrelated CLOSED #11282/#10672/#10669/#5451; `git log --all --grep=deliverLiveNotification` returns only bf635cda98/8168906b38 (the same continuation-fencing commit on two refs). No knip-baseline row (`grep -n liveNotification config/ratchets/knip-baseline.json` is empty), no ratchet or settled-surface conflict — src/tools is not a host, so the @agent/* deep-import baseline does not apply here.

The inline is clean because the call site already has everything the wrapper abstracts: src/tools/github/StreamSubscriptionRegistry.ts:16-19 already imports `currentSession` and `SessionHandle` from @agent/runtime/SessionHandle, and :63/:73 already own `this.logger`. The registry always passes `session: subscription.owner` (:107), so the wrapper's optional `session?` field and its `session ?? currentSession()` fallback (src/agent/followUp/liveNotification.ts:44) are both statically dead at the only call site.

**Proposal**

1. In src/tools/github/StreamSubscriptionRegistry.ts, replace the `deliverLiveNotification({...})` call at :104-115 with the wrapper's body specialized to this site: `void submitFollowUp(streamId, text, { session: subscription.owner, mode: 'live_notification' }).then((result) => { if (result.status !== 'sent' && result.status !== 'queued') return; subscription.owner.events.emit({ scope: 'session', event: { type: 'updateQueuedFollowUps', payload: { streamId } } }); }).catch((err: unknown) => { this.logger.warn('Failed to deliver subscription follow-up', { data: { key, streamId, err } }); });`
2. Swap the import at :15 from `@agent/followUp/liveNotification` to `submitFollowUp` from `@agent/followUp` (the module's documented barrel, which already exports it).
3. Delete src/agent/followUp/liveNotification.ts.
4. Update the registry's module docstring at :7 to stop naming `deliverLiveNotification` and instead say the callback submits a `live_notification` follow-up directly.
5. Drop the now-unused `currentSession` import from the registry only if nothing else in the file uses it (it is used elsewhere — verify before removing).
6. Run `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, and `npm run check:dead-code-ratchet` (no baseline row to remove; the check must simply stay green).

**What we give up**

The named sharing point for "detached live notification" delivery. If a second detached producer is ever added, it re-derives the submit/emit/warn sequence — about ten lines — or re-extracts the helper with two real callers, which is what the repo rule ("single-caller extractions are banned") asks for. Nothing behavioural changes: the same mode, the same queued-follow-ups emit, the same warn-on-failure.

**Verifier corrections to the evidence above**

**C1 (material). "no docs/ file names it" is FALSE — and the stated grep command contradicts the stated result.** The evidence says the grep included `docs` and returned "exactly four hits". Re-running it returns a fifth: `docs/proposals/2026-08-14-delegation-flow-substrate-consolidation.md:54` — "**A8 stage 1 landed** (one `deliverLiveNotification` owner for the copy-pasted post-notification sequence)". That is precisely a dated-proposal ruling that created this module on purpose. It does not veto (its premise was two producers; one was deleted by 93cc177020, `git merge-base --is-ancestor 93cc177020 HEAD` = true), but the PR must say so explicitly rather than claim no doc mentions the symbol. Two more name-hits are build artifacts, not consumers: `packages/agent/dist/types/src/agent/followUp/liveNotification.d.ts:4` and `packages/desktop/dist/main/metafile.json` (3 sites). The honest count is 4 source hits + 1 proposal + 2 build outputs.

**C2. Wrong call-site span.** The call is `StreamSubscriptionRegistry.ts:104-113`, not `:104-115`. (:114 is `};` closing `onEvent`, :115 is `const disposable = ...`.) The `session: subscription.owner` line is :107 — that one is right.

**C3. Coverage attribution is understated.** "NON-PRODUCTION consumers: 0 — no src/test-kernel file names it" is literally true of the symbol but hides that this exact sequence is pinned by three tests in `src/test-kernel/agent/followUp/GitHubSubscriptionProgressEvents.vitest.ts`: `:239` "passes the bind-time session to detached subscription follow-ups" (asserts `{ session, mode: 'live_notification' }`), `:267` "rebinds an existing subscription to the session that rebound it", `:299` "warns instead of leaking an unhandled rejection when delivery fails" (asserts the `logger.warn` message and `{ key, streamId }` data). They drive it through the registry, so they survive the inline unchanged — that is a safety net, and it should be named in the PR as the verification, not as an absence.

**C4. Proposal step 2 picks the wrong door.** `@agent/followUp/index.ts:1-19` documents itself as the _host_ surface ("the hosts (CLI, desktop, extension) import instead of deep-reaching each follow-up module by path") and is pinned per-host in `config/ratchets/host-agent-import-baseline.json`. `src/tools` is not a host, and every other `src/tools` consumer deep-imports the defining module: `DelegationTools.ts:29`, `inquiry/inquiryContinuation.ts:19`, `agentCliShared.ts:24`. Routing through the barrel would also re-export four symbols out of a module the test at C3 mocks with only `submitFollowUp`. Use `import { submitFollowUp } from '@agent/followUp/ToolUseFollowUp';` — same edge shape as the three existing tool consumers, no new specifier anywhere.

**C5 (behavioral). The proposed snippet is NOT behavior-preserving.** `liveNotification.ts:37` destructures `const { streamId, session } = delivery;` — the owner handle is captured _before_ the await and the emit fires on that captured handle. The snippet instead reads `subscription.owner.events.emit(...)` _inside_ `.then`, and `owner` is a mutable field that `bind()` reassigns on rebind (`StreamSubscriptionRegistry.ts:90`: `existing.owner = session;`). A rebind landing between promise resolution and the emit would move the `updateQueuedFollowUps` refresh to a different session. Fix: `const owner = subscription.owner;` before the call, then use `owner` in both the `submitFollowUp` options and the `.then` emit.

**C6. Step 5 is a non-step.** `currentSession` is used at `:88` (`const session = currentSession();` inside `bind()`) and `SessionHandle` at `:57` (`BoundSubscription.owner`); the `@agent/runtime/SessionHandle` import at `:16-19` is untouched. No import is removed — only `:15` is swapped.

**C7. LoC.** The deleted module is 54 lines (`wc -l`). The call site goes from 10 lines to ~17-18 after prettier plus the C5 owner capture; the import line is 1→1. Net ≈ **-46**, not -45. Element count -2 is correct (one module, one export); there is no knip-baseline row to remove (`grep liveNotification config/ratchets/knip-baseline.json` empty — confirmed).

**C8 (minor). "exactly one later commit" holds only after de-duping refs.** `git log --all -S deliverLiveNotification` returns five: `93cc177020` and `b01692b58e` (same executions commit on two refs) plus `868d0940f8`, `3122ace2bc`, `05fcaa9a5a` (three refs of #10475). The conclusion is right; the count as written is not.

<details><summary>Verifier reasoning</summary>

Re-ran every claim rather than trusting the write-up.

**Not a duplicate.** `grep -in 'liveNotification|live notification|StreamSubscription|followUp' scratchpad/round1-index.md` returns only two unrelated round-1 titles (un-exporting trace/roster/review/followUp types; inlining `hasPersistedParent`). `grep -n 'liveNotification|StreamSubscriptionRegistry|deliverLiveNotification|sanitizeTag' docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md` exits 1. Not already done at HEAD — the module and its single call site are both live at `d8c6dfc071`.

**Not already filed.** `gh issue list --state all --search "deliverLiveNotification"` is empty; `--search "StreamSubscriptionRegistry"` returns only CLOSED #10745 (bind() reordering) and CLOSED #10645 (release-hook cross-session wipe), neither of which is this. `git log --oneline -10` on both paths shows the last touch to the helper is `bf635cda98` (continuation-generation fencing), nothing that pre-empts the inline.

**Consumer count re-grepped and confirmed as 1 production consumer.** `grep -rn 'deliverLiveNotification|liveNotification' --include='*.ts' --include='*.tsx' --include='*.json' --include='*.md' --include='*.mjs' src packages config docs scripts prompts` gives exactly: the definition (`liveNotification.ts:20`), the import (`StreamSubscriptionRegistry.ts:15`), the call (`:104`), the registry docstring (`:7`), plus the proposal line and build artifacts in C1. Nothing in `packages/extension/package.json`, `commands.ts`, `resources/`, `src/shared/schemas/*Settings.ts`, `supabase/functions/`, or `prompts/` — the symbol is not a command id, setting key, tool name, or YAML-reachable name, so dynamic dispatch is not in play.

**The second-producer story checks out.** `git log --diff-filter=A` confirms `868d0940f8` (#10475) added the module. `git show --stat 93cc177020` confirms it deleted `ExecutionSubscriptionBinder.ts` (276 lines) along with the `executions` tool's subscribe/unsubscribe actions and `SessionHandle.subscriptions`, and that commit is an ancestor of HEAD. The remaining `mode: 'live_notification'` producers are `ToolUseFollowUp.ts` itself (:54/:139/:161, the option's own definition and handling), `childRunDelivery.ts:17`, and `childRunLoop.ts:883` — the last routes through `deliverChildRunFollowUp`, whose three production callers I re-grepped and confirmed (`DelegationTools.ts:88`, `childRunLoop.ts:728`, `:879`). So `deliverLiveNotification` genuinely has one caller and no sibling producer waiting to reuse it.

**No settled-surface or ratchet conflict.** `config/ratchets/host-agent-import-baseline.json` scopes itself to `packages/cli/src`, `packages/desktop/src`, `packages/extension/src`, and `packages/agent/src` — `src/tools` is outside it, so neither deleting the `@agent/followUp/liveNotification` edge nor adding an `@agent/followUp/ToolUseFollowUp` one moves the baseline. `architecture-edges-baseline.json` has no `followUp` row. `knip-baseline.json` has no row. Nothing in the vscode-free-zone list, the six browser-safe utils, the PocketFlow engine, or the AgentEvent/SessionFact split is touched — `src/agent/followUp` and `src/tools/github` are both already vscode-free and stay that way.

**Not a masking-site misread.** The `.catch` being moved is a loud `logger.warn` with structured data (checklist §15 wants exactly this), and it is preserved verbatim at the call site. No `catch {}`, no `??`-over-failure, no Zod `.catch` is created or destroyed. The one statically-dead branch removed is `session ?? currentSession()` (`liveNotification.ts:44`), dead because `SessionHandle` is non-optional on `BoundSubscription.owner` (`:57`) and `:107` always supplies it — deleting an unreachable fallback is not deleting a fallback.

**Not net-adds.** One module and one export go away; the call site grows ~7-8 lines. No new type, no new helper, no public API widened. The repo rule it satisfies is explicit in CLAUDE.md: "single-caller extractions are banned."

**Not too thin, but thin.** -46 LoC / -2 elements at low risk with three existing tests as the safety net is a real if small win, and it is exactly the shape the "single-caller wrappers, facades and factories" lens exists to find. It should ride in a batch with sibling single-caller inlines rather than as a solo PR.

**Residual risk is C5.** The candidate's own proposed snippet introduces a rebind race the current code does not have. With the `const owner = subscription.owner;` capture it is behaviour-identical; without it, the change is not a refactor. That is the one thing an implementing PR must get right, and `src/test-kernel/agent/followUp/GitHubSubscriptionProgressEvents.vitest.ts:267` is close enough to the seam that a reviewer will ask.

</details>

#### Delete the write-only top-level `ToolResult.lineChanges` roll-up and the `recordEdits` accumulator that feeds it

- **Lens**: `tool-layer-dup` · **Kind**: dead-export · **Risk**: low
- **Net**: -28 LoC, -3 elements (claimed -45 LoC, -3 elements; verifier figure governs)

**Evidence**

`ToolResult` carries `lineChanges` in TWO places: per-edit (`edits[].lineChanges`, `EditRecordSchema`, src/shared/schemas/toolResult.ts:36) and as a top-level roll-up on the executed variant (src/shared/schemas/toolResult.ts:155) with an `z.undefined().optional()` mirror on the error variant (:170).

WRITERS of the top-level field (production, exhaustive):

- src/agent/implementations/flows/tooluse/toolUseRound/ToolUseDispatchNode.ts:435-439 — the ONLY writer: `if (result.status === 'executed' && !result.lineChanges && trackedEdits.lineChanges) { result.lineChanges = ...; extracted.sanitizedResult.lineChanges = ...; }`.
- No tool sets it. I checked every `lineChanges:` literal in src/tools: src/tools/fileEditFlow.ts:287 and src/tools/AcceptRunFilesTool.ts:251 both write it INSIDE the `edits[]` array, not at top level (read both call sites in full).

READERS: none. `rg -n "lineChanges" --glob '!node_modules' --glob '!**/dist/**'` over the whole repo returns 42 hits; classified:

- production writers/strippers: ToolUseDispatchNode.ts:260 (`lineChanges: _lineChanges` discarded in `fanOutDuplicateResults`), :435-439; src/agent/core/tools/toolAttachmentExtraction.ts:12 (`ERROR_PAYLOAD_STRIPPED_KEYS`).
- production readers, all off `edits[]` or off a host-local computation, never off the top-level field: src/shared/transcript/toolRowModel.ts:521-536 (`editsByPath.get(dest)?.lineChanges`, built from `outputEdits(ctx.parsedOutput)`); packages/extension/src/progressView/frontend/formatters/logFormatters/toolFormatters/toolSections.ts:109-110 and packages/cli/src/chat/tui/panes/toolRenderers.tsx:180-181 (both read `ToolSectionFile.lineChanges`, produced by toolRowModel from `edits[]`); packages/desktop/src/main/desktopDiffHost.ts:162 (calls `computeLineChangeSummary` itself); src/tools/approval/toolEditApproval.ts:111,266 (approval result, feeds `edits[]`).
- src/agent/core/state/AgentWorkspaceState.ts:114-150 — `recordEdits` accumulates `totalAdded`/`totalRemoved` and returns `lineChanges` purely to feed ToolUseDispatchNode.ts:438. `rg -n "recordEdits"` gives exactly ONE production caller (ToolUseDispatchNode.ts:430) plus src/test-kernel/agent/AgentWorkspaceWorkPlanState.vitest.ts:36,53.
- src/agent/modelHandlers/utils/toolAttachmentUtils.ts `formatToolResultAsText` (:234-276) — the model-facing serializer — reads output/userPatch/userInstruction/error/summary/attachmentSummary only; never `lineChanges`.
- non-production: 6 test-kernel hits, all on `edits[].lineChanges` or on `recordEdits`'s return.
- packages/trace-viewer and packages/agent/src: zero hits.

The persisted transcript does carry it (ToolUseDispatchNode.ts:469 spreads `extracted.sanitizedResult` into the log payload) but no renderer reads `parsedOutput.lineChanges` — `rg` finds no such access; toolRowModel goes through `outputEdits`.

Dedupe: `gh issue list --label tech-debt --state all --search "lineChanges"` → #10580 (CLOSED) and #6705 (CLOSED). #10580's closing ruling explicitly preserves "the historical `edits[].lineChanges` wire" and says nothing about the top-level roll-up, so this does not re-open it. `git log --all --grep=lineChanges` → only #6493, the FileInteractionState reuse commits, and "eliminate fallbackLineChanges spaghetti" (all older). `git log --oneline -12 -- src/shared/schemas/toolResult.ts` → last touch #11421 (round 1, which dropped a different field, `issues` on ValidationErrorDiagnosticsSchema). Not in the round-1 index of 90 titles, and grepping docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md for `lineChanges` returns only line 1810, where the verifier of an unrelated candidate incidentally confirms the same data path I traced.

**Proposal**

1. src/shared/schemas/toolResult.ts: delete `lineChanges: LineChangesSchema.optional()` from `ExecutedToolResultSchema` (:154-155) and the `lineChanges: z.undefined().optional()` mirror from `ErrorToolResultSchema` (:170). `LineChangesSchema` stays, still used by `EditRecordSchema` (:36).
2. src/agent/implementations/flows/tooluse/toolUseRound/ToolUseDispatchNode.ts: delete the `if (... !result.lineChanges && trackedEdits.lineChanges)` block at :435-439, and drop `lineChanges: _lineChanges` from the destructure at :260 (the field no longer exists, so nothing to strip).
3. src/agent/core/state/AgentWorkspaceState.ts: narrow `recordEdits`'s return type to `{ paths: string[] }` and delete `totalAdded`/`totalRemoved` and the trailing `lineChanges:` ternary (:112-150). The per-path `this.edits` map accumulation stays — `editedFilePaths` and the workspace snapshot still use it.
4. src/agent/core/tools/toolAttachmentExtraction.ts: drop `'lineChanges'` from `ERROR_PAYLOAD_STRIPPED_KEYS` (:12).
5. src/test-kernel/agent/AgentWorkspaceWorkPlanState.vitest.ts:56: delete the `expect(merged.lineChanges)` assertion; keep the per-path merge assertions.
6. Run `npm run typecheck`, `npm test`, `npm run check:dead-code-ratchet`.

**What we give up**

Nothing observable. The persisted tool-use log stops carrying a redundant per-call `+N/-M` roll-up that no renderer or model-facing serializer ever read; the same numbers remain available per file under `edits[].lineChanges`, which is what all three hosts already render. If a future UI wants a call-level total it is a one-line fold over `edits[]` at the render boundary, or a re-add of the accumulator to `recordEdits` (about 10 lines).

**Verifier corrections to the evidence above**

Errors and omissions in the candidate's evidence, all verified at HEAD (8968988375):

1. **Missed parse site — the evidence's "READERS: none" enumeration is incomplete.** `src/transcript/completedRunArchive.ts:128` runs `ToolResultSchema.safeParse({ ...tool.output, status: tool.isError ? 'error' : 'executed' })` over _persisted_ `ToolUseLog.output`, which for historical runs DOES carry a top-level `lineChanges` (written by `ToolUseDispatchNode.ts:439` into `extracted.sanitizedResult`, spread into the log at `:469`). The evidence never mentions this site. It is not a blocker — Zod v4 `z.object` strips unknown keys, and `formatToolResultAsText` (`src/agent/modelHandlers/utils/toolAttachmentUtils.ts:234-275`, verified in full) reads only `output`/`userPatch`/`userInstruction`/`error`/`summary`/`attachmentSummary` — but the claim "no production reader parses this field" needed this site named. Side effect worth stating in the PR body: today a historical _error_-status row that happens to carry a stray `lineChanges` fails `safeParse` against `lineChanges: z.undefined().optional()` and silently falls through to `stringifyConversationValue`; deleting the mirror makes it parse. That is a strict improvement, not a regression.

2. **Wrong line number:** `ERROR_PAYLOAD_STRIPPED_KEYS` has `'lineChanges'` at `src/agent/core/tools/toolAttachmentExtraction.ts:13`, not `:12` (the `new Set([` opens at `:10`). Its only use is `:56` (`if (status === 'error' && ERROR_PAYLOAD_STRIPPED_KEYS.has(key))`), which the evidence never cites.

3. **Wrong block extent:** the fill block in `ToolUseDispatchNode.ts` is `:433-440` (8 lines), not `:435-439`. `:430-432` is the `recordEdits` call, which stays (`trackedEdits.paths` still feeds `editedFiles` at `:442`).

4. **Imprecise range in step 3:** in `src/agent/core/state/AgentWorkspaceState.ts` the method spans `:112-151`, not `:112-150`. The lines that actually go are `:114` (return-type member), `:121-122` (`totalAdded`/`totalRemoved` decls), `:139-141` (blank + the two `+=`), and `:144-150` (the return object, collapsing to one line). `:128-129` (`const added` / `const removed`) must STAY — they feed the per-path `this.edits` map. The `LineChanges` type import stays used by `private readonly edits = new Map<string, LineChanges>()` at `:56`.

5. **Test edit is understated:** deleting only `:56` leaves `const merged =` at `:53` unused (`:57` reads `rehydrated.interactions.toSnapshot()`), which trips `no-unused-vars`. The call at `:53-55` must become a bare `rehydrated.interactions.recordEdits([...])`.

6. **Classification of `src/tools/approval/toolEditApproval.ts:266-267` is right but stated loosely:** `result.lineChanges ??` there is on `ToolEditApprovalResult` (declared `:58-61`), a different type entirely from `ToolResult`; it is unaffected. Same for `src/test-kernel/desktop/DesktopToolEditApproval.vitest.ts:522`, which the evidence omitted from its "non-production" tally — it asserts on `ToolEditApprovalResult`, not `ToolResult`.

7. **Hit count:** `rg -n "lineChanges"` with node_modules/dist excluded returns 45 lines at HEAD, not 42 (three of them — `src/shared/schemas/index.ts:10`, `output.ts:8`, `prompts.ts:12` — are module-path `./lineChanges` imports, not the field).

8. **LoC claim is inflated.** Counted line by line: toolResult.ts −3 (`:154-155` incl. doc comment, `:170`); ToolUseDispatchNode.ts −9 (block `:433-440` = 8, destructure `:260` = 1); AgentWorkspaceState.ts −14 (4→1 signature, −2 decls, −3 blank+`+=`, 7→1 return); toolAttachmentExtraction.ts −1; test −1. Net **−28**, not −45.

9. **Missed adjacent win (upside, not an error):** once `lineChanges` leaves the return, `recordEdits` returns a one-member object and could return `string[]` directly, folding `trackedEdits.paths` to `trackedEdits` at `ToolUseDispatchNode.ts:442`. Another ~2 lines and one fewer shape. Optional; include it or don't, but decide deliberately.

10. **Dedupe framing on #10580 is correct.** I re-read the closing comment: it preserves "the historical `edits[].lineChanges` wire and internal execution tracking" and rules only against a forced canonical schema. The top-level roll-up is neither. `gh issue list --state all --search "recordEdits"` and `--search "ToolResult roll-up write-only"` return nothing; `git log --all --grep=recordEdits` shows only older FileInteractionState reuse work. The `round1-index.md` (95 lines, 90 titles) has zero matches for `lineChanges`/`toolResult`/`AgentWorkspaceState`/`DispatchNode`. `git log -12 -- src/shared/schemas/toolResult.ts` last touch is #11421 (round 1) which removed a different field.

11. **One dedupe fact the evidence got backwards in spirit:** `git log --all --grep=lineChanges` surfaces `10d8c0f5a7 "refactor: eliminate fallbackLineChanges spaghetti code"` (Dec 2025), which is the commit that _created_ this exact fill — it moved the roll-up out of a parameter and onto `result.lineChanges` "so data is set at the source." The evidence lists it as merely "older." It is a prior deliberate decision about this specific code and the PR body must address it: that commit made the roll-up a field instead of a parameter; it never established a reader, and eight months later there still is none. That is an argument for deletion, not against it, but it must be named rather than glossed.

<details><summary>Verifier reasoning</summary>

Independent verification, everything re-run at HEAD 8968988375 on main:

**The field.** `grep -n lineChanges src/shared/schemas/toolResult.ts` → exactly 4 hits: `:3` import, `:36` the per-edit `EditRecordSchema` member, `:155` the executed-variant roll-up, `:170` the `z.undefined().optional()` mirror on the error variant. The two-places claim holds.

**Writers.** Repo-wide `rg -n "lineChanges" --glob '!node_modules' --glob '!**/dist/**' --glob '!**/out/**'` → 45 lines, classified individually. The only assignment to the top-level field is `ToolUseDispatchNode.ts:438-439`. I read both tool-side writers in full: `src/tools/fileEditFlow.ts:278-292` returns `{ status: 'executed', ..., edits: [{ path, lineChanges, ...startLine }] }` — inside `edits[]`; `src/tools/AcceptRunFilesTool.ts:249-253` does `edits.push({ path, lineChanges, startLine })` — also inside `edits[]`. No tool writes the roll-up. Confirmed.

**Readers.** Every remaining hit is off `edits[]` or a host-local recomputation: `src/shared/transcript/toolRowModel.ts:70,521,532,536` builds `ToolSectionFile.lineChanges` from `editsByPath` (populated by `outputEdits`, `:296`/`:309`, which reads `output.edits`); the two renderers (`packages/extension/.../toolSections.ts:109-110`, `packages/cli/src/chat/tui/panes/toolRenderers.tsx:180-181`) read that `ToolSectionFile`, not `ToolResult`; `packages/desktop/src/main/desktopDiffHost.ts:162` calls `computeLineChangeSummary` itself; `toolEditApproval.ts:58,111,266` is a different type. I additionally checked for a generic key-spraying renderer — `rg "Object\.(entries|keys)\(" ` over `packages/extension/src/progressView/frontend/formatters`, `src/shared/transcript`, `packages/cli/src/chat/tui/panes` → zero hits, so no renderer enumerates unknown output keys and no UI would lose a line. `packages/trace-viewer` and `packages/agent/src`: zero `lineChanges` and zero `ToolResult` hits respectively, so the frozen SDK surface is untouched (guardrail 5 clear).

**The accumulator.** `rg -n "recordEdits"` → one production caller (`ToolUseDispatchNode.ts:430`) plus two test lines. I read `AgentWorkspaceState.ts:55-151` in full: `totalAdded`/`totalRemoved` exist solely to build the returned `lineChanges`, whose only consumer is `:438`. The per-path `this.edits` map is separately consumed by `editedFilePaths` (`:98-99`, read at `ToolUseCycleNode.ts:232`) and `toSnapshot` (`:82-88`, whose `edits[].path` is read at `tooluse/nodes/types.ts:77`), so the candidate is right to keep it.

**Type-level blast radius.** The only compile-breaking site beyond the schema is the destructure `lineChanges: _lineChanges` at `ToolUseDispatchNode.ts:260` (destructuring a key absent from both union members is a TS error), which the proposal already removes. `ERROR_PAYLOAD_STRIPPED_KEYS` is a `Set<string>` matched against runtime keys at `:56`, so it neither breaks nor is strictly required to change — dropping `'lineChanges'` is dead-entry cleanup, correct but cosmetic. Because this is a plain identifier deletion and the identifier grep is exhaustive, I did not run a full `npm run typecheck` (working tree on main, other sessions active); the implementing PR must.
