#### Fold the remote-agent-docs CI job into docs-check instead of paying a second runner for the same docs-only PRs

- **Lens**: `scripts-and-ratchets` · **Kind**: duplication · **Risk**: low
- **Net**: -32 LoC, -1 elements

**Evidence**

`.github/workflows/ci.yml:225-251` defines a whole job, `remote-agent-docs`, whose only real step is `:250-251` `run: npm run sync:remote-agents`. Its gate is `:228`: `needs.changes.outputs.code != 'true' && needs.changes.outputs.remote_agent_docs == 'true' && github.event_name != 'schedule'`.

`docs-check` (`:499-536`) is gated at `:505` on `needs.changes.result == 'success' && needs.changes.outputs.code != 'true'` — a strict superset of the condition above (on `schedule`/`push`, `code` is forced to `true` at `:50-54`, so `code != 'true'` already implies `pull_request`). So every PR that runs `remote-agent-docs` also runs `docs-check`.

The two jobs' setup is identical, step for step: checkout `actions/checkout@v6` (`:232-233` vs `:509-510`), `pnpm/action-setup@v6` with `run_install: false` (`:234-237` vs `:511-514`), `actions/setup-node@v5` node 22 + pnpm cache keyed on pnpm-lock.yaml (`:239-245` vs `:516-522`), then `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile --ignore-scripts` (`:248` vs `:525`). That is a duplicated runner, a duplicated cold install, and ~27 lines of YAML to run one `npm run` line that docs-check could run as a conditional step.

The split is a leftover from a hurried fix, not a considered design. `git log --oneline -3 -S remote-agent-docs -- .github/workflows/ci.yml` → `e80f6383ce` ("ci: require docs-only remote agent validation") then `bb93f63495` (2026-08-14, "fix(ci): preserve docs build with remote agent gate"). `git show bb93f63495` shows the prior design classified `docs/supabase/**` as "code", which suppressed the public docs build; the fix carved out a separate job and left the comment at `:82-83` ("their separate drift job must not suppress the public docs build"). Running the step _inside_ docs-check satisfies that comment exactly — the docs build still runs.

The job also drags a tail of plumbing through validate-status: the `needs` entry at `:544`, the loop row at `:578`, and the six-line special case at `:601-606` (which exists only because the separate job can legitimately be `skipped`; a step inside docs-check is covered by the existing docs-only gate at `:595-600`).

Production vs non-production: `sync:remote-agents` (package.json:58 → `scripts/sync-remote-agents.mjs`, 502 lines) is production release tooling and stays untouched; `git grep -n sync:remote-agents` shows exactly two invocations, `:223` in `checks` and `:251` in `remote-agent-docs`. The `remote_agent_docs` output (`:36`, `:53`, `:127-131`) and the `drift_gate_match` classifier plus its three self-tests (`:71-80`, `:98-109`) all stay — the folded step still keys off that flag.

Dedupe checks run: not in the round-1 index (round 1 was directory-local and `.github/workflows/` items in it were prettier/contributes-shaped, not CI-job-shaped); `grep -n "remote-agent-docs|sync-remote-agents" docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md` → no hits. `git log --oneline -5 -- .github/workflows/ci.yml` → 2f032b621e, c60c11c7e7, e7473daae9, 4a17794996, 7892adab7d — none revisited the job split since bb93f63495. `gh issue list --label tech-debt --state all --search "remote agents"` and `--search "ci"` returned nothing on this; the closed #8896 (npm-script matrix prune) is the nearest precedent and is about npm scripts, not jobs. Memory note "Minimize CI spend" argues for this direction.

**Proposal**

1. Delete the `remote-agent-docs` job (`.github/workflows/ci.yml:225-251`).
2. Add one step to `docs-check`, after `Install formatting dependencies` (`:524-525`):
   `- name: Check remote-agents catalog generates` / `if: needs.changes.outputs.remote_agent_docs == 'true'` / `run: npm run sync:remote-agents`.
3. Remove `- remote-agent-docs` from validate-status `needs` (`:544`) and its row from the results loop (`:578`).
4. Delete the now-unreachable special case at `:601-606` — a docs-only PR touching the config file is already covered by the docs-only gate at `:595-600`, which requires `docs-check` to have actually succeeded.
5. Leave `changes` untouched: `remote_agent_docs` (`:36,:53,:127-131`) and `drift_gate_match` + self-tests (`:71-80,:98-109`) still drive the new step's `if:`.
6. Verify by opening a scratch PR that touches only `docs/supabase/remote-agents.config.json` and confirming docs-check runs the step and validate-status gates on it.

**What we give up**

A named check called `remote-agents catalog` disappears from the PR checks list; the same command now reports under `docs build`. Per the repo's own note that main has no branch protection, no required-check name breaks — but confirm before merging, since ci.yml:290-291 says the `test` job's names are pinned by branch protection. Coverage is unchanged: exactly the same command runs on exactly the same set of PRs, and failure still fails validate-status.

**Verifier corrections to the evidence above**

1. **Wrong consumer count (repo-wide grep is false).** The evidence asserts "`git grep -n sync:remote-agents` shows exactly two invocations, `:223` in `checks` and `:251` in `remote-agent-docs`". Re-run at HEAD (d8c6dfc071) there is a third production invocation: `.github/workflows/remote-agents-sync.yml:71` (`npm run sync:remote-agents -- --apply`), plus `.github/workflows/remote-agents-sync.yml:68` (`node scripts/sync-remote-agents.mjs`) and doc callers `docs/supabase/SUPABASE_SETUP.md:581,587` and `docs/supabase/MIGRATION_UNIFY_MULTIPLE.md:36,84,87`. The verdict is unaffected — `remote-agents-sync.yml` is the merge-to-main apply workflow and the proposal does not touch it — but the "exactly two" claim as written is wrong and the PR body must not repeat it.

2. **Commit chronology narrated backwards.** `git log -S` prints newest-first. The real order is `bb93f63495` (2026-08-14 00:55, "fix(ci): preserve docs build with remote agent gate") — which created the `remote-agent-docs` job and the `remote_agent_docs` output — then `e80f6383ce` (2026-08-14 01:04, "ci: require docs-only remote agent validation") — which added the validate-status special case now at `:601-606`. The evidence writes "`e80f6383ce` … then `bb93f63495`", reversing it.

3. **Comment line range off by one, and the fold must edit it.** The classifier comment is `.github/workflows/ci.yml:81-83`, not `:82-83`. Line 83 reads "their separate drift job must not suppress the public docs build" — after the fold there is no separate drift job, so the proposal must reword that comment. The evidence's step list omits this edit.

4. **The "second runner for the same docs-only PRs" framing overstates the spend win.** `docs/supabase/remote-agents.config.json` has 9 commits in the entire repo history; the only docs-only one after the job existed is `01b701980f` (2026-08-14, #10244). The job has fired essentially once in ~11 days. The real win is YAML and one job element, not runner minutes; the "Minimize CI spend" memory note is weak support here and should not be the PR's headline.

5. **The job's third gate clause is already dead.** `github.event_name != 'schedule'` at `:228` is redundant: `:50-54` forces `code=true` for every non-`pull_request` event, so `code != 'true'` already implies `pull_request`. The evidence treats the gate as three meaningful clauses when only two carry information — which is also why the folded step's `if:` needs only `needs.changes.outputs.remote_agent_docs == 'true'`.

6. **Line arithmetic works only with the trailing blank counted.** `:225-251` is 27 lines; `:252` is blank and must go too, so the deletion is `:225-252` = 28 lines. With +4 added (blank separator + 3-line step), -1 (`:544`), -1 (`:578`), -6 (`:601-606`), net = -32. The claimed -32 is right; the cited range that produces it is not.

7. **The branch-protection caveat is already resolved, not open.** `gh api repos/texra-ai/coauthor/branches/main/protection` → 404 Not Found. No branch protection exists on `main`, so the `ci.yml:290-291` comment ("these strings are the check names branch protection pins") is stale and no required check name can break. The candidate's "confirm before merging" is discharged.

8. **Implementation gap the evidence does not flag.** Placing the step after `:524-525` puts it ahead of `Check formatting` (`:527-528`) and the docs build (`:534-536`), so a catalog failure now short-circuits both — a docs-only PR with a formatting error _and_ a catalog error needs two round trips where today it gets both at once from two parallel jobs. Put the step last in `docs-check`, or give it `if: ${{ !cancelled() && needs.changes.outputs.remote_agent_docs == 'true' }}`, to preserve current feedback independence.

<details><summary>Verifier reasoning</summary>

**Every line citation re-read at HEAD d8c6dfc071 (not the evidence's snapshot) and all are exact.** `.github/workflows/ci.yml` is 607 lines. `remote-agent-docs` occupies `:225-251` with `name: remote-agents catalog` (`:226`), gate at `:228`, and the single real step `Check remote-agents catalog generates` / `npm run sync:remote-agents` at `:250-251`. `docs-check` occupies `:499-536`, gate at `:505`. The setup blocks are byte-for-byte the same pattern: checkout `actions/checkout@v6` (`:232-233` / `:509-510`), `pnpm/action-setup@v6` `run_install: false` (`:234-237` / `:511-514`), `actions/setup-node@v5` node 22 + pnpm cache on pnpm-lock.yaml (`:239-245` / `:516-522`), `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile --ignore-scripts` (`:248` / `:525`). Confirmed identical, including the `--ignore-scripts` flag, so the folded step gets the same node_modules.

**Gate-superset argument holds.** `remote-agent-docs` needs `code != 'true' && remote_agent_docs == 'true' && event != schedule`; `docs-check` needs `changes.result == 'success' && code != 'true'`. Both `needs: changes` without `always()`, so a failed/skipped `changes` skips both. Every PR that runs `remote-agent-docs` therefore runs `docs-check`. Verified `:50-54` forces `code=true` and `remote_agent_docs=false` for every non-pull_request event, so the schedule clause is already dead weight.

**Coverage after the fold is exactly preserved — this is not a masking-site deletion (checklist §15 M1-M6 do not apply).** `checks` is gated `code == 'true' && event != 'schedule'` (`:143`), so on a pull_request `checks` is skipped iff `code != 'true'`. The surviving docs-only gate at `:595-600` fires on `pull_request && checks == skipped && docs-check != success` — i.e. on exactly the set of PRs the special case at `:601-606` covers, and a failing step inside `docs-check` fails `docs-check`. So `:601-606` is genuinely subsumed, not a guard being quietly dropped. `:578` (loop row) and `:544` (`needs` entry) must go with it or the workflow references a nonexistent need.

**Dedupe.** Not in `round1-index.md` (95 lines; the four `grep -i workflow` hits are all workflow-_script_ runtime items — `@agent/workflowScript` barrel, `'starting'` call status, `WorkflowScriptTool` handoff, `settledWorkflowCall` — nothing CI-job-shaped). `grep -i "remote-agent|ci\.yml|docs-check"` on `docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md` → one hit at `:2673`, a refuted `contributes.menus` candidate that merely cites `ci.yml:214`; unrelated.

**Already filed / already done.** `git log --oneline -8 -- .github/workflows/ci.yml` → 2f032b621e, c60c11c7e7, e7473daae9, 4a17794996, 7892adab7d, f52e5955f6, 232604572a, e80f6383ce. Nothing revisited the job split since it was created. `gh issue list --label area:ci --state all` (40 rows) and searches for "remote-agent", "remote-agent-docs docs-check fold job" return nothing on this. Nearest neighbours are #10169 (narrow the docs/supabase classifier — the classifier, not the job) and #10070 (add the gate in the first place, closed). The job is present at HEAD, so not already done by #11416-#11422.

**Deliberately justified?** No. Searched `docs/proposals/`, `docs/architecture/`, `AGENTS.md`, and the review checklist. The only rulings touching this machinery (`2026-08-01-open-source-readiness-audit.md:342,642`, `2026-08-01-backend-decoupling-plan.md:748`) are about wiring `check:remote-agents` at all and about keeping the config file beside its script — both satisfied by a step inside `docs-check`. `git show bb93f63495` confirms the separation was a same-hour hotfix for a _classification_ bug (`docs/supabase/**` was counted as "code", suppressing the docs build), not a considered design: the fix narrowed the classifier and then bolted on a job. Running the command inside `docs-check` satisfies the surviving comment's stated intent exactly.

**Settled surface?** No — CI YAML, not the frozen `@agent/*` SDK, the trimmed PocketFlow engine, host/platform ports, the six browser-safe utils, AgentEvent/SessionFact, the five ratchets, or the vscode-free zones. The `changes` job's `remote_agent_docs` output (`:36`, `:53`, `:127-131`) and `drift_gate_match` + its three self-tests (`:71-80`, `:98-109`) all stay and still drive the new step's `if:` — verified by grep.

**Net-adds?** No. Deletion is `:225-252` (28), `:544` (1), `:578` (1), `:601-606` (6) = 36 removed; addition is a 4-line step block plus a one-line comment reword. Net -32 LoC, -1 job. No unrelated churn, no public API involved, single file.

**Dependency check on the folded step:** `scripts/sync-remote-agents.mjs` is 502 lines (evidence correct) and its only non-builtin import is `yaml` plus local `./walkFiles.mjs`, both satisfied by `docs-check`'s root `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile --ignore-scripts` at `:525`, which runs before any of the proposed insertion points.

**Thinness (rule 8).** It is a single-file, ~36-line YAML change and would be thin alone, but it sits squarely in this lens ("overlapping check scripts, ratchets and CI config") and should ship batched with the other CI-config items rather than as a solo PR. That is a packaging condition, not a refutation.

Verification per the proposal's step 6 (a scratch PR touching only `docs/supabase/remote-agents.config.json`) is sound and cheap; note it is the _only_ way to exercise this path, since the job fires roughly once per year in practice.

</details>

#### Sweep four residues in the unlinted, unknipped scripts/ corpus (dead re-export block, unused import, pre-workspace-split fallback, phantom contributes key)

- **Lens**: `scripts-and-ratchets` · **Kind**: dead-export · **Risk**: low
- **Net**: -27 LoC, -10 elements (claimed -25 LoC, -9 elements; verifier figure governs)

**Evidence**

Why nothing in CI can find these: `npm run lint` (package.json:10) covers `src packages/agent/src packages/agent/scripts packages/extension/src packages/desktop/src packages/cli/scripts` — root `scripts/` (~9.5k lines of .mjs) is not in the list. And `knip.json:7` lists `"scripts/**/*.mjs!"` as a root-workspace _entry_ pattern only (root `project` is `src/**/*.ts!`), and `includeEntryExports` is off — the same mechanics the round-1 refutation established at `docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md:2666`. I confirmed empirically: re-running knip with `scripts/**/*.mjs!` added to root `project` still reports nothing under scripts/.

(a) `scripts/check-dead-code-ratchet.mjs:29-38` re-exports eight symbols (`classifyFindings, compareFindings, countByCategory, diffFindings, extractFindings, findingKey, parseKnipIssues, readBaseline`) from `./check-dead-code-ratchet-core.mjs`, then imports six of the same names again at `:39-46`. Consumers, greped repo-wide (`git grep -n check-dead-code-ratchet`): production = `package.json:52` runs `node scripts/check-dead-code-ratchet.mjs` as a CLI; non-production = `src/test-kernel/scripts/CheckDeadCodeRatchet.vitest.ts:5`, which imports `../../../scripts/check-dead-code-ratchet-core.mjs` — the **core** module, not the wrapper; the rest are docs prose (`docs/proposals/2026-08-19-dead-code-gate-blind-spots.md:5,143`, `2026-08-01-directory-organization.md:284,528`, etc.). Zero importers of the wrapper module ⇒ the whole re-export block is a shim with no consumer, exactly the "deleted wrappers leave no re-export shims" rule in CLAUDE.md "Design guardrails". `compareFindings` and `findingKey` are re-exported but not even used inside the wrapper.

(b) `scripts/verify-vsix-contents.mjs:9` imports `CATALOG_DERIVED_CONTRIBUTES` and never references it. `git grep -n CATALOG_DERIVED_CONTRIBUTES` → 3 hits total: the declaration (`scripts/extension-package-utils.mjs:87`), its one real use (`:101`, inside `withoutCatalogDerivedContributes`), and this dead import.

(c) `scripts/verify-extension-package-invariants.mjs:90-100` `findExtensionPackagePath()` falls back to the repo-root `package.json` when `packages/extension/package.json` is absent. `git log --oneline -3 -S findExtensionPackagePath` → introduced by `495dde0e89` (2026-05-03, "ci: verify extension package invariants"), one day before `4d1306e081` (2026-05-04, "feat: move extension sources into workspace package") created `packages/extension/package.json` (`git log --diff-filter=A -- packages/extension/package.json`). It is migration scaffolding that has been unreachable for ~4 months. Its sibling `scripts/verify-vsix-contents.mjs:31` reads `packages/extension/package.json` unconditionally, so the two scripts already disagree about whether the fallback is real.

(d) `scripts/extension-package-utils.mjs:87-91` lists `'configuration'` in `CATALOG_DERIVED_CONTRIBUTES`, but `scripts/sync-package-contributes.mjs:54-58` _throws_ if `packageJson.contributes.configuration` exists, and `.github/workflows/ci.yml:216-217` runs that script in `--check` mode on every code PR — so the key can never be present and the delete at `:101` is a no-op. The two comment blocks that claim otherwise (`sync-package-contributes.mjs:12-16` "Code-generate … `contributes.configuration`", `extension-package-utils.mjs:79-86`) are stale since `958460a0d5` (2026-08-02, "refactor: retire remaining settings compatibility"); CLAUDE.md states `packages/extension/package.json` must NOT contribute `configuration`. Sibling constant `REQUIRED_CATALOG_CONTRIBUTES` (`:95`) already reflects reality (`['commands','keybindings']`).

Dedupe checks run: none of the four appear in the round-1 index; `grep -n "check-dead-code-ratchet-core|CATALOG_DERIVED|findExtensionPackagePath" docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md` → no hits. The refuted round-1 batch "one dead export, one single-caller module, one duplicated zone list" was `aliasUtils.mjs:64 BUILD_EXCLUDED_ALIASES` (0 LoC), `desktop-package-metafile-paths.mjs`, and the eslint/vitest zone mirror — all different, and I deliberately do NOT re-file the aliasUtils item. `git log --oneline -15` on all five paths shows no recent touch to these lines. `gh issue list --label tech-debt --state all --search "scripts"` surfaces only the closed #8896 (npm-script matrix prune) and #11255 (script walker consolidation), both precedent for shipping this kind of change, neither covering these.

**Proposal**

One PR, five files:

1. Delete `scripts/check-dead-code-ratchet.mjs:29-38` (the `export { … } from './check-dead-code-ratchet-core.mjs'` block), keeping the `import { … }` at `:39-46`. Optionally also collapse `:152-154` to a bare `main()` call, since with the re-export gone the module has no importer and the `process.argv[1] === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)` guard is the second redundant "safe to import" mechanism.
2. Delete the `CATALOG_DERIVED_CONTRIBUTES,` line at `scripts/verify-vsix-contents.mjs:9`.
3. Delete `findExtensionPackagePath()` (`scripts/verify-extension-package-invariants.mjs:90-100`) and inline `const packagePath = path.join(rootDir, 'packages', 'extension', 'package.json');` at `:24`.
4. Drop `'configuration'` from `CATALOG_DERIVED_CONTRIBUTES` (`scripts/extension-package-utils.mjs:87-91`), and correct the two stale comment blocks (`extension-package-utils.mjs:79-86`, `sync-package-contributes.mjs:12-16`) to say commands + keybindings are generated and `configuration` is forbidden outright.
5. Verify with `npm run check:dead-code-ratchet`, `npm run check:extension-package-invariants`, `npm run check:package-contributes`, and `npx vitest run src/test-kernel/scripts/CheckDeadCodeRatchet.vitest.ts`.

**What we give up**

(a) nothing — the only consumer already imports the core module directly. (b)(c) nothing. (d) gives up a defensive delete-if-present for a key whose presence already hard-fails `sync-package-contributes.mjs` on every code PR; restoring it is one array element. Whole batch is one revert.

**Verifier corrections to the evidence above**

1. **`npm run lint` scope misquoted.** Evidence lists `src packages/agent/src packages/agent/scripts packages/extension/src packages/desktop/src packages/cli/scripts`. Actual `package.json:10` is `src packages/agent/src packages/agent/scripts packages/extension/src packages/desktop/src packages/cli/src "packages/cli/scripts/**/*.{ts,tsx}"` — it omitted `packages/cli/src`. The load-bearing conclusion (root `scripts/` is not in the list) holds.

2. **"~9.5k lines of .mjs" in root `scripts/` is wrong.** `find scripts -name '*.mjs' | xargs wc -l` → **7054**. Closed issue #8895 independently records "scripts/ + docs/scripts 41 files/7.6k LoC".

3. **The vitest consumer is a dynamic import, not a static one.** `src/test-kernel/scripts/CheckDeadCodeRatchet.vitest.ts:3-15` is `const ratchet = await import(/* @ts-expect-error */ '../../../scripts/check-dead-code-ratchet-core.mjs')` destructured at `:7-15`. Line 5 is the specifier string only. The conclusion (it imports the **core**, never the wrapper) is correct.

4. **Net LoC is understated: measured -27, not -25.** Once `'configuration'` leaves, prettier collapses `CATALOG_DERIVED_CONTRIBUTES` from a 5-line array literal to one line (`export const CATALOG_DERIVED_CONTRIBUTES = ['commands', 'keybindings'];`), an extra -4 the estimate never counted. Measured `git diff --stat` over the four scripts after formatting: **2 insertions, 29 deletions**. Consequence the proposal misses: step 4 **must** be followed by `npm run format`, or the `Check formatting` CI step (ci.yml, same validate job) fails. With the optional `main()`-guard collapse it is -29.

5. **Omission the proposal should handle: step 4 creates two byte-identical exported constants.** After the drop, `CATALOG_DERIVED_CONTRIBUTES` and `REQUIRED_CATALOG_CONTRIBUTES` (`scripts/extension-package-utils.mjs:87` and `:95`) are both `['commands', 'keybindings']`. Either collapse them (another ~-4 LoC, -1 element) or make the two comment blocks state why the names stay distinct (derived-and-stripped vs required-present); shipping two identically-valued exported constants side by side is exactly the dual-system smell a reviewer will flag back.

6. **CI citation incomplete.** `check:package-contributes` is `run:` at `.github/workflows/ci.yml:217` (`- name:` at `:216`) — correct — but the evidence omits the second gate, `.github/workflows/release.yml:67`. Both run the same `--check`, and the `configuration` throw at `sync-package-contributes.mjs:54-58` fires unconditionally before the `--check` branch, so the argument holds on both.

7. **A doc reference to `findExtensionPackagePath` the evidence missed.** `docs/proposals/2026-07-09-state-of-the-architecture.md:567` names `REQUIRED_PACKAGED_PATHS`/`findExtensionPackagePath` as the machine enforcement for MONO-3. I read it: it cites the script as the mechanism, it does **not** rule the root-`package.json` fallback in. No criterion-4 conflict, but the git-grep hit list in the evidence should have shown it (3 hits, not 2).

8. **Issue search was too narrow.** `gh issue list --label tech-debt --search "scripts"` surfaced #8896/#11255 for the candidate; my searches also surface **#8895** (closed R7 tracking issue, area literally "build tooling / scripts / config / test-kernel") and **#8902** (`sync-package-contributes` tsc-out). I read all 8 of #8895's verified findings — npm-script prune, dead-automation purge, trace-viewer hand-mirror, fixture consolidation, test-LoC detector, ratchet-baseline tightening + knip zero-ref sweep, tsc-out shadow compiler, `.vitest.mts` rename — none covers any of these four. Not already filed, confirmed on a wider net than the evidence used.

9. **(c) is an M-class masking site; the PR must say so.** The fallback silently substitutes a _different_ file when the expected one is absent. Deleting it is correct here only because the inlined `path.join(...)` + `readJson` throws ENOENT loudly — i.e. this is "fix the masking loudly", not "delete a guard". State that in the PR body or it reads as a §15 violation.

10. **Provenance of (a) is stronger than stated.** `scripts/check-dead-code-ratchet-core.mjs` was born in `3f06c08646` (#9903, 2026-08-09, "ci: restore Windows kernel test matrix" — sub-commit "fix(windows): load script fixtures portably"), i.e. the split existed to let the Windows kernel test import pure functions without the CLI wrapper's `spawnSync`. The re-export block is the compat shim that split left behind and no consumer ever took. That is textbook CLAUDE.md "deleted wrappers leave no re-export shims".

11. **Element count: -10, not -9.** 8 re-exported bindings + `findExtensionPackagePath` + the dead `CATALOG_DERIVED_CONTRIBUTES` import binding. (The `'configuration'` array member is a value, not an element; counting it gives -11.)

<details><summary>Verifier reasoning</summary>

DEDUPE / ALREADY-DONE. HEAD is `d8c6dfc071` (0.40.6); all seven round-1 PRs (#11416-#11422) are merged and in HEAD. `git log --oneline -10` on all five candidate paths shows the newest touch is `70fda69a7e` (2026-08-21, PocketFlow NOTICE), i.e. **no round-1 PR touched any of these files** — verified. The round-1 index contains two nearby-but-distinct items: "Batch three small script/config cleanups" (refuted; its sub-items are `aliasUtils.mjs:64 BUILD_EXCLUDED_ALIASES`, `desktop-package-metafile-paths.mjs`, and the eslint/vitest zone mirror — I read the full refutation at survey line ~2680 and confirmed zero overlap) and "Derive contributes.menus.commandPalette from the command catalog" (refuted at survey line 2669 for netting ~+50 LoC; it would _add_ to `sync-package-contributes.mjs`, orthogonal to removing `'configuration'`). `grep -n "check-dead-code-ratchet-core|CATALOG_DERIVED|findExtensionPackagePath|verify-vsix|extension-package-utils"` over the 49-candidate survey returns one hit, line 2687, which mentions `extension-package-utils.mjs` only in passing while refuting the metafile-paths item. Not a duplicate.

MECHANICS PREMISE (why nothing catches these). Verified both halves myself. `package.json:10` lint list excludes root `scripts/`. `knip.json:7`: root workspace `entry` = `["src/test-kernel/**/*.vitest.ts", "scripts/**/*.mjs!"]`, `project` = `["src/**/*.ts!", …]` — `scripts/**` are entries but not project files, and `includeEntryExports` is off, so entry-file exports are never reported. `config/ratchets/knip-baseline.json` contains exactly one occurrence of the string `scripts/`, inside the `semantics` prose field — zero `scripts/` findings. This also means removing entry-file exports cannot change knip output, so I did not need the full two-pass `check:dead-code-ratchet` run to clear (a).

(a) CONFIRMED. `scripts/check-dead-code-ratchet.mjs:29-38` is the `export { classifyFindings, compareFindings, countByCategory, diffFindings, extractFindings, findingKey, parseKnipIssues, readBaseline } from './check-dead-code-ratchet-core.mjs';` block; `:39-46` re-imports six of the same names. Repo-wide `git grep -n "check-dead-code-ratchet"` (tracked files, all extensions — includes `.github/`, `.claude/`, `package.json`, `supabase/`): the wrapper module has **zero importers**. Its only consumer is `package.json:52` as a CLI (`node scripts/check-dead-code-ratchet.mjs`). The test imports the core module. `compareFindings` and `findingKey` are re-exported but never referenced inside the wrapper (`git grep` shows their only real uses are internal to the core module and in the vitest, which pulls them from core). File is 154 lines; the `process.argv[1] === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)` guard is at `:152-154`.

(b) CONFIRMED. `git grep -n CATALOG_DERIVED_CONTRIBUTES` → exactly 3 hits: declaration `extension-package-utils.mjs:87`, sole use `:101`, and the unused import at `verify-vsix-contents.mjs:9`. `verify-vsix-contents.mjs` never references the identifier again (its real uses are `REQUIRED_CATALOG_CONTRIBUTES` at `:68` and `withoutCatalogDerivedContributes` at `:92`).

(c) CONFIRMED, including the history. `git log -1 495dde0e89` → 2026-05-03 "ci: verify extension package invariants"; `git log --diff-filter=A -- packages/extension/package.json` → `4d1306e081` 2026-05-04 "feat: move extension sources into workspace package". So the fallback branch has been unreachable since the day after it was written (~3.7 months, not "~4"; close enough). Only two code references: the call at `:24`, the definition at `:90-100`. Sibling `verify-vsix-contents.mjs:31` does read `packages/extension/package.json` unconditionally — the inconsistency claim holds. `fs` stays imported (8 other `fs.` uses in that file), so the import line survives the delete.

(d) CONFIRMED. `sync-package-contributes.mjs:54-58` throws unconditionally (before the `--check` branch at `:51`) if `packageJson.contributes?.configuration !== undefined`; gated in CI at `ci.yml:217` and `release.yml:67`. `grep -n '"configuration"' packages/extension/package.json` → zero hits, and `scripts/extension-package-invariants.snapshot.json` has no `configuration` key either, so `delete trimmedContributes['configuration']` at `:101` is provably a no-op on both sides of both comparisons (committed manifest and unpacked VSIX manifest — vsce copies the manifest, it does not synthesize `contributes.configuration`). The stale-comment claim holds: `extension-package-utils.mjs:79-86` and `sync-package-contributes.mjs:12-16` both still name `contributes.configuration` as generated output. CLAUDE.md's "must NOT contribute `configuration`" is the ruling that makes the array entry dead, not one that protects it.

EMPIRICAL RUN. I applied all four edits to the working tree, ran `npx prettier --write` on the one file that needed it, and executed: `node --check` on all four files (clean); `node -e "import('./scripts/check-dead-code-ratchet.mjs')"` → `imported, exports: []` (confirms the surface removed was empty of consumers and the module still loads without running `main`); `npm run check:extension-package-invariants` → "Extension package invariants are in sync"; `npm run check:package-contributes` → "package.json contributes.* is in sync with the catalogs"; `npx vitest run src/test-kernel/scripts/CheckDeadCodeRatchet.vitest.ts` → 17/17 passed. `git diff --stat` over the four scripts: 2 insertions, 29 deletions. I then reverted only those four paths (`git checkout --`), leaving the four unrelated test-file modifications another session has in the tree untouched.

CRITERIA SWEEP. (1) not a duplicate. (2) not filed and not done — checked #8896, #11255, #8895 (all 8 findings read), #8902; `git log --all --grep` and per-path logs clean. (3) consumer counts re-greped by me, repo-wide, all extensions, including `package.json`, `.github/workflows/`, `packages/extension/package.json`, `src/shared/schemas/*Settings.ts`, `packages/extension/src/commands.ts`, `resources/`, `prompts/`, `supabase/functions/` — one missed doc reference (correction 7), no missed production consumer. (4) no ruling protects any of the four; the two docs that mention them (`2026-08-19-dead-code-gate-blind-spots.md`, `2026-07-09-state-of-the-architecture.md:567`) cite the scripts as mechanisms, not these lines as deliberate. (5) touches no settled surface — no `@agent/*`, no PocketFlow engine, no platform port, no browser-safe utils, no AgentEvent/SessionFact, and it does not weaken any of the five ratchets (the `configuration` prohibition is enforced by a hard throw, not by this array). (6) net-negative and measured, not estimated. (7) the one masking-shaped deletion, (c), is fixed loudly — `readJson` throws ENOENT once the fallback is gone. (8) not too thin: four residues, ~-27 LoC, one file each, one revert.

Verdict: survives, with the corrections above binding on the PR (run `npm run format`; resolve the newly-identical constants; say out loud that (c) is a masking-site fix).

</details>

#### Replace the hand-rolled recursion and stat/ENOENT probes left in the package and release-gating build scripts with readdir({recursive:true}) and statSync({throwIfNoEntry:false})

- **Lens**: `hand-rolled-vs-builtin` · **Kind**: hand-rolled · **Risk**: medium
- **Net**: -15 LoC, 0 elements (claimed -21 LoC, 0 elements; verifier figure governs)

**Evidence**

Issue #11255 / commit ad965a97f5 ("refactor: consolidate six hand-rolled script walkers onto one primitive") consolidated the walkers under repo-root `scripts/` onto scripts/walkFiles.mjs, and #11375 (63ceb641af) did a second pass. Both were scoped to `scripts/`; the same class of code under `packages/*/scripts` and in the async stat probes was never touched, and only a cross-package view shows it.
(A) packages/agent/scripts/fsWalk.mjs:13-23 `walkFiles` hand-rolls depth-first recursion over `readdir(dir, { withFileTypes: true })` + `Promise.all` + `.flat()`. `readdir(dir, { recursive: true, withFileTypes: true })` covers it exactly (`entry.parentPath` + `entry.isFile()`); packages/agent/package.json:17 pins `"node": ">=22.9.0"`, well past where `recursive`/`parentPath` are stable. Production consumers (build tooling, both wired to package.json entry points): packages/agent/scripts/validate-artifacts.mjs:5,17 (`check:artifacts`, packages/agent/package.json:44) and packages/agent/scripts/rewrite-declaration-aliases.mjs:5,87 (`build:types`, :43). Non-production consumers: zero. Note this file is itself a same-name duplicate of scripts/walkFiles.mjs — it was created 2026-08-19 by d7582075cb, four days after ad965a97f5 established the repo-root primitive, by an audit that did not look outside the package.
(B) Four `try { await stat(p) } catch (error) { if (error?.code === 'ENOENT') return X; throw error; }` blocks, each collapsible to one `statSync(p, { throwIfNoEntry: false })` line — the exact form already in use at scripts/format-staged.mjs:310 (`lstatSync(path, { throwIfNoEntry: false })`, landed by #10657):

- scripts/verify-desktop-build-artifacts.mjs:26-33 `fileExists` (8 lines → 1)
- scripts/verify-desktop-package.mjs:252-257 asar-reader `isDirectory`
- scripts/verify-desktop-package.mjs:303-308 directory-reader `isDirectory`
- scripts/verify-desktop-package.mjs:546-551 `onDiskSize`
  Both scripts are production release gates with package.json entry points (package.json:48 `check:desktop-build-artifacts`, :50 `check:desktop-package`) and are covered by src/test-kernel/scripts/DesktopPackagePayload.vitest.ts:152, which spawns the verifier. `throwIfNoEntry: false` suppresses only ENOENT, so the existing "re-throw anything else" behaviour is preserved verbatim.
  Ambiguous corpus resolved: `scripts/` and `packages/agent/scripts/` here are release/CI tooling wired to package.json scripts, so I classified them production and sized risk accordingly.
  Dedupe: `grep -n 'walkFiles\|fsWalk\|throwIfNoEntry'` in docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md returns only line 2687, which discusses scripts/walkFiles.mjs as an existing helper while rejecting an unrelated inlining idea — it does not cover fsWalk.mjs or the stat probes. Neither appears in the round-1 title index. `gh issue list --label tech-debt --state all --search "fsWalk"` = 0 rows; `--search "directory walk"` returns the CLOSED #11255 (scoped to scripts/, shipped) and #7878 (src/ tool files, different subject); `--search "ENOENT"` returns nothing on this topic. `git log --all --grep=fsWalk` shows only its creation commit; `git log --all --grep=throwIfNoEntry` shows only #10657.

**Proposal**

1. packages/agent/scripts/fsWalk.mjs: replace the body of `walkFiles(directory, predicate)` with a single `readdir(directory, { recursive: true, withFileTypes: true })` call, filtering `entry.isFile() && (!predicate || predicate(entry.name))` and mapping to `path.join(entry.parentPath, entry.name)`. Signature, async-ness, and return shape stay identical, so validate-artifacts.mjs:17 and rewrite-declaration-aliases.mjs:87 are untouched. Keep `isFile` as is — `throwIfNoEntry: false` does not cover its deliberate ENOTDIR arm (fsWalk.mjs:8), and that arm is load-bearing for the `path.join(candidate, 'index.ts')` probes at rewrite-declaration-aliases.mjs:39-42.
2. scripts/verify-desktop-build-artifacts.mjs: replace `fileExists` (:26-33) with `statSync(filePath, { throwIfNoEntry: false })?.isFile() ?? false`.
3. scripts/verify-desktop-package.mjs: replace the three try/catch blocks at :252-257, :303-308 and :546-551 with `statSync(p, { throwIfNoEntry: false })`, switching those three call sites off `fs/promises` `stat`; the enclosing methods stay `async` so no caller changes. Leave the two `readdir` ENOENT catches at :288 and :323 alone — `readdir` has no `throwIfNoEntry` equivalent, so those are not hand-rolled.
4. Re-run the gates the changes cover: `npm run check:desktop-build-artifacts`, `npm run check:desktop-package`, `corepack pnpm --filter @texra-ai/agent build`, plus `npx vitest run src/test-kernel/scripts/DesktopPackagePayload.vitest.ts`. No new tests.

**What we give up**

Nothing behavioural: `throwIfNoEntry: false` suppresses exactly ENOENT and re-throws everything else, matching the current catch arms, and recursive `readdir` yields the same file set as the hand-rolled descent (order within a directory is unchanged; cross-directory ordering may differ, which neither consumer depends on — validate-artifacts.mjs joins all declaration text and rewrite-declaration-aliases.mjs rewrites each file independently). The cost is that three probes in verify-desktop-package.mjs become synchronous inside async methods; that is a build-time script where sync fs is already the norm elsewhere in scripts/. Restoring any of this is a one-line revert per site.

**Verifier corrections to the evidence above**

1. **Claimed net LoC (-21) is inflated by ~40%. Measured, not estimated.** I wrote the proposed replacement and ran the repo's own prettier over it. Part (A) is **exactly LoC-neutral**: `packages/agent/scripts/fsWalk.mjs` is 23 lines today and 23 lines after the rewrite. The `{ recursive: true, withFileTypes: true }` options object (3 lines after prettier wraps it) plus prettier's forced wrap of the filter callback consume the entire saving from deleting the recursion. Part (A) buys conceptual simplicity (no recursion, no `Promise.all`, no `.flat()`), not lines. Part (B) is roughly -16 gross (fileExists 8→3 = -5; verify-desktop-package.mjs:252-257 6→4 = -2; :303-308 6→2 = -4; onDiskSize 545-551 7→2 = -5), plus **+1 for a new `import { statSync } from 'node:fs';`** in verify-desktop-package.mjs, which today imports only from `node:fs/promises` (line 1: `import { access, readdir, readFile, stat } from 'node:fs/promises';`) — the candidate never accounts for that added import. Net ≈ **-15**.

2. **The chronology is inverted, and it inverts the candidate's whole narrative.** `packages/agent/scripts/fsWalk.mjs` was created by `d7582075cb` on **Wed Aug 19 2026**. `scripts/walkFiles.mjs` was created by `ad965a97f5` (#11255) on **Fri Aug 21 2026** — two days LATER. The evidence's claim that fsWalk.mjs was "created 2026-08-19 by d7582075cb, four days after ad965a97f5 established the repo-root primitive, by an audit that did not look outside the package" is backwards on both the ordering and the interval: the repo-root primitive did not exist when fsWalk.mjs was written. There was nothing outside the package to look at.

3. **"Same-name duplicate of scripts/walkFiles.mjs" overstates it.** The exported symbol name collides, but the APIs are not interchangeable: `scripts/walkFiles.mjs` is **synchronous** (`fs.readdirSync`), returns `{ absolutePath, relativePath }` objects, and takes `{ include, prune, limit }` over a '/'-joined walk-relative path; `fsWalk.mjs`'s is **async**, returns `string[]`, and takes a predicate over the bare basename. Deduping them is not a drop-in and is not what the proposal does — fine, but the "duplicate" framing should not be used to justify the change.

4. **"Four `try { await stat(p) } catch` blocks" — only three are async.** `scripts/verify-desktop-build-artifacts.mjs:26-33` already uses **synchronous** `fs.statSync(filePath)` (line 28) inside a sync `fileExists`. It needs no import change and no async→sync conversion; only the try/catch collapses.

5. **Coverage attribution is wrong on two counts.** (a) `src/test-kernel/scripts/DesktopPackagePayload.vitest.ts` spawns the verifier from `verifierPath` declared at **:20** via `spawnSync` at **:289**, called from :76, :90, :103, :132, :162. Line **:152 is an unrelated `it(...)` title**, not a spawn. (b) **`scripts/verify-desktop-build-artifacts.mjs` has zero test coverage** — repo-wide grep gives exactly one consumer, `package.json:48`. The evidence's "Both scripts ... are covered by" is false for the one whose rewrite has no safety net.

6. **"Never touched" is imprecise.** `ad965a97f5` DID edit `scripts/verify-desktop-build-artifacts.mjs` (it is in that commit's stat list, -14/+... lines) — it replaced the walker and left the stat probe. `63ceb641af` (#11375) likewise edited `scripts/verify-desktop-package.mjs`. The surviving stat probes are real; the claim that these files were untouched by both passes is not.

7. **A fifth collapsible site was missed.** `scripts/verify-desktop-installer-artifacts.mjs:152-167` — `const iconStat = await stat(requirement.iconPath)` with an ENOENT arm pushing a "Missing installer icon" failure — collapses the same way for roughly another **-7 lines**, and that script is also a release gate (`package.json:57 check:desktop-installers`). Its other three ENOENT catches (:86 `readdir`, :122 `readFile`, :176 `readFile`) correctly have no `throwIfNoEntry` equivalent, matching the candidate's own reasoning for verify-desktop-package.mjs:288/:323. Including it makes the batch ≈ **-22** and is what lifts this above the too-thin line.

8. **Minor line-range error.** verify-desktop-package.mjs:252-257, :303-308 and :546-551 are all correct, but the onDiskSize saving actually spans **545-551** — the `let entryStat;` declaration at 545 disappears too.

9. **`entry.isFile()` is not behavior-identical.** The current walker returns every **non-directory** entry (symlinks, FIFOs, sockets included) that passes the predicate. `entry.isFile()` would drop symlinks-to-files. Use `!entry.isDirectory()` to preserve semantics exactly; the "return shape stays identical" claim requires it. Practically moot under `dist/`, but the proposal as written is a silent behavior change.

10. **Precedent claim verified and tight.** `scripts/format-staged.mjs:310` is the repo's only `throwIfNoEntry` use and is at exactly the cited line. Related closed issue is #10602 (format-staged symlink classification), not on this topic.

11. **Engine claim verified but not the binding constraint.** `packages/agent/package.json:17` does pin `">=22.9.0"`. The binding floor is CI's `node-version: '22'` across every `.github/workflows/*.yml` — still safe (`Dirent.parentPath` since 20.12/21.4, recursive+withFileTypes since 20.1).

Nothing from #11416-#11422. I read all four sites at HEAD and every hand-rolled block described is still present verbatim.

**Already filed (kill test 2) — passed.** `gh issue list --label tech-debt --state all --search "fsWalk"` = 0 rows. `--search "walkFiles"` = 0 rows. `gh issue list --state all --search "throwIfNoEntry"` returns only #10602 (CLOSED, format-staged symlink classification — different subject). #11255 is closed and shipped, scoped to `scripts/` walkers only.
