Call sites confirmed at validate-artifacts.mjs:17 (`await walkFiles(distRoot)`) and rewrite-declaration-aliases.mjs:87 (`await walkFiles(outputRoot, (name) => name.endsWith('.d.ts'))`) — both cited line numbers correct. Both wired to package.json entry points (`check:artifacts` at :44, `build:types` at :43). Repo-wide grep for `verify-desktop-build-artifacts|verify-desktop-package` across ts/yml/json/mjs: package.json:48, package.json:50, a comment reference in packages/desktop/electron-builder.yml:27, and DesktopPackagePayload.vitest.ts:20. No CI workflow invokes them directly by path; no extension contribution, command id, settings schema, resources/, prompts/, or supabase function touches any of this. Counts hold except the coverage misattribution in correction 5.

**Deliberately justified (kill test 4) — passed.** Read both consolidation commit bodies. `ad965a97f5` explicitly scopes itself to "Six build/CI scripts under scripts/" and says nothing about packages/*/scripts. `d7582075cb` explains fsWalk.mjs as an extraction of two duplicated helpers within the package — no ruling that async recursion was preferred over the builtin. No `no-sync`/`readdirSync`/`statSync` rule anywhere in eslint.config.mjs. No dated doc, ratchet, or AGENTS.md rule bears on either change. The repo has a positive precedent for the exact `throwIfNoEntry: false` form at scripts/format-staged.mjs:310.

**Settled surface (kill test 5) — passed.** Build/CI tooling only. Not the `@agent/*` SDK surface (fsWalk.mjs is a build script, excluded from `files: ["dist","LICENSE.txt"]`), not the PocketFlow engine, not a platform port, not a browser-reachable util (`scripts/check-browser-safe-utils.mjs` scans src/utils, not scripts), not AgentEvent/SessionFact, not a vscode-free zone, and touches none of the five ratchets.

**Net-adds / churn (kill test 6) — passed, but only just.** Measured with prettier rather than trusting the claim: part (A) nets **zero** lines, part (B) nets ~-15 including the new `statSync` import. No new files, no new exports, no new module. Element count genuinely 0: `fileExists`, `onDiskSize`, both `isDirectory` methods and `walkFiles` all keep their names, signatures and async-ness; the only symbol churn is swapping one import binding (`stat` out of fs/promises, `statSync` in from node:fs) for another. This does not trip checklist §13's net-positive-LoC trap — but it is much thinner than advertised.

**Masking-site misread (kill test 7) — passed.** Ran each site against checklist §15's M1-M6. None is a masking site: every block is a narrow existence probe that suppresses exactly `ENOENT` and re-throws everything else (`if (error?.code === 'ENOENT') return X; throw error;`). `statSync(p, { throwIfNoEntry: false })` returns `undefined` on ENOENT and throws every other errno unchanged, so the loud-failure behavior is preserved bit for bit. This is not deleting a fallback, it is replacing a hand-rolled one with the builtin that encodes the same policy. Verified the candidate correctly excludes the two `readdir` catches (:288, :323) — `fsPromises.readdir` has no `throwIfNoEntry`, and neither does `fsPromises.stat` (options there are `{ bigint }` only, which is exactly why the sync switch is forced rather than gratuitous). Also verified the candidate is right to leave `fsWalk.isFile` alone: `throwIfNoEntry` suppresses ENOENT only, not the ENOTDIR arm at fsWalk.mjs:8, and that arm is load-bearing for the `path.join(candidate, 'index.ts')` probes at rewrite-declaration-aliases.mjs:39-42, which stat paths whose parent is a file.

**Runtime floor.** Confirmed `readdir(dir, { recursive: true, withFileTypes: true })` yields `parentPath` correctly on this machine (v26.7.0), and the binding CI floor is `node-version: '22'` in every workflow — past `Dirent.parentPath` (20.12/21.4) and recursive+withFileTypes (20.1). Symlink handling is unchanged: recursive readdir does not descend into symlinked directories, and neither does the current `entry.isDirectory()` descent. Ordering does change cross-directory (breadth-ish vs depth-first), and I confirmed neither consumer depends on it — validate-artifacts.mjs joins all declaration text into one blob and only reports `declarationFiles.length`, rewrite-declaration-aliases.mjs rewrites each file independently.

**Too thin (kill test 8) — marginal pass.** As written the PR is ~-15 lines and 0 elements, and one of its three files contributes nothing. It survives because it is batched across four sites with one coherent rationale, has an in-repo precedent, and carries no product risk. But I would only ship it with the missed fifth site (verify-desktop-installer-artifacts.mjs:152-167) folded in, taking it to ~-22 across three release gates; and I would consider dropping part (A) or keeping it explicitly as a readability change rather than a reduction, since a reviewer measuring lines will find it delivers none.

**Risk: medium, agreed but for a different reason than stated.** Not because the transformation is subtle — it is mechanical — but because `verify-desktop-build-artifacts.mjs` is an untested release gate (correction 5b). A wrong rewrite there fails open: `fileExists` returning `true` for a missing artifact would let a broken desktop build through silently. The `?.isFile() ?? false` form must be exact. Verification plan in the proposal is otherwise right, though `npx vitest run src/test-kernel/scripts/DesktopPackagePayload.vitest.ts` exercises only the verify-desktop-package.mjs half.

</details>

### M9-progress — Progress-view and session-fact plumbing

**Files**: `packages/cli/src/chat/tui/state/sessionSignalsAdapter.ts`, `packages/cli/src/runtime/cliNdjsonProgressEvents.ts`, `packages/cli/src/runtime/runProgressRenderer.ts`, `packages/cli/src/runtime/sessionProgressSubscription.ts`, `packages/desktop/src/main/desktopAgentExecution.ts`, `packages/desktop/src/main/desktopHostInteractions.ts`, `packages/extension/src/commands/housekeeping/fileOpRunner.ts`, `packages/extension/src/commands/housekeeping/fileOpSchemas.ts`, `packages/extension/src/commands/housekeeping/streamEventUtils.ts`, `src/agent/runtime/SessionEventHub.ts`, `src/controllers/approval/ToolEditApprovalController.ts`, `src/controllers/progressView/ProgressWorkflowActionsController.ts`, `src/controllers/progressView/backend/LitSessionRenderer.ts`, `src/controllers/progressView/backend/ProgressBackend.ts`, `src/controllers/progressView/backend/progressHostInteractions.ts`, `src/controllers/session/SessionFactApplier.ts`, `src/controllers/session/SessionRendererPort.ts`, `src/shared/schemas/inquiry.ts`, `src/shared/schemas/progressEvents.ts`, `src/test-kernel/agent/followUp/HumanPromptProgressEvents.vitest.ts`, `src/test-kernel/agent/progressTestUtils.ts`, `src/test-kernel/agent/runtime/SessionInteractions.vitest.ts`, `src/test-kernel/architecture/sessionPresentationBoundary.vitest.ts`, `src/test-kernel/desktop/DesktopHostInteractions.vitest.ts`, `src/test-kernel/desktop/DesktopToolEditApproval.vitest.ts`, `src/test-kernel/progressView/ExtensionHostInteractions.vitest.ts`, `src/test-kernel/progressView/ProgressBackendFactProjection.vitest.ts`, `src/tools/inquiry/ExternalInquiryTool.ts`, `src/tools/inquiry/inquiryContinuation.ts`, `src/transcript/StreamSnapshotStore.ts`

#### Inline the two-line createDesktopHostInteractions wrapper and delete its 405-line suite, which re-tests the shared progress-host machine the extension suite already covers

- **Lens**: `desktop-dead-on-arrival` · **Kind**: single-caller-wrapper · **Risk**: medium
- **Net**: -330 LoC, -7 elements (claimed -407 LoC, -3 elements; verifier figure governs)

**Evidence**

`packages/desktop/src/main/desktopHostInteractions.ts` is 22 lines whose entire body is `return { ...createProgressHostInteractions(options), showInfoMessage: options.showInfoMessage }`. It adds no logic, no host coupling, and no captured context — it fills in one field that `src/agent/runtime/HostInteractions.ts:356` already declares as optional (`showInfoMessage?(message: string): Promise<void> | void`).

Production consumers: exactly 1. `grep -rn "createDesktopHostInteractions" packages src` (node_modules and dist excluded) returns `packages/desktop/src/main/desktopAgentExecution.ts:113` (import) and `:370` (the sole call). `packages/desktop/src/main/mainViewIpc.ts:27` imports only the _type_ of a sibling module, not this one. Non-production consumers: 2 files — `src/test-kernel/desktop/DesktopHostInteractions.vitest.ts:113-138` and `src/test-kernel/agent/runtime/SessionInteractions.vitest.ts:18,142`. There is no `loadSourceModule` registry row for it; the desktop suite dynamic-imports it by file URL at :113-115.

This is the residue of a _completed_ consolidation, not a duplicate of it. `gh issue list --label tech-debt --state all --search "hostInteractions"` surfaces CLOSED #9413 'Tech debt: merge the twin extension/desktop HostInteractions pending-map implementations'. That merge shipped in `97643d6fca feat: implement the approved cross-host consistency plan (#9489)` (2026-07-31): the module went 256 lines -> 22 lines in that commit (`git show 656c991419:.../desktopHostInteractions.ts | wc -l` = 256; `git show 97643d6fca:.../desktopHostInteractions.ts | wc -l` = 22). What #9489 did not sweep is the now-trivial wrapper and its test suite.

The suite is the bigger half. `src/test-kernel/desktop/DesktopHostInteractions.vitest.ts` is 405 lines / 9 tests, and not one of them touches `showInfoMessage` — the only thing the wrapper contributes (`:135` passes `showInfoMessage: vi.fn()` and never asserts on it). All 9 pin shared `createProgressHostInteractions` behavior, and the counterpart suite `src/test-kernel/progressView/ExtensionHostInteractions.vitest.ts` (884 lines, 25 tests) already pins the same machine through the other host. Overlaps, several verbatim: 'approves already-pending delegated work only in the selected stream' (desktop :147, extension :188 — same title, same assertion sequence on `parallel`/`bash`/`other`, same `handlers.transport.proposal.dismiss` check, same `submitProposalDecision`/`submitBashDecision` settlement); 'forwards a bash cancellation cause without user provenance' (desktop :263, extension :697 — same title); 'delegates tool edit approvals to the window controller' (desktop :215) vs 'delegates tool edit approval to the session approval controller' (extension :866); 'routes tool-edit cancellation to the window controller' (desktop :311) vs extension :683; 'rejects a plan decision for a pending bash request' (desktop :233) vs 'rejects a resolution whose kind does not match the pending request' (extension :717); 'reveals the owning stream before showing an external inquiry' (desktop :327) vs extension :340 + :775; 'cancels all pending requests on dispose with a stable cause' (desktop :377) vs extension :644/:664. The one assertion the desktop version has that the extension version lacks is the await-tool-edits gate at desktop :168-192 (`releaseToolEdits`, `approvalCompleted === false` before release) — shared-machine behavior worth porting, ~12 lines.

`src/test-kernel/agent/runtime/SessionInteractions.vitest.ts` only uses the wrapper as a convenient factory at :142 with `showInfoMessage: vi.fn()`; its actual `showInfoMessage` assertions (:339, :361, :381, :405, :418, :495, :515, :534) all build plain object literals, so the import is a one-line swap. Removing it also drops a `@desktop/*` import from a kernel test under `src/test-kernel/agent/`.

The extension's counterpart, `packages/extension/src/progressView/extensionHostInteractions.ts`, is NOT in scope: it adds four real VS Code capabilities (`readDiagnostics`, `addCriticism`, `openPdf`, `reportReviewIssue`) with real bodies, so it is a legitimate host adapter.

Dedupe: `git log --all --oneline --grep=createDesktopHostInteractions` returns nothing. `grep -c desktopHostInteractions docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md` is 0; not in the round-1 index. The `docs/proposals/2026-07-09-*` and `2026-07-26-*` references to `desktopHostInteractions.ts` all cite line numbers 70-133 of the pre-#9489 256-line version and are historical, not a justification for the current 22-line shape.

**Proposal**

1. At `packages/desktop/src/main/desktopAgentExecution.ts:370`, replace `createDesktopHostInteractions({...})` with `{ ...createProgressHostInteractions({ interactions: presentationHost, session: this.session, getApprovalHandlers: ..., getToolEditApprovals: ..., setApprovalBypassState: ... }), showInfoMessage: (message) => this.options.host.showInfoMessage(message) }` — every argument is already constructed inline at that call site, so nothing else moves (no R5 churn). Update the import at :113.
2. Delete `packages/desktop/src/main/desktopHostInteractions.ts` (exported function `createDesktopHostInteractions`, file-local interface `DesktopHostInteractionsOptions`).
3. Port the await-tool-edits gate from `src/test-kernel/desktop/DesktopHostInteractions.vitest.ts:168-192` into the matching extension test at `src/test-kernel/progressView/ExtensionHostInteractions.vitest.ts:188` (~12 lines), then delete `DesktopHostInteractions.vitest.ts`.
4. In `src/test-kernel/agent/runtime/SessionInteractions.vitest.ts`, swap the import at :18 to `createProgressHostInteractions` from `@controllers/progressView/backend/progressHostInteractions` and drop the now-unused `showInfoMessage: vi.fn()` line at :146.
5. PR body carries the R6 net-element table and the R8 consumer counts above.

**What we give up**

The desktop host's instantiation of the shared progress-host port loses its own dedicated test file; regressions in `createProgressHostInteractions` would be caught by the extension suite over the same code path rather than by a desktop-flavored one. Since the desktop wrapper contributes zero logic after #9489, the only desktop-specific fact left unpinned is 'desktop supplies showInfoMessage', which the TypeScript structural check at the call site already enforces. Restoring is trivial (re-add a wrapper); no runtime behavior changes.

**Verifier corrections to the evidence above**

Ten factual errors in the evidence, none fatal but several material to the LoC math and the risk rating.

1. WRONG UNIQUE-COVERAGE COUNT (the big one). The claim "The one assertion the desktop version has that the extension version lacks is the await-tool-edits gate at desktop :168-192 ... ~12 lines" is false. I ran the suite (`npx vitest run src/test-kernel/desktop/DesktopHostInteractions.vitest.ts` -> 9 passed) and diffed every test against `src/test-kernel/progressView/ExtensionHostInteractions.vitest.ts`. FIVE behaviors are unique, not one:
   - the await-tool-edits gate inside desktop :147 (`releaseToolEdits`, `approvalCompleted === false` before release) — extension :188 calls `approvePendingDelegatedWork` and immediately awaits, never pinning the gate. ~15 lines.
   - desktop :289 'can cancel synchronously while presenting a request' — extension :315 'rejects a request when its show transport fails synchronously' is a _throwing_ transport (`rejects.toThrow`), a different code path from a re-entrant `interactions.cancel()` inside `show`. 21 lines.
   - desktop :348 'preserves typed proposal approval overrides' — extension :188 submits `{ action: 'approve' }` with no `model`/`agent` override; no extension test asserts typed override passthrough. 28 lines.
   - desktop :377 'cancels all pending requests on dispose with a stable cause' — `grep -n 'dispose\|SESSION_DISPOSED' src/test-kernel/progressView/ExtensionHostInteractions.vitest.ts` returns exactly one hit, `:54  for (const session of testSessions.splice(0)) session.dispose();`. This is the ONLY test in the repo pinning `createProgressHostInteractions().dispose()` -> cancel-all with `SESSION_DISPOSED_CAUSE` (progressHostInteractions.ts:447-448). `SessionInteractions.vitest.ts`'s dispose tests (:549, :580) use hand-built adapter literals, not this factory. 28 lines.
   - desktop :327 'reveals the owning stream before showing an external inquiry' asserts `presentationSink.emit('requestEnsureProgressView', {})` plus `setActiveStream{suppressViewSwitch,ensureVisible}`. I read extension :775-805 in full: it asserts only `externalInquiry.show` args and `dismissExternalInquiry`. Extension :340 is a _retry_ focus test. Neither covers the reveal. ~20 lines.
     Port budget is ~90-110 lines, not 12.

2. WRONG OVERLAP ATTRIBUTION for desktop :377. The candidate maps it to extension :644/:664. Actual titles: :644 'wraps cancel-path settlement when the dismiss transport throws', :664 'cancels pending retry requests for a removed stream'. Neither is a dispose test.

3. THE "WHAT WE GIVE UP" SECTION IS BACKWARDS. "the TypeScript structural check at the call site already enforces" that desktop supplies `showInfoMessage` is false. `showInfoMessage?(...)` is OPTIONAL at `src/agent/runtime/HostInteractions.ts:356`, and `ProgressHostInteractions extends HostInteractions` (progressHostInteractions.ts:54), so the inlined object literal would compile fine with the field dropped. `DesktopHostInteractionsOptions` (desktopHostInteractions.ts:7-9) redeclares it as REQUIRED. That narrowing is the wrapper's one real contribution, so "it adds no logic, no host coupling, and no captured context" overstates: it narrows a type. Small, but it should be named honestly in the PR body rather than asserted away.

4. Also unlisted: the candidate says "All 9 pin shared createProgressHostInteractions behavior" then tables overlaps for only 7 (:147, :215, :233, :263, :311, :327, :377), silently omitting :289 and :348 — the two that turn out to be unique.

5. PRODUCTION LoC UNDERSTATED. `desktopAgentExecution.ts:29` currently imports `ProgressHostInteractions` **type-only**; it must become a value import of `createProgressHostInteractions` too (prettier will wrap it), and the call site grows 370-377 (8 lines) -> 10 lines. Deleting the 22-line file nets about **-18** production lines, not -22.

6. `packages/desktop/src/main/mainViewIpc.ts:27` is a red herring. `grep -rn 'desktopHostInteractions' packages src` (node_modules/dist excluded) returns zero mainViewIpc hits at all. The production-consumer count of 1 (`desktopAgentExecution.ts:113` import, `:370` call) is CORRECT as stated.

7. MISSED JUSTIFICATION FOR THE DELETION (helps the candidate, but it left it out): the desktop harness at `DesktopHostInteractions.vitest.ts:132` does **not** pass `setApprovalBypassState`, which `ProgressHostInteractionsOptions` (progressHostInteractions.ts:42-48) declares as required. The suite only compiles because it dynamic-imports by file URL behind a hand-written 54-line mirror interface (`:21-74`). So the suite (a) never exercises the bypass path and (b) carries a hand-mirrored type that drifts silently. That is a stronger argument than the ones given.

8. Blast-radius checks the evidence never ran, all clean: `createRecordingApprovalHandlers` survives via `ExtensionHostInteractions.vitest.ts:14` and `AgentPresentationHostReplayGate.vitest.ts:18`; `moduleFileUrl`/`desktopSourcePath` survive via 7 other desktop suites; `desktopHostInteractions` appears in no ratchet (`grep -rn 'desktopHostInteractions' config/ratchets/*.json` = 0 hits, including `knip-baseline.json`).

9. SessionInteractions swap is correct as stated (:18 import, :142 call, :146 `showInfoMessage: vi.fn()`), and I confirmed the eight real `showInfoMessage` assertions (:339, :361, :381, :405, :418, :495, :515, :534) all build standalone `session.interactions.use({...})` literals on separate sessions. `setApprovalBypassState` is already passed at :145, so no other change is needed.

10. Claimed net -407 LoC is optimistic by roughly 85 lines once the four extra behaviors are ported.

<details><summary>Verifier reasoning</summary>

DUPLICATE (kill criterion 1): no. `grep -in 'hostInteraction'` over round1-index.md returns nothing (its five 'desktop' rows are icons, theme tokens, CLI bundle, getAgents seam, Sentry). `grep -c -i 'hostinteraction' docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md` = 0. I pulled the file list for every round-1 PR #11416-#11422 via `gh pr view --json files`; none touches `desktopHostInteractions.ts` or `DesktopHostInteractions.vitest.ts`. Both still present and passing at HEAD.

ALREADY FILED (2): no. `gh issue list --label tech-debt --state all --search 'hostInteractions'` and `--search 'DesktopHostInteractions'` return 20+ CLOSED issues, all predecessors — #9413 (the twin-pending-map merge, the very consolidation whose residue this is), #7447, #8556, #9251, #9142, #10584. Nothing open, nothing matching this residue. `git log --all --oneline --grep=createDesktopHostInteractions` = empty.

HISTORY CLAIM VERIFIED: `git show 656c991419:...desktopHostInteractions.ts | wc -l` = 256, `git show 97643d6fca:... | wc -l` = 22. The 97643d6fca (#9489) attribution is correct, and `git log --oneline -- packages/desktop/src/main/desktopHostInteractions.ts` confirms only a test-sweep commit (b0e0341c88) touched it since. This really is unswept residue of a completed merge, not a re-proposal of it.

CONSUMER COUNT (3): re-grepped myself across `packages` and `src` for the symbol and the module basename, including .json/.md. Production = 1 call site. Non-production = 2 test files. Nothing in `packages/extension/package.json`, `commands.ts`, `*Settings.ts` schemas, `resources/`, `prompts/`, or `supabase/functions/` — this is a main-process factory with no command id, config key, or wire string, so there is no dynamic-dispatch surface to miss. No `loadSourceModule` registry row (the suite uses `moduleFileUrl(desktopSourcePath(...))` at :113-115, as claimed).

DELIBERATELY JUSTIFIED (4): no. The docs mentioning `desktopHostInteractions.ts` (2026-07-09 host-parity-audit :120, 2026-07-09 tech-debt-error-ownership :16/:50, 2026-07-09 tech-debt-audit-runtime-ui :121/:331/:560, 2026-07-26 agent-sdk-foundation-gap :120/:437) all cite line numbers 64-133 of the 256-line pre-#9489 file. Historical, not a ruling on the current shape. No ratchet row. AGENTS.md 'Testing discipline' and CLAUDE.md's 'Tests are a budget, not proof of work' cut FOR this, not against.

SETTLED SURFACE (5): no. `@controllers/*`, not `@agent/*`, so the host deep-import ratchet is untouched; not the PocketFlow engine, not a platform port, not a browser-safe util, not the AgentEvent/SessionFact split.

NET-ADDS (6): no. Production -18, tests -405 +~95 port -1 = -311. Total about -330. No unrelated churn: every argument at :370-377 is already constructed inline, so the inline is mechanical.

MASKING SITE (7): n/a — no catch or fallback is deleted.

TOO THIN (8): borderline on the production half alone (-18 LoC, one 22-line file), but the test consolidation carries it. It is the same shape as the `checkCliUpdateAvailable` candidate already documented in the round-1 survey doc, so it is consistent with what this campaign ships.

WHY MEDIUM AND NOT LOW: five behaviors must be ported, not one, and one of them (dispose -> cancel-all with SESSION_DISPOSED_CAUSE) is the sole coverage anywhere of a real lifecycle path in a 452-line concurrency machine. If the implementing PR follows the candidate's own step 3 literally (port 12 lines, delete the file), it silently drops four behaviors. The PR must port all five or this becomes a coverage regression dressed as a simplification.

One judgment note for the implementer, not a refutation: `createProgressHostInteractions` (452 lines, the actual machine) has no suite of its own — both host suites test it by proxy. Folding desktop's into `ExtensionHostInteractions.vitest.ts` grows a file named after a 41-line VS Code adapter to ~980 lines. Retargeting the desktop suite into `src/test-kernel/progressView/ProgressHostInteractions.vitest.ts` (testing the shared factory directly, which also drops the 54-line hand-mirrored module interface and the file-URL dynamic import) would be the better landing spot and costs no extra lines. Either way the wrapper deletion stands.

</details>

#### Delete the always-true skipProgressViewClear flag and the clearMissingOutputs fact it makes unreachable

- **Lens**: `options-and-boolean-params` · **Kind**: dead-export · **Risk**: medium
- **Net**: -260 LoC, -12 elements (claimed -225 LoC, -8 elements; verifier figure governs)

**Evidence**

The boolean is single-valued at its only producer, and that makes an entire session-fact channel unreachable.

PRODUCER (1, always `true`): `src/controllers/progressView/ProgressWorkflowActionsController.ts:96` sets `skipProgressViewClear: true` inside `runFileOperation`. The field is REQUIRED on the request type (`ProgressWorkflowActionsController.ts:32` `skipProgressViewClear: boolean;`), so no producer can omit it, and `ProgressWorkflowActionsController.ts:82` is the only construction site of `WorkflowFileOperationRequest` (`rg 'runFileOperation|WorkflowFileOperation\b'` production hits: ProgressViewCommandHandlers.ts:551,553 -> controller:82 -> deps at packages/extension/src/progressView/ProgressViewMessageHandler.ts:681 and packages/desktop/src/main/desktopAgentExecution.ts:475).

CONSUMER (1, statically false): `packages/extension/src/commands/housekeeping/fileOpRunner.ts:81` `if (!config.skipProgressViewClear && config.streamId) emitClearMissingOutputs(config.streamId);`. The desktop leg ignores both fields entirely — `packages/desktop/src/main/desktopAgentExecution.ts:591` destructures only `{ agent, model, inputFile, executionId }`.

NO OTHER CONFIG PRODUCER. The config object reaches the runner only through `texra.pack` / `texra.clean` (`extensionCommandHandlers.ts:247` and `:262`, both `definedHandler(z.tuple([PackConfigSchema|CleanConfigSchema]))`), and those two commands are invoked in production from exactly one place: `ProgressViewMessageHandler.ts:682` `runViewCommand(`texra.${operation}`, [request])`. Both are `"when": "false"` in the commandPalette contribution (verified by parsing packages/extension/package.json contributes.menus — the only two entries for these ids), and there are no view/title or editor menu entries for them. The main-view Pack/Clean buttons use the positional-arg commands instead (`packages/extension/src/webview/managers/executionHandlers.ts:130-153` -> `texra.packSingle`/`packMultiple`/`cleanSingle`/`cleanMultiple`), which carry no streamId and deliberately do not clear (`packages/extension/src/commands/housekeeping/packCommands.ts:64-66` comment).

CASCADE — `clearMissingOutputs` has ZERO production emitters. `rg 'clearMissingOutputs|CLEAR_MISSING_OUTPUTS'`: the only producer is `packages/extension/src/commands/housekeeping/streamEventUtils.ts:15` (whole file, 19 lines, one call site = the dead guard above). Non-production emitters: `src/test-kernel/support/storeTestDrivers.ts:161-164` only. Consumers that therefore go dead: `src/agent/runtime/SessionEventHub.ts:35` (fact variant), `src/shared/schemas/progressEvents.ts:97` (`ClearMissingOutputsPayload`), `src/controllers/session/SessionFactApplier.ts:118` and `:333-337`, `src/transcript/StreamSnapshotStore.ts:728-731` plus the private `clearMissingOutputs` method at `:1185-1200` and its 12-line provenance comment, `packages/cli/src/runtime/sessionProgressSubscription.ts:72-73`, `packages/cli/src/runtime/cliNdjsonProgressEvents.ts:66`. Second-order: `SessionFactApplier.ts:336` is the ONLY caller passing `{ reset: true }` to `onMissingOutputsChanged`, so the `options?: { reset?: boolean }` arm of the port (`src/controllers/session/SessionRendererPort.ts:89-95`) and its branch in `src/controllers/progressView/backend/LitSessionRenderer.ts:217-238` also die (the other `reset: true` at LitSessionRenderer.ts:166 belongs to UPDATE_COMPILE_FAILURES, a different command; `SessionFactApplier.ts:200` calls onMissingOutputsChanged with no options).

HISTORY / DEDUPE. `git log -S'skipProgressViewClear: true'` — introduced by `fb60dc5868` "fix: scope progress storage actions per run", i.e. the progress-view path was deliberately opted OUT of the clear; #9590/#9647 (`51e21cb764`) then rewrote the emit for exact addressing but left it with no live caller. `gh issue list --label tech-debt --state all --search "missing outputs"` -> #8034, #9143, #5451, all CLOSED and unrelated. Not in the round-1 index or docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md (grepped `skipProgressViewClear`, `clearMissingOutputs`: 0 hits).

Non-production consumers to delete with the behavior: src/test-kernel/support/storeTestDrivers.ts:113,161-164; src/test-kernel/transcript/StreamSnapshotStore.vitest.ts:784-830, :871, :1572; src/test-kernel/cli/CliSessionProgressSubscription.vitest.ts:171-172; src/test-kernel/cli/TuiStateAndFocus.vitest.ts:3858,3876; src/test-kernel/progressView/ProgressBackendFactProjection.vitest.ts:850; src/test-kernel/controllers/ProgressWorkflowActionsController.vitest.ts:101,129; src/test-kernel/progressView/ProgressViewOnboardingRefresh.vitest.ts:515.

**Proposal**

1. Delete `skipProgressViewClear` from `WorkflowFileOperationRequest` (ProgressWorkflowActionsController.ts:32) and from the literal at :96; delete `streamId` from the same request (no reader survives) and from `fileOpConfigFields` (fileOpSchemas.ts:35-37).
2. Delete the `if (!config.skipProgressViewClear && config.streamId)` block (fileOpRunner.ts:79-83), its `emitClearMissingOutputs` import (:8), and the `skipProgressViewClear`/`streamId` fields on `FileOpConfig` (:17-18). Delete packages/extension/src/commands/housekeeping/streamEventUtils.ts entirely.
3. State the R8 consumer counts (emitters: 1 production, now 0; subscribers listed above) and delete the `clearMissingOutputs` variant from SessionEventHub.ts:34-37, `ClearMissingOutputsPayload` from progressEvents.ts:97, the two SessionFactApplier arms, the StreamSnapshotStore case arm and private method, the CLI subscription arm and the NDJSON event-map key.
4. Drop the now-unreachable `reset` option from `onMissingOutputsChanged` on the port and in LitSessionRenderer, plus the `reset: true` arm of the UPDATE_MISSING_OUTPUTS message and its frontend handling.
5. Delete the tests listed above rather than rewriting them (AGENTS.md testing discipline: tests pinning retired behavior go with it).
   If a reviewer prefers a smaller first cut, steps 1-2 alone are behavior-identical and self-contained; steps 3-5 are the deletion the flag was hiding.

**What we give up**

The ability for a pack/clean run to clear a workflow tab's missing-outputs badge. Nothing has exercised it since the progress-view path opted out, so this is a no-behavior-change deletion; if the product later wants that clear, the right implementation is a snapshot re-read in the progress backend after a file operation, not re-emitting a session fact through the extension command layer. Also removes a never-emitted `clearMissingOutputs` key from the CLI NDJSON progress-event map — a wire-contract shrink worth calling out in the PR body.

**Verifier corrections to the evidence above**

The structural core holds, but the evidence has one wrong deletion claim, one missed dated ruling, one missed governance rule, and a materially undercounted deletion set.

**C1 — WRONG (step 4, half of it is undeletable).** "Drop ... the `reset: true` arm of the UPDATE_MISSING_OUTPUTS message and its frontend handling" is false. `reset` is not a per-command field: `src/shared/schemas/progressView/outbound.ts:116-125` defines one generic `RoundUpdateMessageSchema<C, T>` with `rounds?` + `reset?`, instantiated three times (`UpdateFilesMessageSchema` :127, `UpdateMissingOutputsMessageSchema` :132, `UpdateCompileFailuresMessageSchema` :137). The frontend handler is equally shared: `packages/extension/src/progressView/frontend/slices/runTrackingSlice.ts:31-54` `applyRoundUpdate(data, field)` is keyed by all three commands at :57-64. `UPDATE_COMPILE_FAILURES` still sends `reset: true` unconditionally (`LitSessionRenderer.ts:166`), so neither the schema field nor one line of frontend code can be deleted. The only deletable surface in step 4 is the port option (`SessionRendererPort.ts:90-96`, doc + param) and the `if (options?.reset)` branch in `LitSessionRenderer.ts:221-228`.

**C2 — MISSED RULING (guardrail 4).** The evidence says "Not in ... docs/proposals", but `rg clearMissingOutputs docs/proposals` returns `2026-08-16-define-out-of-existence.md:262-267` and `:302`, a dated **REFUTED, keep** ruling on exactly the reset arm: "the verifier found ... a factual error in the deletion case (the `reset` arm is the _only_ path that propagates `clearMissingOutputs`) ... Recorded so nobody re-proposes the UI-side deletion", reaffirmed 2026-08-19 at :302. The candidate beats it — that ruling presumes a live producer, and there is none — but a PR that does not cite and amend that doc will be bounced by a reviewer who greps for it. The store-side overlay machinery is _not_ what that ruling protects ("seed-authoritative sender reads" is about the non-reset merge path, which this deletion leaves alone).

**C3 — MISSED GOVERNANCE (guardrail 4, partial).** Deleting `clearMissingOutputs` from `packages/cli/src/runtime/cliNdjsonProgressEvents.ts:66` is covered by `docs/proposals/2026-08-03-ssot-consolidation-plan.md` §0.1 item 6 ("Frozen wire stays frozen: CLI NDJSON names — deletions ride the D3 deprecation clock, 0.40 deprecate / 0.41 delete") and §10.1 ("Deletions of public keys ride the D3 deprecation clock, never a refactor"), owned by open issue #6984. HEAD is 0.40.6. The deletion is still defensible — zero emitters means byte-parity is trivially preserved and the key is a type-map entry no consumer can ever have received — but calling it "a wire-contract shrink worth calling out in the PR body" without citing the clock is exactly the framing that gets it reverted.

**C4 — UNDERCOUNTED DELETION (in the candidate's favour).** Killing the fact also collapses the store's reset-aware overlay, which exists solely for it: `RoundOverlay<T>` + its 8-line doc (`StreamSnapshotStore.ts:219-231`), `mergeMissingOutputsOverlay` + its 10-line doc (`:297-317`), the `{ reset: false, patch }` wrapper at `:1013`, `OverlayPatches.missingOutputs` (`:246`) and the 3-line `consumeOverlay` callback at `:2180-2183` all reduce to the `mergeRoundPatch` / `Map<number, string[] | null>` shape already used by `outputFiles` and `compileFailures`. That is ~45 more lines than the evidence counted, and 2 more elements.

**C5 — LINE/COUNT NITS.** (a) `fileOpSchemas.ts:35-37` is not contiguous for the two doomed fields — `streamId` is :35, `skipProgressViewClear` is :37, `executionId` (:36, live, read by `fileOpRunner.ts:62`) sits between them. (b) The `clearMissingOutputs` provenance comment is 18 lines (`StreamSnapshotStore.ts:1167-1184`), not 12. (c) `SessionRendererPort.ts:89-95` is off by one: doc :90-92, method :93-96. (d) The desktop destructure is `desktopAgentExecution.ts:591` — correct. (e) Everything else I re-checked matched: `ProgressWorkflowActionsController.ts:32,82,96`; `fileOpRunner.ts:8,17-18,81`; `ProgressViewCommandHandlers.ts:551,553`; `ProgressViewMessageHandler.ts:681-682`; `extensionCommandHandlers.ts:247,262`; `streamEventUtils.ts` 19 lines total; `SessionEventHub.ts:34-37`; `progressEvents.ts:97`; `SessionFactApplier.ts:118,199-200,333-337`; `StreamSnapshotStore.ts:728-731,1185-1200`; `sessionProgressSubscription.ts:72-73`; `cliNdjsonProgressEvents.ts:66`; `LitSessionRenderer.ts:166,217-238`.

**C6 — CONTEXT the PR should carry (strengthens the case).** #9647's own PR body enumerates every emitter and states "The progress-view toolbar path ... already passes its exact `streamId` plus `skipProgressViewClear: true` and is unchanged" — i.e. the author who made the addressing exact recorded, without noticing, that the last live caller was already opted out. This is unnoticed dead code, not a path held open for future use, and no doc or issue states an intent to flip the flag back.

**C7 — MERGE RISK.** `fileOpRunner.ts` was touched by round-1 PR #11417 (`16d66fd077`), which un-exported `FileOpConfig`/`FileOpActions`; the survey doc's line 565 still describes them as exported. Rebase before citing line numbers.

<details><summary>Verifier reasoning</summary>

**1. Producer census — re-grepped, holds.** `rg 'skipProgressViewClear|clearMissingOutputs|CLEAR_MISSING_OUTPUTS|emitClearMissingOutputs'` over the whole tree: 47 matches, 20 files. The only production emit site is `packages/extension/src/commands/housekeeping/streamEventUtils.ts:15`; the only caller of `emitClearMissingOutputs` is the guard at `fileOpRunner.ts:81`; the only construction of `WorkflowFileOperationRequest` is `ProgressWorkflowActionsController.ts:89-97`, which hardcodes `skipProgressViewClear: true` on a field typed `boolean` (required, :32), so no producer can omit it. Guard is statically false at every production entry.

**2. Config entry points — checked the ones the brief demands.** I parsed `packages/extension/package.json` `contributes.menus` with Python: `texra.pack` and `texra.clean` appear in exactly one menu each, both `commandPalette` with `"when": "false"`. No view/title, editor, or explorer entries. The walkthrough (`package.json:595`) links `command:texra.cleanOutput` / `command:texra.cleanBuild`, different commands; the `onCommand:texra.pack` entries at :603-604 are walkthrough completion events, not invocations. `src/shared/commands/catalog.ts:155,163` declares them, `extensionCommandHandlers.ts:247,262` binds them to `z.tuple([PackConfigSchema])` / `z.tuple([CleanConfigSchema])`. The only production dispatcher is `ProgressViewMessageHandler.ts:682` `runViewCommand(\`texra.${operation}\`, [request])`. The main-view Pack/Clean buttons route to `texra.packSingle`/`packMultiple`/`cleanSingle`/`cleanMultiple`via`executionHandlers.ts:130-153`and`mainViewActions.ts:378-390`— positional args, no config object, and`packCommands.ts:63-66`/`cleanCommands.ts:60-62`carry explicit comments that they clear nothing. Desktop reads`{ agent, model, inputFile, executionId }` only (`desktopAgentExecution.ts:591`); `streamId`and`skipProgressViewClear`are never read there. No`executeCommand('texra.pack'|'texra.clean')`anywhere. Nothing in`prompts/`, `packages/extension/resources/`, `supabase/functions/`, or `src/shared/schemas/*Settings.ts` references any of these symbols.

**3. Fact channel — no other producer, no replay path.** `SessionFact` is an in-memory TS union with no Zod parse, no persistence, and no inbound wire construction: `rg SessionFact` over non-test production returns only `SessionFactApplier.ts`, `ProgressBackend.ts`, and a mention in `AppSignals.ts`; `TexraTranscriptRecorder.ts` has zero `SessionFact` hits (it records run-scoped `updateMissingOutputs` at :797, a different channel). So there is no persisted or replayed `clearMissingOutputs` record to break, and no generic forwarder that could synthesize the arm from untyped input — every `scope: 'session'` emit site is a typed literal. The only non-production emitter is `storeTestDrivers.ts:161-164`.

**4. Cascade — verified each consumer, and found more than the claim.** `SessionEventHub.ts:34-37`, `progressEvents.ts:90-99`, `SessionFactApplier.ts:118` + `:333-337`, `StreamSnapshotStore.ts:728-731` + private method `:1185-1200`, `sessionProgressSubscription.ts:72-73`, `cliNdjsonProgressEvents.ts:66` — all real, all confirmed. Beyond the claim, `SessionFactApplier.ts:333-337` is the only `{ reset: true }` caller (`:199-200` passes no options; the other `reset: true` at `LitSessionRenderer.ts:166` is the compile-failures slice, correctly identified), and the store's `RoundOverlay` / `mergeMissingOutputsOverlay` / `consumeOverlay(... patch.reset ...)` triple exists only to interleave a clear with an update on an unseeded stream — with no clear, `missingOutputs` becomes structurally identical to `outputFiles` and `compileFailures`. The CLI/TUI implementors (`runProgressRenderer.ts:442`, `sessionSignalsAdapter.ts:159`) already take `streamId` only, so dropping the port option costs them nothing.

**5. Guardrail sweep.** (1) Not in the 95-line round-1 index (grepped `pack|clean|missing|SessionFact|NDJSON|snapshot` — no related title) and not in the 49-candidate survey (`skipProgressViewClear|clearMissingOutputs|streamEventUtils|fileOpRunner|onMissingOutputsChanged` → 0 hits; the survey's only fileOpRunner mention is the `FileOpConfig`/`FileOpActions` un-export, already landed by #11417). (2) Not filed: `gh issue list --state all --search` on clearMissingOutputs / skipProgressViewClear / "missing outputs badge pack clean" returns only CLOSED #9590 (tracking, Stages 1-6 landed) and CLOSED #9144, neither covering this. `git log` on the four primary paths shows the last structural touches are #11417, #11402, #11393 — none removed it. (3) Consumer counts re-derived from my own greps, above. (4) Two rulings found and weighed — see corrections C2 and C3; the reset-arm ruling is beatable because its stated premise (a live `clearMissingOutputs` producer) is false, and the NDJSON clock is satisfiable because the key has zero emitters so emitted bytes cannot change. (5) No settled surface: no `@agent/*` deep-import specifier added (one is _removed_ — `defaultSession` from `@agent/runtime` in `streamEventUtils.ts`), no PocketFlow engine, no platform port, no browser-safe util, no ratchet baseline widened; the AgentEvent/SessionFact split is respected — an arm is removed from `SessionFact`, the split itself is untouched. (6) Pure subtraction, no relocation: nothing moves to a new home, and the store simplification folds onto machinery that already exists for two sibling accumulators. (7) §15 does not apply — `skipProgressViewClear: true` is an always-on feature flag, not a `catch {}`, a `??` over a failed read, or a `.catch(default)`; nothing is being silently swallowed. (8) Not thin: 12 files, ~156 production lines, ~12 elements.

**6. The one genuine counter-argument, and why it does not kill it.** A maintainer could reasonably say "flip the flag to `false` instead" — `fb60dc5868` (2025-11-11) opted the progress-view path out because the emit used config-derived stream-id fan-out that hit look-alike tabs, and #9647 later made addressing exact, which would make re-enabling safe. But nine months passed, no issue reports the stale badge, no doc records an intent to re-enable, and #9647's own body treats the opted-out path as settled. That is a product decision the owner can reverse cheaply later (the candidate names the correct re-implementation: a snapshot re-read in the progress backend). It is the reason risk is medium rather than low, not a refutation.

</details>

#### Delete the two write-only SessionFact payload fields: setActiveStream.ensureVisible and inquiryThreadUpdated.resumeOutcome

- **Lens**: `event-fact-plumbing` · **Kind**: dead-export · **Risk**: medium
- **Net**: -46 LoC, -7 elements (claimed -38 LoC, -6 elements; verifier figure governs)

**Evidence**

Both are fields on `SessionFact` payload types that production writes and nothing anywhere reads. Batched because each alone is a ~15-line deletion.

(1) `SetActiveStreamPayload.ensureVisible` — declared at src/shared/schemas/progressEvents.ts:36-40 ("When suppressing a switch, widen a restrictive category filter so this stream remains reachable").
Production WRITERS (3): src/controllers/approval/ToolEditApprovalController.ts:396, src/controllers/progressView/backend/progressHostInteractions.ts:109, src/tools/inquiry/ExternalInquiryTool.ts:311 — all three pass `{ suppressViewSwitch: true, ensureVisible: true }`.
Production READERS: ZERO. `rg -n "ensureVisible" src/controllers src/agent packages/cli/src packages/extension/src packages/desktop/src` returns only the three writer lines. The three fact consumers each destructure `setActiveStream` payloads without touching it: src/controllers/session/SessionFactApplier.ts:651-663 reads only `streamId`/`agentCategory`/`isRemote`; src/controllers/progressView/backend/ProgressBackend.ts:398 reads only `suppressViewSwitch`; packages/cli/src/chat/tui/state/sessionSignalsAdapter.ts:269 reads only `suppressViewSwitch`.
Non-production: 8 lines, all writers or fixtures — src/test-kernel/progressView/ExtensionHostInteractions.vitest.ts:287,365; src/test-kernel/agent/progressTestUtils.ts:198; src/test-kernel/desktop/DesktopHostInteractions.vitest.ts:104; src/test-kernel/desktop/DesktopToolEditApproval.vitest.ts:400; src/test-kernel/agent/followUp/HumanPromptProgressEvents.vitest.ts:118,180 — plus one negative-regex token at src/test-kernel/architecture/sessionPresentationBoundary.vitest.ts:28 (`not.toMatch(/suppressViewSwitch|ensureVisible|.../)`), i.e. an assertion that the name must NOT appear.
Why it died: `git log -S"ensureVisible"` → introduced by aa2bc2eea5 (2026-07-12, "fix(progress): stop interaction requests from stealing active-stream focus") where the reader set `this.state.agentCategoryFilter = 'all'`; 88e20e4358 (#9519, 2026-08-01) deleted both reader blocks along with the whole `agentCategoryFilter` concept — `rg -n "agentCategoryFilter" src packages` now returns ZERO hits repo-wide. Ambiguous corpus (scripts/, docs/): zero — `rg -n "ensureVisible" .` outside src/ and packages/ returns nothing, so no doc pins it as public NDJSON.

(2) `InquiryThreadUpdatedEvent.resumeOutcome` — declared at src/shared/schemas/inquiry.ts:44-58, comment says "Resume outcome — UI badge metadata for inquiryThreadUpdated events".
Production WRITERS (3): src/tools/inquiry/inquiryContinuation.ts:116 (the `extra: { resumeOutcome }` parameter of `emitInquiryThreadUpdate`), :135 (`{ resumeOutcome: 'parent_finished' }`), :171 (`{ resumeOutcome: outcome }`). It is merged into the emitted fact at :121 and travels the wire via src/shared/schemas/progressView/outbound.ts:269,274 (SYNC_INQUIRY_THREADS / UPDATE_INQUIRY_THREAD) and packages/cli/src/runtime/cliNdjsonProgressEvents.ts:74.
Production READERS: ZERO. `rg -n "resumeOutcome" packages` → no hits at all; `rg -n "resumeOutcome" src/controllers src/transcript` → no hits. The one host that renders these threads, packages/extension/src/progressView/frontend/components/BackgroundTasksPanel.ts, reads only `status`, `lastQuestionPreview` (:338) and `lastActivityIso` (:351,:356); packages/extension/src/progressView/frontend/progressState.ts:344 sorts on `lastActivityIso`.
Non-production: 1 line (src/test-kernel/progressView/ProgressBackendFactProjection.vitest.ts:778, a fixture setting it to `null`).
Age: introduced 2026-05-16 by 85d785326a (#4086 "feat(inquiry): non-blocking, durable, thread-keyed inquiry pipeline"); >3 months with no consumer. `InquiryResumeOutcome` (inquiry.ts:53) is exported and imported only by inquiryContinuation.ts:31; it is not in config/ratchets/knip-baseline.json.

Dedupe: not in the round-1 index (49 shipped / 41 refuted) and `grep -in "ensureVisible|resumeOutcome"` over docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md returns nothing. `gh issue list --label tech-debt --state all --search "setActiveStream"` → 5 unrelated closed progress-view issues; `--search "inquiry"` → 15 unrelated. `git log --all --grep=resumeOutcome` and `--grep=ensureVisible` return nothing. Touches no settled surface: no ratchet row, no `@agent/*` deep import, no new bus.emit or subscribe surface, and it does not move any fact between the AgentEvent and SessionFact rails — it deletes two fields from one existing SessionFact payload.

**Proposal**

1. Delete `ensureVisible` (src/shared/schemas/progressEvents.ts:36-40) and the three `ensureVisible: true` lines at ToolEditApprovalController.ts:396, progressHostInteractions.ts:109, ExternalInquiryTool.ts:311. `suppressViewSwitch: true` stays — that one has live readers.
2. Drop the `ensureVisible|` alternation from the negative regex at sessionPresentationBoundary.vitest.ts:28 and the 7 fixture lines listed above.
3. Delete `resumeOutcome` (inquiry.ts:57), `InquiryResumeOutcomeSchema` (:48-52), `export type InquiryResumeOutcome` (:53) and the section banner (:44-46).
4. `InquiryThreadUpdatedEventSchema` (:55-58) then extends nothing, so collapse it: delete it and `InquiryThreadUpdatedEvent`, and rename the 13 files that import them to the already-existing `InquiryThreadSummarySchema` / `InquiryThreadSummary` (SessionEventHub.ts, SessionRendererPort.ts, LitSessionRenderer.ts, ExternalInquiryRequestHandler.ts, outbound.ts, cliNdjsonProgressEvents.ts, runProgressRenderer.ts, sessionSignalsAdapter.ts, store.ts, streamContexts.ts, BackgroundTasksPanel.ts, StreamConversation.ts, progressState.ts). This is a mechanical identifier swap, no shape change — the fact arm is still named `inquiryThreadUpdated`.
5. In inquiryContinuation.ts drop the `extra` parameter of `emitInquiryThreadUpdate` (:114-127) so it takes `(threadId, session?)`, and simplify its two call sites (:132-137, :169-173); the `outcome` local at :161 is still returned to the caller, only the emission argument goes.
6. Delete the `resumeOutcome: null` fixture line (ProgressBackendFactProjection.vitest.ts:778).
   If step 4's rename is judged too much churn for a reviewer, stop after step 3 and leave `InquiryThreadUpdatedEventSchema = InquiryThreadSummarySchema` — but that leaves two names for one type, so the rename is the better ending.

**What we give up**

(1) The ability for an interaction request (tool-edit approval, external inquiry, bash/plan approval) to ask a host to widen a restrictive stream filter so its background-registered row stays reachable. No host has implemented that since 2026-08-01, and no host has a category filter any more. Restoring it means re-adding both the filter concept and the field. (2) The ability for a UI badge to distinguish an inquiry continuation that was `sent` from one that was `queued` or `parent_finished`. That badge was never built; the same three outcomes are already logged (inquiryContinuation.ts:165-168) and returned as the tool's `InjectionOutcome`, so re-adding the wire field later is a one-line schema change.

**Verifier corrections to the evidence above**

Seven factual errors / omissions in the evidence. None kills it, but all must be fixed before the PR is written.

**C1 — Missing test file with three LIVE assertions (not fixtures).** The evidence says `resumeOutcome` non-production is "1 line (ProgressBackendFactProjection.vitest.ts:778, a fixture setting it to null)". It is 4 lines in 2 files. `src/test-kernel/tools/InquiryContinuationSession.vitest.ts:153, :186, :225` each assert `resumeOutcome: 'sent'` inside a `toEqual([...])` / `expect.objectContaining({...})` block — these are readers-in-test that will FAIL on deletion, not inert fixtures. That file is absent from the candidate's Paths list entirely, and step 6 of the proposal only names the ProgressBackendFactProjection line. Add `src/test-kernel/tools/InquiryContinuationSession.vitest.ts` to Paths and to the plan.

**C2 — Missing production pass-through: the field is on the headless NDJSON wire.** The evidence lists `cliNdjsonProgressEvents.ts:74` as "travels the wire" but never names the actual emit site: `packages/cli/src/runtime/sessionProgressSubscription.ts:70-71` forwards the whole fact payload verbatim (`return { event: 'inquiryThreadUpdated', payload: fact.payload }`). No edit is needed (whole-payload spread), but it means deleting `resumeOutcome` is an observable change to `texra --output-format ndjson` output, not a purely internal deletion. It still survives: `docs/guide/texra-cli.md:304`'s "stable for scripts" guarantee is scoped to `history list` records, and no doc enumerates progress-record fields. State this in the PR body rather than claiming it is internal-only.

**C3 — "Ambiguous corpus: zero" is established only for `ensureVisible`, and is FALSE for `resumeOutcome`.** I confirmed `rg "ensureVisible"` outside `src/` and `packages/` returns nothing. But `resumeOutcome` has two doc hits the evidence never surfaced: `docs/prds/2026-05-15-prd-external-inquiry-async.md:282` explicitly specs "The Background Tasks UI consumes `resumeOutcome` to render the appropriate badge (e.g. `parent_finished` → `answered · parent finished`)", plus :244-:282 spec the whole `extra` parameter; and `docs/proposals/2026-07-09-host-parity-audit.md:102` cites `resumeOutcome 'queued'`. Neither survives as a rule-4 ruling — the PRD badge was never built (`packages/extension/src/progressView/frontend/components/BackgroundTasksPanel.ts` renders only `status` at :635, `lastQuestionPreview` at :338, `lastActivityIso` at :351/:356), and the host-parity line is descriptive of an unwired CLI `tryResumeStream` port that this field does not fix. But the PR must cite and retire the PRD line, not assert the docs corpus is silent.

**C4 — `git log --all --grep=resumeOutcome` does NOT return nothing.** It returns ten commits: the five #4086 introduction commits (85d785326a, 0ca86a3915, 34b9e41ae8, 7ea2474a4f, 27750098fd) and five #4076 PRD commits. No prior deletion attempt exists, so the dedupe conclusion still holds — the claim as written is just wrong.

**C5 — `InquiryThreadSummarySchema` is not exported.** `src/shared/schemas/inquiry.ts:34` is a bare `const InquiryThreadSummarySchema = z.object({...})`; only the derived type `InquiryThreadSummary` (:41) is exported. Step 4's "rename to the already-existing `InquiryThreadSummarySchema`" additionally requires adding `export` to it before `outbound.ts:269,274` can import it. Not free, but net-neutral on element count.

**C6 — `git log -S"ensureVisible"` lists six commits, not two.** The evidence names aa2bc2eea5 and 88e20e4358. The full list also includes 76fca89f1c, e59761dd36 (#10117), 84231d6ed3 (#9827), 97643d6fca (#9489). The substantive claim is CONFIRMED by direct verification: at `88e20e4358^` the readers exist at `src/controllers/progressView/backend/events/ProgressFactApplier.ts:438` (`payload.ensureVisible === true &&`) and `:479` (`if (filterChanged && payload.ensureVisible === true)`); at `88e20e4358` both are gone and only the declaration remains. `rg "agentCategoryFilter" src packages` = 0 hits repo-wide, verified.

**C7 — LoC undercounted.** Claimed -38. My count: `ensureVisible` = -15 (5-line declaration block at progressEvents.ts:36-40, 3 writer lines, 7 test-fixture lines, 1 modified regex line at net 0). `resumeOutcome` = ~-31 (inquiry.ts:43-61 banner+enum+type+extend collapse ≈ -19, inquiryContinuation.ts import + `extra` param + two call-site collapses ≈ -8, 4 test lines). Total ≈ -46. Elements: -7, not -6 (`ensureVisible` field, `resumeOutcome` field, `InquiryResumeOutcomeSchema`, `InquiryResumeOutcome` type, `InquiryThreadUpdatedEventSchema`, `InquiryThreadUpdatedEvent` type, the `extra` parameter of `emitInquiryThreadUpdate`).

**One plan note, not an error:** the candidate's stop-after-step-3 fallback ("leave `InquiryThreadUpdatedEventSchema = InquiryThreadSummarySchema`") should be dropped from the PR. It mints a second name for one type, which AGENTS.md rules against directly. Step 4 is the only correct ending; it shrinks the exported surface by two names while adding one `export` keyword to an existing const.

And `src/controllers/progressView/backend/ExternalInquiryRequestHandler.ts:53` feeds `syncThreads` from `listThreadsByStatus`, whose return type at `src/tools/inquiry/externalInquiryStorage.ts:585` is `Promise<InquiryThreadSummary[]>` — assigned into an over-wide `InquiryThreadUpdatedEvent[]` parameter. Two of the three producers already produce the narrower shape. `manifestToSummary` (externalInquiryStorage.ts:534-549) never mints the field. Only `inquiryContinuation.ts:121` adds it, and nothing downstream distinguishes the two shapes. The step-4 rename is therefore not churn — it corrects a type that was already lying about its own value.

It loses: the badge was never implemented in any of the four hosts, the field has sat unread for over three months, and the same three outcomes are already logged at `inquiryContinuation.ts:165-168` and returned to the caller as `InjectionOutcome`. The one architecture test that names `ensureVisible` (`sessionPresentationBoundary.vitest.ts:28`) is a negative assertion that gets strictly stronger-per-token when the alternation shrinks.

Batched they clear the bar at ~-46 LoC / -7 elements, and the batch is coherent: both are write-only fields on one event channel, both were introduced for a consumer that no longer exists (or never did), and both are found by the same lens.

**Verdict: SURVIVES.** Real, unread, un-ruled, unbatched elsewhere. Risk stays medium for two reasons: deleting `resumeOutcome` changes an observable field on the headless NDJSON progress stream (C2), and step 4 touches 13 files across three hosts, so a type error there is a build break rather than a runtime bug. Both are checkable with `npm run typecheck` plus the two named vitest files.

#### Drop the single-valued includeActiveState knob from the SessionRendererPort stream-content sync

- **Lens**: `options-and-boolean-params` · **Kind**: speculative-generality · **Risk**: low
- **Net**: -21 LoC, -2 elements (claimed -20 LoC, -1 elements; verifier figure governs)

**Evidence**

`syncStreamContent(stream, options?: { includeActiveState?: boolean })` is declared on the host-neutral port at src/controllers/session/SessionRendererPort.ts:123-126. `rg 'syncStreamContent'` over src/ + packages/*/src, production: the port declaration; the Lit implementation (src/controllers/progressView/backend/LitSessionRenderer.ts:299); two CLI implementations that ignore the parameter outright — packages/cli/src/runtime/runProgressRenderer.ts:466 and packages/cli/src/chat/tui/state/sessionSignalsAdapter.ts:200, both spelled `_options?: { includeActiveState?: boolean }`; the IPC command name at src/shared/ipc.ts:125; and exactly TWO call sites, both passing the same literal — src/controllers/progressView/backend/ProgressBackend.ts:316 `this.renderer.syncStreamContent('', { includeActiveState: true })` and :374 `this.renderer.syncStreamContent(stream, { includeActiveState: true })`. Non-production call sites: src/test-kernel/progressView/StreamContentSync.vitest.ts:142, :189, :253 and src/test-kernel/progressView/ProgressBackendFactProjection.vitest.ts:1338 — all four also pass `{ includeActiveState: true }`. Ambiguous corpus (scripts/): 0 hits. So the `false` default at LitSessionRenderer.ts:307 (`const { includeActiveState = false } = options;`) is unreachable in every host and in every test.

The dead branch is not free: it threads a boolean through a second function (`buildStreamContent(stream, includeActiveState)` at :318 and :335-337) and forks twice inside it — `if (includeActiveState) state.getOrCreateStreamState(stream, category);` at :344-346 and `const executionState = includeActiveState ? state.getStreamState(stream) : undefined;` at :347-349 — which in turn makes the payload's `activeState` conditionally undefined for a case that never occurs.

PRECEDENT, not duplicate: `b2572a55a2` "refactor(progress-view): delete the never-produced activeStream metadata option (#11393)" is the same class of find one option over, which is why this one is worth taking rather than leaving. `gh issue list --label tech-debt --state all --search "includeActiveState"` -> only #9143, CLOSED, about a different projector. No hit for `includeActiveState` or `syncStreamContent` in the round-1 index or docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md.

**Proposal**

1. Drop the `options` parameter from `SessionRendererPort.syncStreamContent` (SessionRendererPort.ts:123-126) and from both CLI no-op implementations (runProgressRenderer.ts:464-466, sessionSignalsAdapter.ts:198-200).
2. In LitSessionRenderer, drop the parameter and the `const { includeActiveState = false }` destructure (:299-307), call `buildStreamContent(stream)`, and inline the always-taken arms: keep `state.getOrCreateStreamState(stream, category)` unconditionally (:344-346) and replace the ternary at :347-349 with `const executionState = state.getStreamState(stream);`.
3. Drop `{ includeActiveState: true }` from ProgressBackend.ts:316 and :374 and from the four test call sites; no assertion changes, since every existing case already exercises the include-active path.

**What we give up**

The ability for a host to request a stream-content sync that omits active execution state. No host has ever asked for it and the two CLI renderers do not implement the distinction at all, so re-adding it would mean designing it for a real second consumer rather than restoring this one.

**Verifier corrections to the evidence above**

1. WRONG TEST-CALL-SITE COUNT, and the central "unreachable in every test" claim is FALSE. The evidence lists exactly four non-production call sites (StreamContentSync.vitest.ts:142, :189, :253 and ProgressBackendFactProjection.vitest.ts:1338) and says "all four also pass { includeActiveState: true } ... So the `false` default at LitSessionRenderer.ts:307 is unreachable in every host and in every test." There are EIGHT test call sites. Four omit the option and therefore DO run the `false` default today: StreamContentSync.vitest.ts:217 ('projects workflow outputs without tool-use capabilities'), :270 ('projects clear without placeholder stream content'), :295 ('includes host-provided stream controls in synced content'), and ProgressBackendFactProjection.vitest.ts:1185. (:270 hits the `!stream` early return before the flag is read, so three tests genuinely exercise the false branch.) The claim that survives verification is narrower and is the one that matters: the `false` default is unreachable in PRODUCTION — the only two production callers, ProgressBackend.ts:316 and :374, both pass `{ includeActiveState: true }`.

2. Proposal step 3 is mis-scoped as a consequence. It says to "drop `{ includeActiveState: true }` ... from the four test call sites; no assertion changes, since every existing case already exercises the include-active path." Only four sites carry the literal and need editing; the other four need no edit but DO change behavior (they now call getOrCreateStreamState and get an `activeState` in the payload). I verified this is harmless: all use partial matchers (toMatchObject / objectContaining), and the two negative assertions at StreamContentSync.vitest.ts:231-232 are `not.toHaveProperty('workPlan'|'controls')`, which are category-gated, not activeState-gated. I ran the whole directory after applying the change: `npx vitest run src/test-kernel/progressView/` → 59 files, 552 tests, all pass.

3. LOAD-BEARING DETAIL THE EVIDENCE DOES NOT FLAG. `state.getStreamState(stream)` is not a plain getter: SessionState.ts:381-390 filters removed children out of `subagents`, while `getOrCreateStreamState` (SessionState.ts:313-338) returns the raw stored row unfiltered. The obvious-looking further collapse to `const executionState = state.getOrCreateStreamState(stream, category);` is WRONG and I reproduced the failure — ProgressBackendFactProjection.vitest.ts:1338's removal-barrier assertion (`activeState.badges.subagents: []`) fails, because the removed child is no longer filtered. The candidate's written step 2 happens to keep both calls (getOrCreateStreamState for the side effect, then getStreamState for the value), which is correct, but the implementing PR must not "simplify" one step further. This should be a comment in the PR.

4. LINE NUMBERS (measured at HEAD d8c6dfc071, which is 4 commits ahead of the tree the evidence was taken on — 8968988375). Correct: SessionRendererPort.ts:123-126 ✓; LitSessionRenderer.ts:299 ✓, :307 ✓. Wrong: `this.buildStreamContent(stream, includeActiveState)` is at :319, not :318; `buildStreamContent`'s signature is :335-338, not ":318 and :335-337"; the `if (includeActiveState)` guard is :345-347, not :344-346; the ternary is :348-350, not :347-349. CLI implementations span :464-467 (runProgressRenderer, not 464-466) and :198-201 (sessionSignalsAdapter, not 198-200).

5. LoC. Measured on the applied patch: 7 files changed, 13 insertions, 34 deletions = net -21 (claimed -20, close enough). Element count is -2, not -1: the `options` parameter on the port method and the `includeActiveState` parameter on the private `buildStreamContent`.

6. Adjacency worth knowing (not a duplicate). The refuted round-1 candidate "Drop the last defensive copies in syncSlice and the TOOL_ICON_MAP entry for a tool named 'runs'" analyzes `buildStreamContentRender` and its trace-viewer replay consumer in the same payload. It does not touch `includeActiveState` and does not block this.

<details><summary>Verifier reasoning</summary>

DUPLICATE (rule 1): No hit for `includeActiveState` or `syncStreamContent` in scratchpad/round1-index.md (95 lines, read in full — nearest entries are the SHIPPED "Drop the `compactRows` parameter from the scroll-bounds helpers — every call site passes the same constant", which establishes this exact class as accepted, and the REFUTED "Sweep three residues in the progress-view backend", which is a different set of residues). Grep of docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md for `includeActiveState`/`syncStreamContent` → 0 hits. Not already done at HEAD: the knob is still present at d8c6dfc071.

ALREADY FILED/DONE (rule 2): `gh issue list --label tech-debt --state all --search "includeActiveState syncStreamContent"` → only #9143, CLOSED/COMPLETED, "delete the hand-mirrored streamContentSync projector" (the src/shared/streams/streamContentSync.ts flat mirror plus a `forceRebuild` flag shipped in #9160) — a different change, as the evidence states. `git log --all --grep=includeActiveState` → only the four #9155 commits, which introduced adjacent roster work, not a ruling. `git log --oneline -12` on the two core paths shows the cited precedent b2572a55a2 (#11393, "delete the never-produced activeStream metadata option") — precedent, not duplicate.

CONSUMER COUNT (rule 3): I re-grepped `syncStreamContent` and `includeActiveState` across the whole repo excluding node_modules, not just src/ + packages/*/src. Production: the port declaration (SessionRendererPort.ts:123), one real implementation (LitSessionRenderer.ts:299), two no-op CLI implementations that already ignore the parameter (runProgressRenderer.ts:464, sessionSignalsAdapter.ts:198), the unrelated IPC command-name constant (src/shared/ipc.ts:125), and exactly two call sites (ProgressBackend.ts:316, :374), both passing the same literal. Zero hits in packages/extension/package.json, packages/extension/src/commands.ts, src/shared/schemas/*Settings.ts, packages/extension/resources/, prompts/, supabase/functions/, scripts/. No dynamic dispatch: the only string `'syncStreamContent'` is the IPC command constant, whose webview handler takes the payload, not the option. Desktop has no implementation of the port method. Definitive proof there is no missed implementer: I removed the parameter everywhere and ran `npm run typecheck` — extension, cli, trace-viewer and desktop projects all type check clean.

DELIBERATELY JUSTIFIED / SETTLED SURFACE (rules 4, 5): SessionRendererPort lives in src/controllers/session, host-neutral orchestration — not the frozen @agent/* SDK, not a platform port, not touched by host-agent-import-baseline, shared-schemas-deep-import, host-agent-mock, or architecture-edges. The one architecture ratchet that names `syncStreamContent`, src/test-kernel/architecture/sessionPresentationBoundary.vitest.ts:28, only forbids the identifier from appearing in SessionFactApplier.ts; it passes unchanged (I ran it). No dated doc or AGENTS.md rule argues for a host-selectable "sync without active state" mode; the opposite direction has a shipped precedent one commit class away (#11393).

MASKING SITE (rule 7): Nothing deleted is a catch, a `??` over a failed read, a Zod `.catch`, or a silent `default: return`. The `= false` default is speculative generality (checklist §13 class), not an M1-M6 masking site. No failure is being swallowed and none is being made quiet.

NET-ADDS / CHURN (rule 6): I applied the candidate's exact proposal. `git diff --shortstat` → 7 files changed, 13 insertions(+), 34 deletions(-) = net -21. It removes a knob from a host-neutral interface (surface shrinks), touches no schema, no wire contract, no barrel, no knip-baseline row. The blast radius is 5 production files plus 4 one-line test edits — no unrelated churn.

THIN (rule 8): -21 LoC across a port, its one real implementation, two no-op implementations and two call sites, removing a dead fork inside the payload builder that makes `activeState` conditionally undefined for a case that never occurs. That is the same size and shape as the precedent #11393, which shipped on its own, and as the round-1-shipped compactRows item. It stands alone; batching with another progress-view knob would be fine but is not required.

VERIFICATION PERFORMED (then reverted; `git status --porcelain` clean): applied the patch, `npx vitest run src/test-kernel/progressView/` → 59 files / 552 tests pass; `npx vitest run src/test-kernel/architecture/sessionPresentationBoundary.vitest.ts` → pass; `npm run typecheck` → all four projects clean. I also deliberately tried the over-reaching variant (collapsing both state lookups into one getOrCreateStreamState call) and confirmed it FAILS ProgressBackendFactProjection.vitest.ts:1338 — recorded in corrections so the implementing PR does not fall into it.

## 3. Refuted — do not re-file

14 candidates were killed on independent verification, grouped by lens.

### `webview-tree-parity`

- **One webview-storage singleton per page: the two `createWebviewStorage` caches silently clobber each other in the desktop renderer**

  <details><summary>Why it was refuted</summary>

  REFUTED — the mechanism is real but the harm is not, and what remains is a comment dedupe that costs a new module.

  1. THE CLOBBER IS UNOBSERVABLE, ON EVERY HOST. `createWebviewStorage` reads the host bridge EXACTLY ONCE, at construction (`src/shared/state/PersistedState.ts:23`). Every subsequent `get` reads `cache[key]` (:25-28) — the per-instance in-memory map — and never calls `hostBridge.getState()` again. I grepped every `getState()` call site under `packages/desktop/src`, `packages/extension/src`, `src/shared`, `src/platform`: the only `hostBridge.getState()` in the tree is `PersistedState.ts:23`. `PersistedState.reload()` (`persistence.ts:313` via `resetPersistenceRuntime`) does NOT re-read the bridge either — it calls `this.storage.get(this.key)`, i.e. the same cache. So on the desktop page, instance M and instance P each own a private, correct map for their own keys for the entire life of the document. `setState(cache)` is a write into a slot that nothing ever reads back. There is no state a component can observe being clobbered.

  2. THE ONE PATH THAT COULD MAKE IT OBSERVABLE DOES NOT EXIST ON DESKTOP. The clobber would only bite if the host slot survived to be re-read at the next construction, i.e. across a page load. `packages/desktop/src/preload/hostBridge.ts:25-34` backs the bridge with a bare `let state;` local to `installElectronHostBridge`, called once from `packages/desktop/src/preload/index.ts:20`. I grepped all of `packages/desktop/src` for `getState|setState`: those two lines in the preload are the only hits outside the renderer's `createWebviewStorage`. Nothing relays state to the main process, nothing writes it to disk. The preload re-executes on every document (`window.location.reload()` at `main.ts:1153`/`:1187`), so `state` resets to `undefined` and both caches start from `{}` again — exactly as the candidate itself observes ("Both caches initialize from `undefined`"). The candidate treats that as the proof of the bug; it is actually the proof that the bug cannot fire: the slot is write-only memory that is discarded before anyone could read the loser's keys. The claimed payoff, "correctness on the desktop host," is not a real defect.

  3. THE VS CODE STORY IS BACKWARDS, AND IT VINDICATES THE CURRENT DESIGN. The evidence says "In VS Code the two bundles never share a document (MainViewProvider swaps `webview.html` wholesale)." They DO share a document and a state slot: `MainViewProvider.switchMode` (`packages/extension/src/webview/MainViewProvider.ts:418-446`) assigns the PROGRESS content provider's html into the MAIN view's own `webviewView.webview` (:432-434) and swaps back at :440-442 — one webview, one `acquireVsCodeApi` state slot, alternating bundles. That is sequential, not concurrent: each new document constructs its storage by snapshotting the FULL current state, so the progress instance carries `mainViewState` forward and writes it back untouched. The whole-object cache is correct precisely because instances are sequential. Two-writer overlap exists only on desktop, where per (1) and (2) nothing reads.

  4. WHAT ACTUALLY REMAINS IS TOO THIN, AND IT NETS +1 MODULE. Strip the false correctness claim and the PR is: delete two one-line `const`s and their two doc blocks (~14 lines, almost all comment), add a new `src/shared/state/webviewStorage.ts` (~8-10 lines) plus one import line in `persistence.ts` and one extra import line each in `LogList.ts` and `streamLifecycleSlice.ts` (they currently pull `logListStateKey` and `webviewStorage` from one specifier and would need two). Real net is roughly -6 LoC of comment text and +1 module — not the "-5 LoC, -1 elements" in the header, and the candidate's own "What we give up" section already contradicts its header by conceding "roughly LoC-neutral." A new frontend-only singleton module in `src/shared/` also cuts against CLAUDE.md's scoping of `src/shared/` to wire contracts and UI-shared message types.

  5. THE PROPOSED SHAPE IS THE WRONG ONE ANYWAY. If a future change ever did make the desktop host slot persist, the fix that costs zero modules and covers every present and future call site is memoizing inside `createWebviewStorage` itself — cache the returned `StateStore` per bridge object in a module-level `WeakMap` (2-3 lines in `PersistedState.ts`, no new file, no consumer churn, no `@shared/hostBridge` import in a backend-reachable module). Adding a third module to enforce singleton-ness of a factory that can enforce it itself is the more expensive answer to a problem that has no live symptom.

  </details>

### `dual-representation`

- **Give the latexdiff math-markup setting and the external-tool status one type owner each**

  <details><summary>Why it was refuted</summary>

  REFUTED on rule 6 (NET-ADDS) and rule 8 (TOO THIN), with the load-bearing risk premise overstated.

  **1. It net-ADDS LoC. The claimed -11 is off by ~13.** I built the proposed mathMarkup.ts and ran the repo's prettier on it. Current src/latex/latexdiff/mathMarkup.ts = 21 lines. The proposed file (the multi-line `import { LATEXDIFF_MATH_MARKUP_VALUES, LATEX_CONFIG_DEFAULTS } from '@shared/constants/latexConfig';` at 80 cols = 4 lines, plus the wrapped `DEFAULT_MATH_MARKUP` assignment = 2 lines) = 22 lines. That is **+1**. Step 1's freeze move is line-neutral: `export const LATEXDIFF_MATH_MARKUP_VALUES = Object.freeze([` … `] as const);` is the same 6 lines as latexConfig.ts:22-27 today. Step 2 as written ("leave the type argument") is a zero-line change. Step 3 replaces one line (src/tools/toolAvailability.ts:36) with one line and adds `import type { ToolStatus } from '@shared/schemas';` = **+1**. Total measured: **+2 LoC, 0 elements removed**. Nothing is unexported, nothing is deleted; MATH_MARKUP_OPTIONS, MathMarkupOption, DEFAULT_MATH_MARKUP and describeMathMarkupOption all survive by design (the candidate explicitly picks the "rename-free" variant). The entire stated payoff was the LoC number, and the LoC number has the wrong sign.

  **2. The drift hazard is already closed at runtime, and the evidence's central claim is wrong.** Evidence: "the value written under the shared schema is read back through an unchecked type argument into the other vocabulary … Nothing checks that the two lists agree." I traced it. `readPlatformSetting<T>` (src/utils/config/platformSettings.ts:28-32) is `readSetting(requireEntry(key), platform()) as T`, and `readSetting` (src/shared/config/settingsAccess.ts:93-120) does `entry.schema.safeParse(normalized)` at :112, returns `result.data` on success, and on failure logs `log.warn('Ignoring invalid persisted value for setting …')` at :116-118 and returns `settingDefault(entry)`. The entry schema for LATEXDIFF_MATH_MARKUP _is_ `z.enum(LATEXDIFF_MATH_MARKUP_VALUES)` (stateSettings.ts:1278-1280). So the value crossing diffCommandExecutor.ts:242-243 is **already guaranteed** to be a member of LATEXDIFF_MATH_MARKUP_VALUES, with a loud warn on corruption — a correctly-implemented §15 site, not a masking one. The residual exposure is a compile-time type lie that only materializes if someone edits one four-element list and not the other, and even then the runtime clamps and warns. Not worth a +2-LoC PR.

  **3. Proposal step 2 is a no-op it does not know is a no-op.** "Replace `readPlatformSetting<MathMarkupOption>(...)` with a read parsed by the setting's own schema" — the read is _already_ parsed by the setting's own schema (settingsAccess.ts:112). Both offered branches of step 2 reduce to "change nothing," which is the whole of the candidate's claimed safety improvement.

  **4. It misses a fourth copy of the vocabulary, which undercuts the "one owner" framing.** src/latex/latexdiff/diffCommandExecutor.ts:43 restates the union in prose: `* @property mathMarkup - Math markup mode ('off' | 'whole' | 'coarse' | 'fine').` The candidate names three copies and misses this one, and its proposal does not touch it — so after the PR the literal set still appears in three source locations (latexConfig.ts:22-27, the diffCommandExecutor JSDoc at :43, and the hardcoded assertion in src/test-kernel/shared/stateSettings.vitest.ts:327-331). "One type owner" is not what ships.

  **5. Item (B) is a one-line type-alias swap that cannot carry a PR, and its direction is arguable.** `ToolStatus` is defined in src/shared/schemas/settingsViewMessages.ts:409-415 — a **settings-view wire contract**. Item (B) makes the tool-probing producer type (src/tools/toolAvailability.ts, which owns the vocabulary the view mirrors) depend on the view's message schema, inverting producer/consumer ownership. Defensible either way, not a cleanup, and worth exactly +1 line. Batching two hygiene one-liners does not clear rule 8; the batch is still 0 elements and net-positive LoC.

  Not a duplicate and not already filed — those checks pass. `grep -c 'mathMarkup|MATH_MARKUP|ToolStatus|toolAvailability|ExternalToolCheckResult'` over docs/proposals/2026-08-25-simplification-survey-49-candidates.md = 0; no round1-index.md title matches; `git log --oneline -6` on both path sets (d7c30c3efc, 549ae9f80c, b0bfa1f41e, 8b71e6149d, e3f156e9a8 / 794a524d8e, 23bda83d59, 9960863306, 88e20e4358, a39311a995, 9530c9f3ea) shows none attempting this; the tech-debt search returns only #9059 (shared-literal freezing, closed by #9081) and unrelated latexdiff rows. It dies on merit — net-adds and too thin — not on dedupe.
